📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 所有分析的分离株均属于东北亚谱系,主要在 2016 年至 2017 年左右分为两个区域分支(tMRCA:HA,2017 年 12 月;NA,2016 年 11 月)。尽管蒙古和韩国之间地理距离遥远,但这些 H6N1 分离株在东北亚谱系内表现出密切的遗传连通性(贝叶斯因子 > 100)。
  • 在距离农场 2 公里范围内采样的 12 只猫中,有 3 只具有针对 H5N1 病毒的中和抗体,并且靠近奶牛场是血清阳性的一个具有统计学意义的风险因素。 [10.3201/eid3209.260785]。
  • 该研究确定新南威尔士州 (NSW) 和维多利亚州 (VIC) 的 HPAI H5N1 家禽 暴发 预测风险最高,西澳大利亚州 (WA)、南澳大利亚州 (SA) 和塔斯马尼亚州 (TAS) 也有其他高风险地区。相比之下,北领地 (NT) 和昆士兰 (QLD) 的大部分地区、西澳和南澳预计风险较低。
  • 新兴的 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒(但不是历史的 H5 谱系)结合人 MHC-II HLA-DR 并介导不依赖唾液酸的细胞进入;结合随 MHC-II 表达和 HLA-DR 等位基因变异而变化;来自 clade 2.3.4.4b H5-naive 人类的交叉反应单克隆抗体可以阻断这种相互作用。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • All analyzed isolates belonged to the Northeast Asian lineage, primarily diverging into two regional subclades around 2016–2017 (tMRCA: HA, Dec 2017; NA, Nov 2016). [10.1007/s00705-026-06705-2].
  • Three of the 12 cats sampled within 2 km of farms had neutralizing antibodies against H5N1 virus, and proximity to dairy farms was a statistically significant risk factor for seropositivity. [10.3201/eid3209.260785].
  • The study identified New South Wales (NSW) and Victoria (VIC) as having the highest predicted risk of HPAI H5N1 poultry outbreaks, with additional high-risk areas in Western Australia (WA), South Australia (SA), and Tasmania (TAS).
  • Emerging H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, but not historical H5 lineages, bind human MHC-II HLA-DR and mediate sialic acid-independent cell entry; binding varies with MHC-II expression and HLA-DR allelic variation; and cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies from clade 2.3.4.4b H5-naive humans can block this interaction.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 澳大利亚维多利亚州确认了首例H5N1禽流感病例,该病例在波特兰附近的一只大凤头燕鸥中发现。
  • 维多利亚州确认首例H5N1禽流感病例,病毒在大凤头燕鸥中发现。
  • 当局呼吁家禽饲养者将鸟类隔离至少14天以减少传播风险。
  • H5N1毒株被确认为不可根除,澳大利亚政府已投入1.13亿美元应对措施。
  • Victoria, Australia, has confirmed its first case of the deadly H5N1 bird flu strain, detected in a greater crested tern near Portland.
  • Victoria confirmed its first H5N1 bird flu case in a greater crested tern near Portland.
  • Authorities are urging poultry keepers to house their birds for at least 14 days to reduce transmission risk.
  • The H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has been declared ineradicable, with $113 million allocated for response efforts.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: bioRxiv (Cold Spring Harbor Laboratory)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-26  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.07.22.739677  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

H5N1 流感通过人 II 类 MHC 结合和进入细胞,并通过交叉反应抗体阻断

H5N1 influenza binding and cell entry via human class II MHC, and blocking by cross-reactive antibodies
作者： Taylor Pursell, Artem Mikelov, Oliver F. Wirz, Jordan T. Ort, Shuk Hang Li, Reilly K. Atkinson, Jiabao Zhong, Jefferson J. S. Santos, Shilpa A. Joshi, Jumana Afghani
摘要中文翻译摘要段落 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 病毒目前负责影响 野生鸟类、家禽、众多哺乳动物物种和人类的多物种 暴发。甲型流感病毒通常通过与唾液酸结合来启动感染,尽管某些蝙蝠和人类流感病毒也可以利用 II 类主要组织相容性复合物 (MHC-II) 分子进入细胞。在这里,我们表明新兴的 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒(而不是历史的 H5 谱系)结合人类 MHC-II HLA-DR 并介导不依赖唾液酸的细胞进入。血凝素与初级人类免疫细胞的结合随 MHC-II 表达的变化而变化,并进一步受到 HLA-DR 等位基因变异的影响,从而识别出可能影响感染易感性的 宿主 遗传决定因素。血凝素唾液酸受体结合域内的哺乳动物适应性取代减少了 MHC-II 结合,表明这种相互作用在进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 适应人类 宿主 过程中被重塑。最后,从 clade 2.3.4.4b H5-naive 人类中分离出的交叉反应单克隆抗体可以阻断血凝素 - MHC-II 相互作用。这些发现确定了当代 H5N1 病毒中以前未被识别的受体途径,并揭示人类遗传变异和预先存在的体液免疫可以调节这种相互作用,对 宿主 范围、细胞向性、跨物种溢出 风险和治疗干预具有影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了当代 H5N1 病毒中出现的一种新受体途径,该途径结合人类 II 类 MHC (MHC-II) 分子进入细胞,这在历史 H5 谱系中未观察到,并探讨了 宿主 遗传变异和预先存在的免疫如何调节这种相互作用。
设计与对象
该研究利用人类原代免疫细胞研究了新兴 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的结合和细胞进入机制,并分析了 MHC-II 表达和 HLA-DR 等位基因变异对易感性的影响。
核心方法
该研究评估了血凝素与初级人类免疫细胞的结合,检查了血凝素受体结合域中哺乳动物适应性取代的影响,并测试了交叉反应单克隆抗体阻断血凝素-MHC-II相互作用的能力。
主要结果
新兴的 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒(但不是历史的 H5 谱系)结合人 MHC-II HLA-DR 并介导不依赖唾液酸的细胞进入;结合随 MHC-II 表达和 HLA-DR 等位基因变异而变化;来自 clade 2.3.4.4b H5-naive 人类的交叉反应单克隆抗体可以阻断这种相互作用。
解释与创新
作者解释说,血凝素和 MHC-II 之间的相互作用在进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 适应人类 宿主 的过程中被重塑,代表了当代 H5N1 病毒中以前未被识别的受体途径。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于理解 宿主 范围、细胞向性、跨物种溢出 风险和治疗干预具有重要意义,因为人类遗传变异和预先存在的体液免疫都可以调节 H5N1-MHC-II 相互作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究为 H5N1 病毒中的新型受体途径提供了强有力的证据,尽管其局限性包括侧重于体外结合和细胞进入测定而没有体内验证,并且由于直接的实验观察和机制见解,该证据被评估为高水平。

H5N1 influenza binding and cell entry via human class II MHC, and blocking by cross-reactive antibodies

Authors: Taylor Pursell, Artem Mikelov, Oliver F. Wirz, Jordan T. Ort, Shuk Hang Li, Reilly K. Atkinson, Jiabao Zhong, Jefferson J. S. Santos, Shilpa A. Joshi, Jumana Afghani
Original abstract (English)
Summary paragraph Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses are currently responsible for a multi-species outbreak affecting wild birds, poultry, numerous mammalian species, and humans. Influenza A viruses typically initiate infection through binding to sialic acid, although select bat and human influenza viruses can also exploit class II major histocompatibility complex (MHC-II) molecules for cell entry. Here we show that emerging H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, but not historical H5 lineages, bind human MHC-II HLA-DR and mediate sialic acid-independent cell entry. Hemagglutinin binding to primary human immune cells varies with MHC-II expression and is further shaped by HLA-DR allelic variation, identifying host genetic determinants that may influence susceptibility to infection. Mammalian-adaptive substitutions within the hemagglutinin sialic acid receptor-binding domain reduce MHC-II binding, suggesting this interaction is remodeled during clade 2.3.4.4b H5 adaptation to a human host. Lastly, cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies isolated from clade 2.3.4.4b H5-naive humans can block the hemagglutinin–MHC-II interaction. These findings identify a previously unrecognized receptor pathway in contemporary H5N1 viruses and reveal that both human genetic variation and pre-existing humoral immunity can modulate this interaction, with implications for host range, cellular tropism, spillover risk, and therapeutic intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the emergence of a novel receptor pathway in contemporary H5N1 viruses that bind human class II MHC (MHC-II) molecules for cell entry, which is not observed in historical H5 lineages, and explored how host genetic variation and pre-existing immunity modulate this interaction.
Design and population
The research investigated the binding and cell entry mechanisms of emerging H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses using primary human immune cells and analyzed the impact of MHC-II expression and HLA-DR allelic variation on susceptibility.
Core methods
The study assessed hemagglutinin binding to primary human immune cells, examined the effects of mammalian-adaptive substitutions in the hemagglutinin receptor-binding domain, and tested the ability of cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies to block the hemagglutinin–MHC-II interaction.
Main results
Emerging H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, but not historical H5 lineages, bind human MHC-II HLA-DR and mediate sialic acid-independent cell entry; binding varies with MHC-II expression and HLA-DR allelic variation; and cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies from clade 2.3.4.4b H5-naive humans can block this interaction.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the interaction between hemagglutinin and MHC-II is remodeled during clade 2.3.4.4b H5 adaptation to human hosts, representing a previously unrecognized receptor pathway in contemporary H5N1 viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for understanding host range, cellular tropism, spillover risk, and therapeutic intervention, as both human genetic variation and pre-existing humoral immunity can modulate the H5N1–MHC-II interaction.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides strong evidence for a novel receptor pathway in H5N1 viruses, though limitations include the focus on in vitro binding and cell entry assays without in vivo validation, and the evidence is assessed as high due to the direct experimental observations and mechanistic insights.

澳大利亚 HPAI H5N1 风险:家禽预测模型 暴发

HPAI H5N1 risk in Australia: a model for the prediction of poultry outbreaks
作者： Pan Zhang, Samsung Lim, Ashley Quigley, C Raina MacIntyre
摘要中文翻译澳大利亚大陆现已检测到泛动物 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒,来自亚南极地区的入侵对国内野生动物和家禽种群构成了越来越大的威胁。我们的研究旨在利用一系列有影响的风险因素,在地方政府地区 (LGA) 层面预测澳大利亚各地 HPAI H5N1 家禽 暴发 的风险。我们首先使用最大熵 (MaxEnt) 模型来估计澳大利亚 HPAI H5N1 发生的环境适宜性。然后将所得的适宜性图层与另外五个预测图层相集成,其中包括两个南大洋 野生鸟类 的丰度数据,其中一个已将 HPAI H5N1 引入澳大利亚;28 种澳大利亚本土 野生鸟类 的丰度数据;澳大利亚本地鸟类的飞行路线;澳洲鸡密度;和家禽养殖场密度。对六个图层进行聚合和平均,生成澳大利亚地方政府区域 (LGA) 家禽 暴发 的 HPAI H5N1 风险地图。尽管大多数入侵发生在西澳大利亚州 (WA) 和南澳大利亚州 (SA),但我们确定新南威尔士州 (NSW) 和维多利亚州 (VIC) 的 HPAI H5N1 家禽 暴发 预测风险最高。西澳州、南澳州和塔斯马尼亚州 (TAS) 还确定了其他高风险地区。相比之下,北领地 (NT) 和昆士兰 (QLD) 的大部分地区、西澳和南澳预计风险较低。这些发现提供了一个空间明确的框架来支持有针对性的 监测、准备和生物安全措施,旨在减轻未来 HPAI H5N1 暴发 对澳大利亚家禽的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过预测当地政府地区 (LGA) 层面的 暴发 风险,解决 HPAI H5N1 对澳大利亚家禽和野生动物日益增长的威胁,特别是来自亚南极洲的入侵。
设计与对象
该研究使用 MaxEnt 模型来估计澳大利亚各地 HPAI H5N1 发生的环境适宜性,整合鸟类丰度、飞行路线和家禽密度的数据,以生成 LGA 的风险地图。
核心方法
采用最大熵 (MaxEnt) 模型来估计 HPAI H5N1 的环境适宜性,并与五个附加预测层相结合,包括 野生鸟类 丰度、鸟类飞行路线和家禽密度数据。
主要结果
该研究确定新南威尔士州 (NSW) 和维多利亚州 (VIC) 的 HPAI H5N1 家禽 暴发 预测风险最高,西澳大利亚州 (WA)、南澳大利亚州 (SA) 和塔斯马尼亚州 (TAS) 也有其他高风险地区。相比之下,北领地 (NT) 和昆士兰 (QLD) 的大部分地区、西澳和南澳预计风险较低。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为有针对性的 监测 和生物安全措施的空间明确框架,提供了一种预测澳大利亚家禽 HPAI H5N1 风险的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为有针对性的 监测、准备和生物安全策略提供信息,以减轻未来 HPAI H5N1 暴发 对澳大利亚家禽的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于预测模型,不包括 暴发 预测的实证验证,这可能会限制其结果的可信度。由于使用建模数据而不是直接观察,证据的强度中等。

HPAI H5N1 risk in Australia: a model for the prediction of poultry outbreaks

Authors: Pan Zhang, Samsung Lim, Ashley Quigley, C Raina MacIntyre
Original abstract (English)
The panzootic highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 virus has now been detected on the Australian mainland, with incursions from the sub-Antarctic region posing an increasing threat to domestic wildlife and poultry populations. Our study aimed to predict the risk of HPAI H5N1 poultry outbreaks across Australia at the local government area (LGA) level using a range of influential risk factors. We first used a Maximum Entropy (MaxEnt) model to estimate the environmental suitability for HPAI H5N1 occurrence across Australia. The resulting suitability layer was then integrated with five additional predictor layers, including abundance data for two Southern Ocean wild birds, one of which has introduced HPAI H5N1 into Australia; abundance data for 28 native Australian wild birds; native bird flyways across Australia; Australian chicken density; and poultry farm density. The six layers were aggregated and averaged to generate an HPAI H5N1 risk map for poultry outbreaks across Australian LGAs. Although most incursions have occurred in Western Australia (WA) and South Australia (SA), we identified New South Wales (NSW) and Victoria (VIC) as having the highest predicted risk of HPAI H5N1 poultry outbreaks. Additional high-risk areas were identified in WA, SA, and Tasmania (TAS). In contrast, the Northern Territory (NT) and large parts of Queensland (QLD), WA, and SA were predicted to be at low risk. These findings provide a spatially explicit framework to support targeted surveillance, preparedness, and biosecurity measures aimed at mitigating the impact of future HPAI H5N1 outbreaks in Australian poultry.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing threat of HPAI H5N1 to Australian poultry and wildlife, particularly from sub-Antarctic incursions, by predicting outbreak risks at the local government area (LGA) level.
Design and population
The study used a MaxEnt model to estimate environmental suitability for HPAI H5N1 occurrence across Australia, integrating data on bird abundance, flyways, and poultry density to generate a risk map for LGAs.
Core methods
A Maximum Entropy (MaxEnt) model was employed to estimate environmental suitability for HPAI H5N1, and this was combined with five additional predictor layers, including wild bird abundance, bird flyways, and poultry density data.
Main results
The study identified New South Wales (NSW) and Victoria (VIC) as having the highest predicted risk of HPAI H5N1 poultry outbreaks, with additional high-risk areas in Western Australia (WA), South Australia (SA), and Tasmania (TAS). In contrast, the Northern Territory (NT) and large parts of Queensland (QLD), WA, and SA were predicted to be at low risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as a spatially explicit framework for targeted surveillance and biosecurity measures, offering a novel approach to predict HPAI H5N1 risk in Australian poultry.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance, preparedness, and biosecurity strategies to mitigate the impact of future HPAI H5N1 outbreaks in Australian poultry.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on predictive modeling and does not include empirical validation of outbreak predictions, which may limit the confidence in its results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the use of modeled data rather than direct observation.

Journal: Vaccine: X  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.jvacx.2026.100861  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 31, 100861

去糖基化 H7N9 VLP 免疫可增强小鼠针对 禽流感 H7N9 变种的跨品系保护性抗体反应

Immunization with deglycosylated H7N9 VLP enhances cross-strain protective antibody responses against avian influenza H7N9 variants in mice
作者： Asmaul Husna, Yu-Chieh Cheng, Chia-Chun Lai, Tsai-Chuan Weng, Min-Han Lin, Min-Shi Lee, Shih-Che Sue, Wang-Chou Sung, Yu‐Chieh Cheng, Shih‐Che Sue
摘要中文翻译简介 快速进化的 禽流感 H7N9 病毒仍然对全球公共卫生构成威胁。迫切需要开发广谱 疫苗,能够引发针对新出现的病毒变种的功能性跨毒株保护性免疫。方法 N-连接糖基化可以保护关键残基免受免疫识别。在这里,基于 A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 病毒的抗原序列构建了原型病毒样颗粒 (VLP)。利用糖苷酶修剪聚糖分子,以生成单糖基化 VLP (VLPmg) 和非糖基化 VLP (VLPng) 衍生物。对这些 VLP 的结构完整性、抗原性和免疫原性进行了系统评估。结果 VLPmg 和 VLPng 均保持与完全糖基化原型 VLP (VLPfg) 一样的血凝活性和抗原性。用去糖基化的 VLP 进行免疫可以在小鼠体内诱导出强大的病毒特异性抗体和平衡的 Th1/Th2 免疫特征,而滴度与 VLPfg 相当。其中,VLPng针对不同的H7N9病毒株诱导了更高的血凝抑制和中和抗体滴度,表明跨病毒株反应性抗体反应得到改善。结论 这项研究表明,流感 H7N9 VLP 的酶促去糖基化可改善小鼠体内广泛保护性抗体反应的诱导。非糖基化的 H7N9 VLP 代表了潜在的 疫苗 候选药物,可对抗抗原不同的 H7N9 病毒变种的威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 H7N9 构成持续的公共卫生威胁,现有的 疫苗 可能无法针对抗原不同的变种提供足够的跨毒株保护。该研究解决了对能够引发功能性交叉菌株免疫的广谱 疫苗 的需求。
设计与对象
该研究根据A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9病毒的抗原序列构建了病毒样颗粒(VLP),并在小鼠中评估了其结构完整性、抗原性和免疫原性。
核心方法
N-连接糖基化经过酶促修饰,生成单糖基化 (VLPmg) 和非糖基化 (VLPng) VLP,然后评估其血凝活性、抗原性和免疫原性。
主要结果
VLPmg 和 VLPng 均保持了与完全糖基化的原型 VLP 相当的血凝活性和抗原性。与 VLPmg 或 VLPfg 相比,用 VLPng 免疫可诱导针对不同 H7N9 毒株的更高血凝抑制和中和抗体滴度。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,H7N9 VLP 的酶促去糖基化可改善小鼠中广泛保护性抗体反应的诱导,这表明 VLPng 是针对抗原不同的 H7N9 变体的有前途的 疫苗 候选药物。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持开发基于去糖基化 VLP 的 疫苗,以增强针对 H7N9 变种的跨毒株保护,可能为未来的 疫苗 设计和大流行防范策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,研究结果需要在人体中进行验证,以在临床转化之前评估安全性、免疫原性和有效性。

Immunization with deglycosylated H7N9 VLP enhances cross-strain protective antibody responses against avian influenza H7N9 variants in mice

Authors: Asmaul Husna, Yu-Chieh Cheng, Chia-Chun Lai, Tsai-Chuan Weng, Min-Han Lin, Min-Shi Lee, Shih-Che Sue, Wang-Chou Sung, Yu‐Chieh Cheng, Shih‐Che Sue
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The rapidly evolving avian influenza H7N9 virus remains a threaten to public health globally. There is an urgent need to develop broad-spectrum vaccines capable of eliciting functional cross-strain protective immunity against emerging viral variants. Methods N-linked glycosylation can shield critical residues from immune recognition. Here, a prototype virus-like particle (VLP) was constructed based on the antigenic sequences derived from A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 virus. Glycosidase enzymes were utilized to trim the glycan molecules for generating monoglycosylated VLP (VLPmg) and nonglycosylated VLP (VLPng) derivates. The structural integrity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity of these VLPs were systematically evaluated. Results Both VLPmg and VLPng maintained hemagglutination activity and antigenicity like the fully glycosylated prototype VLP (VLPfg). Immunization with deglycosylated VLPs induced robust virus-specific antibodies and a balanced Th1/Th2 immune profile in mice, whereas the titers were comparable to that of VLPfg. Among them, VLPng induced higher hemagglutination inhibition and neutralizing antibody titers against different H7N9 virus strains, indicating improved cross-strain reactive antibody responses. Conclusion This study demonstrated the enzymatic deglycosylation of influenza H7N9 VLP improves the induction of broadly protective antibody responses in mice. The nonglycosylated H7N9 VLP represents a potential vaccine candidate to against the threat of antigenically divergent H7N9 virus variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza H7N9 poses an ongoing public health threat, and existing vaccines may not provide sufficient cross-strain protection against antigenically divergent variants. The study addresses the need for broad-spectrum vaccines that can elicit functional cross-strain immunity.
Design and population
The study constructed virus-like particles (VLPs) based on the antigenic sequences of the A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 virus and evaluated their structural integrity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity in mice.
Core methods
N-linked glycosylation was enzymatically trimmed to generate monoglycosylated (VLPmg) and nonglycosylated (VLPng) VLPs, which were then assessed for hemagglutination activity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity.
Main results
Both VLPmg and VLPng maintained hemagglutination activity and antigenicity comparable to the fully glycosylated prototype VLP. Immunization with VLPng induced higher hemagglutination inhibition and neutralizing antibody titers against different H7N9 strains than VLPmg or VLPfg.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that enzymatic deglycosylation of H7N9 VLPs improves the induction of broadly protective antibody responses in mice, suggesting that VLPng is a promising vaccine candidate against antigenically divergent H7N9 variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the development of deglycosylated VLP-based vaccines to enhance cross-strain protection against H7N9 variants, potentially informing future vaccine design and pandemic preparedness strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mouse models, and the findings require validation in humans to assess safety, immunogenicity, and efficacy before clinical translation.

H5N1 之外:牛乳房类器官中的甲型流感病毒感染。

Beyond H5N1: influenza A virus infection in bovine udder organoids.
作者： Henrik Fritsch, Prerna Arora, Miaomiao Yan, Inga Grotha, Christiane Pfarrer, Paul Becher
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 最近将其 宿主 范围扩大到了牛物种,引发了有关组织向性、病毒复制能力以及对乳制品生产和人畜共患风险的影响的关键问题。美国自然感染牛的牛奶中 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) H5N1 的大量脱落突出表明牛乳腺是病毒复制的相关位点。为了研究这些动力学,我们建立并表征了源自原代上皮细胞并维持在化学成分确定的培养基中的牛乳腺类器官(MGO)。这种高效、可扩展的模型重建了牛乳房的结构和功能特征,包括管腔基底极性、谱系特异性细胞角蛋白模式和哺乳相关分化。然后,我们使用该系统研究低致病性和高致病性禽类、猪和人类 IAV。来自不同 宿主 物种的病毒可有效感染 MGO,很大程度上与培养条件无关。哺乳动物来源的 H1N1 毒株在效率上与牛 H5N1 相当,但低致病性禽类 H9N2 的复制受到限制,表明向乳性方面存在亚型特异性差异。这项适用性研究开发了一个 MGO 平台,为牛乳腺感染生物学和流感 发病机制 的详细分子研究提供了易于处理的基础,为保护动物和公众健康的策略提供了信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒最近将其 宿主 范围扩大到了牛物种,引发了有关组织向性、病毒复制能力以及对乳制品生产和人畜共患风险的影响的关键问题。
设计与对象
为了研究这些动力学,我们建立并表征了源自原代上皮细胞并维持在化学成分确定的培养基中的牛乳腺类器官(MGO)。
核心方法
我们使用该系统研究低致病性和高致病性禽类、猪和人类 IAV,并评估 MGO 中的病毒复制效率。
主要结果
来自不同 宿主 物种的病毒可有效感染 MGO,很大程度上与培养条件无关;哺乳动物来源的 H1N1 毒株在效率上与牛 H5N1 相当,但低致病性禽类 H9N2 的复制受到限制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,向乳腺倾向存在亚型特异性差异,MGO 平台为牛乳腺感染生物学和流感 发病机制 的详细分子研究提供了易于处理的基础。
科研与公卫意义
本研究开发了一个 MGO 平台,可以通过阐明牛中的流感 发病机制 来为保护动物和公众健康提供策略。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究表明多种 IAV 可以有效感染 MGO,但没有解决体内验证或长期病毒动力学问题。

Beyond H5N1: influenza A virus infection in bovine udder organoids.

Authors: Henrik Fritsch, Prerna Arora, Miaomiao Yan, Inga Grotha, Christiane Pfarrer, Paul Becher
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A viruses (IAVs) have recently expanded their host range to bovine species, raising critical questions about tissue tropism, viral replication capacity, and impacts on dairy production and zoonotic risk. Extensive shedding of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) H5N1 in milk from naturally infected cattle in the USA highlights the bovine mammary gland as a relevant site of viral replication. To investigate these dynamics, we established and characterized bovine mammary gland organoids (MGOs) derived from primary epithelial cells and maintained in chemically defined media. This efficient, scalable model reconstructs structural and functional hallmarks of the bovine udder, including luminal-basal polarity, lineage-specific cytokeratin patterns, and lactation-associated differentiation. We then used this system to study low and highly pathogenic avian, as well as swine and human IAVs. Viruses from diverse host species productively infected MGOs, largely independent of culturing conditions. Mammalian origin H1N1 strains matched bovine H5N1 in efficiency, but low pathogenic avian H9N2 showed restricted replication, indicating subtype-specific differences in mammary tropism. This suitability study develops a MGO platform that provides a tractable basis for detailed molecular investigation of mammary infection biology and influenza pathogenesis in cattle, to inform strategies that protect both animal and public health.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A viruses have recently expanded their host range to bovine species, raising critical questions about tissue tropism, viral replication capacity, and impacts on dairy production and zoonotic risk.
Design and population
To investigate these dynamics, we established and characterized bovine mammary gland organoids (MGOs) derived from primary epithelial cells and maintained in chemically defined media.
Core methods
We used this system to study low and highly pathogenic avian, as well as swine and human IAVs, and assessed viral replication efficiency in MGOs.
Main results
Viruses from diverse host species productively infected MGOs, largely independent of culturing conditions; mammalian origin H1N1 strains matched bovine H5N1 in efficiency, but low pathogenic avian H9N2 showed restricted replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate subtype-specific differences in mammary tropism, with the MGO platform providing a tractable basis for detailed molecular investigation of mammary infection biology and influenza pathogenesis in cattle.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study develops a MGO platform that can inform strategies to protect both animal and public health by elucidating influenza pathogenesis in cattle.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study demonstrates efficient infection of MGOs by diverse IAVs but does not address in vivo validation or long-term viral dynamics.

突变在严重的人类H5N1病例中有助于逃避人类粘液和抗病毒药物

Mutations in severe human H5N1 cases facilitate evasion from human mucus and antivirals
作者： Ksenia Sukhova, Jiayun Yang, Antonio Di Maio, Jin Yu, Maria R Oliveira, Hannah Hens, Jean-Remy Sadeyen, Sareeta Bagri, Sam Jones, Jack A Hassard
摘要中文翻译2024 年末,加拿大和美国的两名患者因感染 禽流感 A 病毒 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 基因型 D1.1 引起的急性呼吸窘迫而在重症监护室接受治疗。从这些患者样本中获得的病毒序列数据表明,血凝素 (HA) 第 190 和 226 位存在混合等位基因。这些位置的 突变 是 HA 使用 α2,6 连接唾液酸 (SA)(人类上呼吸道中最丰富的流感受体)的关键决定因素。因此,这些 突变 引起了人们对 H5N1 病毒的人类适应和大流行潜力的担忧。在本研究中,我们研究了 突变 对 H5 HA 残基 190 和 226 的影响。我们使用重组蛋白、假型慢病毒以及在流感病毒的背景下使用反向遗传学研究了 突变 的受体结合特性、细胞进入表型和适应性影响。突变 没有赋予任何可检测的α2,6-连接唾液酸受体单独或组合使用。相反,携带这些 突变 的病毒表现出与 α2,3 连接唾液酸受体的结合减弱。这与逃避人类气道粘液的能力增强以及对奥司他韦和扎那米韦的敏感性降低相关。这项研究强调,除了HA作为进入受体与SA相互作用的方式外,影响HA/NA平衡的其他因素也可能影响人类呼吸道中人畜共患病毒的进化轨迹。这项研究为 HA 的进化驱动因素提供了一个新的范例,其中唾液酸结合的减少可以作为逃避 宿主 屏障和 抗病毒药物 的优势。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究的动机是需要了解 禽流感 A 病毒适应人类的潜力,加拿大和美国的两名个体因感染 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 基因型 D1.1 引起的急性呼吸窘迫而在重症监护室接受治疗。
设计与对象
该研究涉及分析两名感染 H5N1 2.3.4.4b 基因型 D1.1 的患者的病毒序列数据,这两名患者因急性呼吸窘迫而在重症监护室接受治疗。研究重点是在这些病例中观察到的 HA 位点 190 和 226 处的 突变。
核心方法
研究人员使用重组蛋白、假型慢病毒和反向遗传学来研究流感病毒中 突变 在 HA 残基 190 和 226 处的受体结合特性、细胞进入表型和适应性影响。
主要结果
HA位置190和226处的突变不赋予可检测的α2,6-连接的唾液酸受体单独或组合使用。相反,带有这些 突变 的病毒与 α2,3 连接唾液酸受体的结合减弱。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HA中的突变可导致唾液酸结合减少,这可能为病毒逃避宿主屏障和抗病毒药物提供优势,为HA的进化驱动因素提供了新的范例。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于了解 禽流感 病毒对人类的适应具有重要意义,并可为 监测、诊断 以及针对人畜共患流感的 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于两名患者的数据,这可能会限制普遍性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它直接检查了 突变 在流感病毒背景下的受体结合特性和适应性影响。

Mutations in severe human H5N1 cases facilitate evasion from human mucus and antivirals

Authors: Ksenia Sukhova, Jiayun Yang, Antonio Di Maio, Jin Yu, Maria R Oliveira, Hannah Hens, Jean-Remy Sadeyen, Sareeta Bagri, Sam Jones, Jack A Hassard
Original abstract (English)
In late 2024, two individuals in Canada and the United States were treated in intensive care for acute respiratory distress caused by infection with the avian Influenza A Virus H5N1 2.3.4.4b genotype D1.1. Viral sequence data obtained from sampling these patients indicated mixed alleles at haemagglutinin (HA) positions 190 and 226. Mutations at these positions are key determinants of HA usage of α2,6-linked sialic acids (SA), the most abundant influenza receptors in human upper respiratory tracts. Thus, these mutations raised concerns about human adaptation and pandemic potential of the H5N1 virus. In this study, we investigated the impact of the mutations at residues 190 and 226 in H5 HA. We studied the receptor binding properties, cell entry phenotypes and fitness impacts of the mutations using recombinant proteins, pseudotyped lentiviruses, and in the context of influenza viruses using reverse genetics. The mutations did not confer any detectable α2,6-linked sialic acid receptor usage either alone or in combination. Rather, viruses carrying these mutations exhibit weakened binding towards α2,3-linked sialic acid receptors. This correlated with an enhanced capacity to evade human airway mucus, and a reduced susceptibility to oseltamivir and zanamivir. This research underscores that in addition to the way HA interacts with SA as entry receptors, other factors that impact the HA/NA balance might influence the evolutionary trajectory of a zoonotic virus in the human respiratory tract. This study presents a new paradigm for the evolutionary drivers of HA, where reduced sialic acid binding can serve as an advantage for escape from host barriers and antivirals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was motivated by the need to understand the potential for avian influenza A viruses to adapt to humans, as two individuals in Canada and the United States were treated in intensive care for acute respiratory distress caused by infection with the H5N1 2.3.4.4b genotype D1.1.
Design and population
The study involved analyzing viral sequence data from two patients infected with the H5N1 2.3.4.4b genotype D1.1, who were treated in intensive care for acute respiratory distress. The research focused on the mutations at HA positions 190 and 226 observed in these cases.
Core methods
The researchers used recombinant proteins, pseudotyped lentiviruses, and reverse genetics to study the receptor binding properties, cell entry phenotypes, and fitness impacts of the mutations at HA residues 190 and 226 in the context of influenza viruses.
Main results
The mutations at HA positions 190 and 226 did not confer detectable α2,6-linked sialic acid receptor usage either alone or in combination. Instead, viruses with these mutations showed weakened binding towards α2,3-linked sialic acid receptors.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that mutations in HA can lead to reduced sialic acid binding, which may provide an advantage for the virus to evade host barriers and antivirals, presenting a new paradigm for the evolutionary drivers of HA.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for understanding the adaptation of avian influenza viruses to humans and could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and the development of antiviral strategies against zoonotic influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on data from two patients, which may limit generalizability. However, the evidence is strong as it directly examines the receptor binding properties and fitness impacts of the mutations in the context of influenza viruses.

H5N1 高致病性禽流感 病毒感染奶牛牛奶样品中流感抗体检测和定量血清学测定的评估

Evaluation of serologic assays for detection and quantification of influenza antibodies in milk samples from H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus infected dairy cows
作者： Sherif Ibrahim, Youngsub Lee, Erin Posey, Mia K. Torchetti, Tim Olivier, Erica Spackman, David L. Suarez, Chang-Won Lee
摘要中文翻译自 2024 年 3 月 25 日在美国奶牛中确认 H5N1 HPAI 病毒以来,截至 2026 年 4 月 2 日,在 19 个州报告的牛 HPAI 暴发 已在 1,000 多个确诊牛群中继续存在。为了确保持续监测 HPAI 传播,已经评估了额外的血清学检测,以补充支撑当前流行病学方法的现有商业 ELISA 和分子工具。我们之前建立了一种 N1 抗体特异性神经氨酸酶抑制 (NI)-酶联凝集素测定 (ELLA),用于 DIVA(区分感染者与接种动物)监测,以检测具有灭活异源 NA 亚型 (H5N9) 疫苗 的接种家禽中的 H5N1 感染。在本研究中,我们应用 NI-ELLA 对个体(n = 110)和散装罐(n = 104)牛乳样品中的 N1 抗体进行检测和定量。我们证明,根据样品质量,未经处理的样品和通过离心然后热灭活收集的下层液均可用于不同的血清学测定。NI-ELLA 和商业 ELISA 之间的抗体检测具有很强的相关性。NI-ELLA 测定的终点滴度也与病毒中和滴度密切相关,病毒中和滴度用于评估牛奶样品中的保护性抗体水平。对散装罐奶样品中 N1 抗体的检测和定量进一步显示,与病毒分离阳性样品相比,病毒分离阴性样品(无论是否存在通过 PCR 检测到的病毒 RNA)的检出率更高,抗体滴度更强。较高水平的中和抗体可能导致缺乏感染性病毒检测,具体取决于收集牛奶时病毒感染的阶段。未来的研究有必要描述奶牛个体长时间内牛奶样本中抗体反应的持续时间和动态,NI-ELLA 将作为评估这些研究中抗体反应的有价值的定量工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究需要评估用于监测 H5N1 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 在奶牛中传播的额外血清学测定,以补充现有的商业 ELISA 和分子工具。目的是评估 N1 抗体特异性神经氨酸酶抑制 (NI)-酶联凝集素测定 (ELLA),用于检测和定量受感染奶牛牛奶样品中的抗体。
设计与对象
本研究应用 NI-ELLA 检测和定量从感染 H5N1 HPAI 病毒的奶牛采集的个体(n = 110)和散装罐(n = 104)牛乳样品中的 N1 抗体。使用未经处理的制剂和通过离心随后热灭活收集的下层液来处理样品。
核心方法
研究人员使用已建立的 N1 抗体特异性 NI-ELLA 进行 DIVA(区分感染者与接种疫苗的动物)监测。对个体和散装罐牛奶样品进行抗体检测和定量,并与商业 ELISA 和病毒中和滴度进行比较。
主要结果
NI-ELLA 与用于抗体检测的商业 ELISA 具有很强的相关性,并且终点滴度与病毒中和滴度具有良好的相关性。无论 PCR 结果如何,与病毒分离阳性样品相比,散装罐牛奶样品在病毒分离阴性样品中显示出更高的检出率和更强的抗体滴度。
解释与创新
作者解释说,较高水平的中和抗体可能导致缺乏感染性病毒检测,具体取决于收集牛奶时的感染阶段。新颖之处在于 NI-ELLA 作为监测牛奶样品中抗体反应的定量工具得到了验证。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持 NI-ELLA 作为 监测 的有价值的定量工具,评估 H5N1 感染奶牛的抗体动态,从而加强流行病学监测和 暴发 管理。未来的研究可以利用这种方法来描述长时间内的抗体反应。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究侧重于特定的测定和样本类型,没有更广泛的普遍性。局限性包括依赖单一地理 暴发 以及需要在不同人群和时间范围内进行进一步验证。

Evaluation of serologic assays for detection and quantification of influenza antibodies in milk samples from H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus infected dairy cows

Authors: Sherif Ibrahim, Youngsub Lee, Erin Posey, Mia K. Torchetti, Tim Olivier, Erica Spackman, David L. Suarez, Chang-Won Lee
Original abstract (English)
Since the confirmation of H5N1 HPAI virus in US dairy cattle on March 25, 2024, the bovine HPAI outbreak has continued with more than 1,000 confirmed herds in 19 states reported as of April 2, 2026. To ensure ongoing monitoring of HPAI spread, additional serologic assays have been evaluated to complement existing commercial ELISAs and molecular tools that underpin current epidemiological approaches. We previously established an N1 antibody-specific neuraminidase inhibition (NI)-enzyme linked lectin assay (ELLA) for DIVA (differentiation of infected from vaccinated animals) surveillance to detect H5N1 infection in vaccinated poultry with inactivated heterologous NA subtype (H5N9) vaccine. In this study, we applied NI-ELLA for the detection and quantification of N1 antibody in individual ( n = 110) and bulk tank ( n = 104) bovine milk samples. We demonstrated that both untreated samples and infranatant collected by centrifugation followed by heat inactivation can be used for different serologic assays depending on sample quality. There was a strong correlation in antibody detection between NI-ELLA and commercial ELISAs. Endpoint titers determined by NI-ELLA also correlated well with virus neutralization titers, which are used to evaluate protective antibody levels in milk samples. Detection and quantification of N1 antibodies in bulk tank milk samples further showed higher detection rates and stronger antibody titers among virus isolation-negative samples (regardless of the presence or absence of viral RNA detected by PCR) compared to virus isolation-positive samples. Higher levels of neutralizing antibody likely contributed to the lack of infectious virus detection depending on the stage of viral infection when milk was collected. Future studies are warranted to describe the duration and dynamics of antibody responses in milk samples over extended periods across individual cows, and NI-ELLA will serve as a valuable quantitative tool for evaluating antibody responses in those studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to evaluate additional serologic assays for monitoring the spread of H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) in dairy cattle, complementing existing commercial ELISAs and molecular tools. The objective was to assess the N1 antibody-specific neuraminidase inhibition (NI)-enzyme linked lectin assay (ELLA) for detecting and quantifying antibodies in milk samples from infected cows.
Design and population
This study applied NI-ELLA to detect and quantify N1 antibodies in individual (n = 110) and bulk tank (n = 104) bovine milk samples collected from H5N1 HPAI virus-infected dairy cows. The samples were processed using untreated preparations and infranatant collected by centrifugation followed by heat inactivation.
Core methods
The researchers used an established N1 antibody-specific NI-ELLA for DIVA (differentiation of infected from vaccinated animals) surveillance. Antibody detection and quantification were performed on both individual and bulk tank milk samples, with comparisons made to commercial ELISAs and virus neutralization titers.
Main results
NI-ELLA demonstrated strong correlation with commercial ELISAs for antibody detection, and endpoint titers correlated well with virus neutralization titers. Bulk tank milk samples showed higher detection rates and stronger antibody titers in virus isolation-negative samples compared to virus isolation-positive samples, regardless of PCR results.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that higher levels of neutralizing antibodies likely contributed to the lack of infectious virus detection, depending on the infection stage when milk was collected. The novelty lies in the validation of NI-ELLA as a quantitative tool for monitoring antibody responses in bovine milk samples.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support NI-ELLA as a valuable quantitative tool for surveillance and evaluating antibody dynamics in H5N1-infected dairy cattle, enhancing epidemiological monitoring and outbreak management. Future studies can leverage this method to describe antibody responses over extended periods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study focused on a specific assay and sample type without broader generalizability. Limitations include the reliance on a single geographic outbreak and the need for further validation across diverse populations and timeframes.

2024-2026 年美国加利福尼亚州奶牛 暴发 中心家猫中的 血清流行率 流感 A(H5N1) 病毒

Seroprevalence of Influenza A(H5N1) Virus in Domestic Cats at Epicenter of Dairy Cattle Outbreaks, California, USA, 2024–2026
作者： Ian G. Bemis, Ismaila Shittu, Jacob F. Gomez, Esther A. Fadaka, Sofia Perez, Gregory C. Gray, Kristen K. Coleman
摘要中文翻译我们对美国加利福尼亚州甲型 H5N1 流感奶牛 暴发 震中附近的家猫进行了血清学研究。在农场 2 公里范围内采样的 12 只猫中,有 3 只体内含有针对 H5N1 病毒的中和抗体。靠近奶牛场是血清阳性的一个具有统计学意义的危险因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查美国加利福尼亚州奶牛暴发震中附近家猫体内的甲型H5N1流感病毒血清流行率,以评估潜在的人畜共患血清流行率风险。
设计与对象
对美国加利福尼亚州受甲型H5N1流感暴发影响的奶牛场2公里范围内的家猫进行了血清学研究。
核心方法
该研究包括测试家猫的 H5N1 病毒中和抗体,并分析靠近奶牛场作为血清阳性的危险因素。
主要结果
在距离农场 2 公里范围内采样的 12 只猫中,有 3 只具有针对 H5N1 病毒的中和抗体,并且靠近奶牛场是血清阳性的一个具有统计学意义的风险因素。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,家猫可能在有奶牛的地区接触过 H5N1 病毒 暴发,这表明该病毒对猫科动物群体具有潜在的 跨物种溢出 影响。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调,需要在 暴发 地区附近的家猫中检测 H5N1 病毒的 监测,以监测潜在的人畜共患 传播 并为公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
样本量较小(12 只猫)限制了研究结果的普遍性,但靠近农场与血清阳性之间统计上显着的关联提供了暴露风险的中等证据。

Seroprevalence of Influenza A(H5N1) Virus in Domestic Cats at Epicenter of Dairy Cattle Outbreaks, California, USA, 2024–2026

Authors: Ian G. Bemis, Ismaila Shittu, Jacob F. Gomez, Esther A. Fadaka, Sofia Perez, Gregory C. Gray, Kristen K. Coleman
Original abstract (English)
We conducted a serologic study of domestic cats near the epicenter of the dairy cattle outbreaks of influenza A(H5N1) in California, USA. Three of the 12 cats sampled within 2 km of farms had neutralizing antibodies against H5N1 virus. Proximity to dairy farms was a statistically significant risk factor for seropositivity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the seroprevalence of influenza A(H5N1) virus in domestic cats near the epicenter of dairy cattle outbreaks in California, USA, to assess potential zoonotic transmission risks.
Design and population
A serologic study was conducted on domestic cats within 2 km of dairy farms affected by influenza A(H5N1) outbreaks in California, USA.
Core methods
The study involved testing domestic cats for neutralizing antibodies against H5N1 virus and analyzing proximity to dairy farms as a risk factor for seropositivity.
Main results
Three of the 12 cats sampled within 2 km of farms had neutralizing antibodies against H5N1 virus, and proximity to dairy farms was a statistically significant risk factor for seropositivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that domestic cats may be exposed to H5N1 virus in areas with dairy cattle outbreaks, indicating potential spillover of the virus to feline populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need for surveillance of H5N1 virus in domestic cats near outbreak areas to monitor potential zoonotic transmission and inform public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size (12 cats) limits the generalizability of the findings, but the statistically significant association between proximity to farms and seropositivity provides moderate evidence of exposure risk.

抗原进化枝匹配增强了电穿孔或脂质纳米粒子递送的 H5N1 DNA 疫苗 的保护功效 2267127

Antigen clade matching enhances protective efficacy of H5N1 DNA vaccines delivered by electroporation or lipid nanoparticles 2267127
作者： Ebony Gary, Nicholas Tursi, Casey Hojecki, Robert Vendramelli, Bryce Warner, Martina Tomirotti, Cory Livingston, Yangcheng Gao, Sachchidanand Tiwari, Norbert Pardi
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAI) 继续威胁着农业和人类健康。最近的 H5N1 分支已引起人类人畜共患感染,死亡率 感染率接近 50%。然而,目前获得许可的 H5 疫苗 基于祖先菌株,可能无法提供针对循环变种的最佳保护。快速适应的 DNA 疫苗 平台为进化枝特异性保护提供了一种有前途的方法。方法 产生来自两个最近流行的 H5N1 进化枝(2.3.2.1c 和 2.3.4.4b)的表达血凝素 (HA) 的密码子优化质粒 DNA 疫苗,并通过肌内电穿孔 (EP) 或脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 制剂进行递送。分别通过多参数流式细胞术和 ELISpot 以及 ELISA 和假病毒中和评估细胞和体液反应。在致死性 H5N1 小鼠攻击模型中评估了保护功效。结果 进化枝 2.3.2.1c HA (pCamb) 的 EP 递送引发强烈的体液和细胞反应,并实现了针对同源病毒攻击的完全保护,但仅针对异源 2.3.4.4b 攻击提供了部分保护。相比之下,具有进化枝 2.3.4.4b HA (pMich) DNA 的 疫苗接种 支持强大的 免疫应答 和针对当代进化枝挑战的全面保护。通过 EP 使用两种质粒进行联合免疫诱导了广泛的结合和中和抗体,并提供了针对进化枝 2.3.4.4b 攻击的完全保护。此外,pMich 质粒优化的 LNP 的配方在单剂量后产生持久的保护性免疫力,在急性时间点和记忆时间点均有效。结论 这些研究表明,抗原进化枝匹配可能对于预防 H5N1 至关重要,并表明目前储存的 H5N1 疫苗 可能无法预防当代病毒。此外,该数据表明,包括 EP 或 LNP 制剂的 DNA 疫苗 平台可以提供一种灵活的方法,用于快速适应不断变化的流感毒株。资金来源 NIH NIAID CIVICs 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)

设计与对象
通过肌内电穿孔 (EP) 或脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 制剂递送来自最近流行的两个 H5N1 进化枝(2.3.2.1c 和 2.3.4.4b)的表达血凝素 (HA) 的密码子优化质粒 DNA 疫苗,并在致死性 H5N1 小鼠攻击模型中评估保护功效。

分别通过多参数流式细胞术和 ELISpot 以及 ELISA 和假病毒中和评估细胞和体液反应。
主要结果
进化枝 2.3.2.1c HA (pCamb) 的 EP 递送引发了强烈的体液和细胞反应,并实现了针对同源病毒攻击的完全保护,但仅针对异源 2.3.4.4b 攻击提供了部分保护,而具有进化枝 2.3.4.4b HA (pMich) DNA 的 疫苗接种 支持强大的 免疫应答 和针对当代进化枝攻击的全面保护。

这些研究表明,抗原进化枝匹配可能对于预防 H5N1 至关重要,并表明目前储存的 H5N1 疫苗 可能无法预防当代病毒。
科研与公卫意义
DNA 疫苗 平台包括 EP 或 LNP 制剂,可以提供一种灵活的方法来快速适应不断演变的流感毒株,并通过 EP 与两种质粒进行联合免疫,诱导广泛的结合和中和抗体,并提供针对进化枝 2.3.4.4b 挑战的完全保护。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的局限性或偏见;然而,证据强度很高,因为该研究直接观察了小鼠模型的保护功效,并使用多种测定法评估了 免疫应答。

Antigen clade matching enhances protective efficacy of H5N1 DNA vaccines delivered by electroporation or lipid nanoparticles 2267127

Authors: Ebony Gary, Nicholas Tursi, Casey Hojecki, Robert Vendramelli, Bryce Warner, Martina Tomirotti, Cory Livingston, Yangcheng Gao, Sachchidanand Tiwari, Norbert Pardi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIs) continue to threaten both agriculture and human health. Recent H5N1 clades have caused zoonotic infections in humans with mortality rates approaching 50%. However, currently licensed H5 vaccines are based on ancestral strains and may provide suboptimal protection against circulating variants. Rapidly adaptable DNA vaccine platforms offer a promising approach for clade-specific protection. Methods Codon-optimized plasmid DNA vaccines expressing hemagglutinin (HA) from two recently circulating H5N1 clades (2.3.2.1c and 2.3.4.4b) were generated and delivered by either intramuscular electroporation (EP) or a lipid nanoparticle (LNP) formulation. Cellular and humoral responses were evaluated by multiparameter flow cytometry and ELISpot and by ELISA and pseudovirus neutralization, respectively. Protective efficacy was evaluated in lethal H5N1 murine challenge models. Results EP delivery of clade 2.3.2.1c HA (pCamb) elicited strong humoral and cellular responses and achieved complete protection against homologous viral challenge, but only partial protection against heterologous 2.3.4.4b challenge. In contrast, vaccination with clade 2.3.4.4b HA (pMich) DNA supported robust immune responses and full protection against contemporary clade challenge. Co-immunization with both plasmids via EP induced broad binding and neutralizing antibodies and conferred complete protection from clade 2.3.4.4b challenge. Moreover, formulation of the pMich plasmid optimized LNPs generated durable, protective immunity following a single dose, effective at both acute and memory timepoints. Conclusion These studies demonstrate that antigenic clade-matching is likely critical for protection against H5N1 and suggest that currently stockpiled H5N1 vaccines may not protect against contemporary viruses. Further this data suggests that DNA vaccine platforms including EP or LNP formulations can provide a flexible approach for rapid adaptation to evolving influenza strains. Funding Source NIH NIAID CIVICs Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)

Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIs) continue to threaten both agriculture and human health, and currently licensed H5 vaccines based on ancestral strains may provide suboptimal protection against circulating variants.
Design and population
Codon-optimized plasmid DNA vaccines expressing hemagglutinin (HA) from two recently circulating H5N1 clades (2.3.2.1c and 2.3.4.4b) were delivered by either intramuscular electroporation (EP) or a lipid nanoparticle (LNP) formulation, and protective efficacy was evaluated in lethal H5N1 murine challenge models.
Core methods
Cellular and humoral responses were evaluated by multiparameter flow cytometry and ELISpot and by ELISA and pseudovirus neutralization, respectively.
Main results
EP delivery of clade 2.3.2.1c HA (pCamb) elicited strong humoral and cellular responses and achieved complete protection against homologous viral challenge but only partial protection against heterologous 2.3.4.4b challenge, while vaccination with clade 2.3.4.4b HA (pMich) DNA supported robust immune responses and full protection against contemporary clade challenge.
Interpretation and novelty
These studies demonstrate that antigenic clade-matching is likely critical for protection against H5N1 and suggest that currently stockpiled H5N1 vaccines may not protect against contemporary viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
DNA vaccine platforms including EP or LNP formulations can provide a flexible approach for rapid adaptation to evolving influenza strains, and co-immunization with both plasmids via EP induced broad binding and neutralizing antibodies and conferred complete protection from clade 2.3.4.4b challenge.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or bias; however, the evidence strength is high as the study directly observed protective efficacy in murine models and evaluated immune responses using multiple assays.

针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的牛顿差分优化

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2
作者： Siti Amiroch
摘要中文翻译禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。本研究提出了牛顿除差(NDD)优化,这是一种轻量级正对角曲率感知训练策略,作为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。NDD 在支架密钥和相似性聚类协议下的 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC(接收者操作特征 - 曲线下面积)和 PR-AUC(曲线下精确召回面积),并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。这项工作的新颖性在于首次系统评估 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在稀疏高维配体筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案,尽管在实际 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前仍然需要外部验证和前瞻性实验确认。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。
设计与对象
本研究提出牛顿差分(NDD)优化作为一种​​针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。
核心方法
由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。
主要结果
NDD 在支架关键和相似性聚类协议下在 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC 和 PR-AUC,并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。
解释与创新
这项工作的新颖性在于首次对 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD 进行系统评估,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在基于稀疏高维配体的筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的 抗病毒药物 化合物优先排序提供了一种计算有效的优化策略,这可以加速针对这种人畜共患威胁的潜在治疗方法的发现。
局限与证据强度
在实施实际的 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前,外部验证和前瞻性实验确认仍然是必要的。由于研究的计算性质和实验验证的需要,证据强度中等。

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2

Authors: Siti Amiroch
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening. This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization, a lightweight positive diagonal curvature-aware training strategy, as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols. An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds. NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC (Receiver Operating Characteriztic – Area Under the Curve) and PR-AUC (Precision-Recall Area Under the Curve) on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance. The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings, although external validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening.
Design and population
This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols.
Core methods
An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds.
Main results
NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC and PR-AUC on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance.
Interpretation and novelty
The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a computationally efficient optimization strategy for antiviral compound prioritization against avian influenza A/H9N2, which could accelerate the discovery of potential therapeutics for this zoonotic threat.
Limitations and evidence strength
External validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization can be implemented. The evidence strength is moderate due to the computational nature of the study and the need for experimental validation.

激活实验室 监测 对退伍军人健康管理局 H5N1 流感威胁的响应,2024-25

Activating laboratory surveillance response to the threat of H5N1 influenza in the Veterans Health Administration, 2024–25
作者： Cynthia Lucero-Obusan, Joyce S. Chung, Gina Oda, Connor Edson, Aarthi Chary, Mark Holodniy
摘要中文翻译简介 退伍军人健康管理局 (VHA) 动员并调整其已建立的国家基础设施,以加强 2024 年至 2025 年 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 流感大流行。此外,VHA 的公共卫生参考实验室 (PHRL) 扩大了 H5 亚型分析和整个 基因组 测序 (WGS) 的能力,以支持检测。方法 通过定制查询增强常规流感 监测,以识别报告在动物中检测到 H5N1 的高风险县的病例。在与 VHA 临床实验室的协调下,选定的样本被送往 PHRL。收到的 PHRL 亚型标本,其中包括住院病例。将 VHA 中甲型流感病例的人口统计数据与居住在高风险“监测”县(报告在动物中检测到 H5N1)的病例以及样本送往 PHRL 进行 H5 亚型分析的病例进行了比较。结果 从2024年4月1日至2025年5月31日,我们发现了40,406例甲型流感VHA阳性病例(12.5%进行了当地亚型分型),其中5,348例来自“监测”县。PHRL 总共收到了 1,155 份样本进行 H5 亚型分析,并完成了 180 份全基因组测序。与其他甲型流感病例相比,“受监测”县的病例中女性较少,50-69 岁年龄段的病例较少,西班牙裔病例较多。没有检测到H5N1。结论 我们强调了在一次公认的大流行病期间,大型国家卫生系统中 H5N1 流行病学和实验室 监测 的复杂性和所需资源。由于 PHRL 的集中亚型分析和全基因组测序并不全面,因此当地 VA 临床实验室需要额外资源来进行分散的甲型流感亚型分析。尽管尚未在 VHA 内的退伍军人中观察到 H5 流感,但持续存在的风险值得继续保持警惕和监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
退伍军人健康管理局 (VHA) 动员并调整其已建立的国家基础设施,以在 2024 年至 2025 年期间加强 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 流感大流行。
设计与对象
在与 VHA 临床实验室的协调下,选定的样本被送往 PHRL 进行分型,其中包括住院病例。收到的 PHRL 亚型样本,并将 VHA 中甲型流感病例的人口统计数据与居住在高风险“监测”县的病例进行了比较。
核心方法
VHA 的公共卫生参考实验室 (PHRL) 扩大了 H5 亚型分析和整个 基因组 测序 (WGS) 的能力以支持检测。通过定制查询增强了常规流感 监测,以识别报告在动物中检测到 H5N1 的高风险县的病例。
主要结果
从2024年4月1日至2025年5月31日,共发现40,406例甲型流感VHA阳性病例(12.5%为当地亚型),其中5,348例来自“监测”县。PHRL 总共收到了 1,155 份样本进行 H5 亚型分析,并完成了 180 份全基因组测序。没有检测到H5N1。
解释与创新
作者强调了在公认的泛动物疫情期间,大型国家卫生系统内 H5N1 流行病学和实验室 监测 的复杂性和所需资源,强调尽管没有观察到病例,但仍需要继续保持警惕。
科研与公卫意义
尽管尚未在 VHA 内的退伍军人中观察到 H5 流感,但持续存在的风险值得继续保持警惕和监测。由于 PHRL 的集中亚型分析和全基因组测序并不全面,因此当地 VA 临床实验室需要额外资源来进行分散的甲型流感亚型分析。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏 H5N1 检测且依赖集中分型(不全面),证据强度中等。研究设计的局限性包括需要更分散的子类型资源来增强 监测 的覆盖范围。

Activating laboratory surveillance response to the threat of H5N1 influenza in the Veterans Health Administration, 2024–25

Authors: Cynthia Lucero-Obusan, Joyce S. Chung, Gina Oda, Connor Edson, Aarthi Chary, Mark Holodniy
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The Veterans Health Administration (VHA) mobilized and adapted its established national infrastructure to enhance influenza surveillance during 2024–2025 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) panzootic. Additionally, VHA's Public Health Reference Laboratory (PHRL) expanded capacity for H5 subtyping and whole genome sequencing (WGS) to support detection. Methods Routine influenza surveillance was enhanced with customized queries to identify cases in high-risk counties with reported H5N1 detected in animals. In coordination with VHA clinical laboratories, select samples were sent to PHRL. PHRL subtyped specimens received, which included hospitalized cases. Demographics of influenza A cases in VHA were compared with those residing in high-risk “surveilled” counties (with reported H5N1 detection in animals) and those whose specimens were sent to PHRL for H5 subtyping. Results From 4/1/2024-5/31/2025, we identified 40,406 positive influenza A VHA cases (12.5% with local subtyping performed), including 5,348 cases from “surveilled” counties. A total of 1,155 specimens were received by PHRL for H5 subtyping and 180 completed WGS. Cases from “surveilled” counties had fewer females, fewer in 50–69 age group and more of Hispanic ethnicity compared to other influenza A cases. No H5N1 was detected. Conclusion We highlight the complexity and resources necessary for epidemiologic and laboratory surveillance of H5N1 within a large, national health system during a recognized panzootic. Additional resources for decentralized influenza A subtyping at local VA clinical laboratories are needed as centralized subtyping and WGS at PHRL was not comprehensive. Although H5 influenza has not yet been observed among Veterans within VHA, the ongoing risk warrants continued vigilance and monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The Veterans Health Administration (VHA) mobilized and adapted its established national infrastructure to enhance influenza surveillance during 2024–2025 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) panzootic.
Design and population
In coordination with VHA clinical laboratories, select samples were sent to PHRL for subtyping, which included hospitalized cases. PHRL subtyped specimens received, and demographics of influenza A cases in VHA were compared with those residing in high-risk “surveilled” counties.
Core methods
VHA's Public Health Reference Laboratory (PHRL) expanded capacity for H5 subtyping and whole genome sequencing (WGS) to support detection. Routine influenza surveillance was enhanced with customized queries to identify cases in high-risk counties with reported H5N1 detected in animals.
Main results
From 4/1/2024-5/31/2025, 40,406 positive influenza A VHA cases were identified (12.5% with local subtyping), including 5,348 cases from “surveilled” counties. A total of 1,155 specimens were received by PHRL for H5 subtyping and 180 completed WGS. No H5N1 was detected.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight the complexity and resources necessary for epidemiologic and laboratory surveillance of H5N1 within a large, national health system during a recognized panzootic, emphasizing the need for continued vigilance despite no observed cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
Although H5 influenza has not yet been observed among Veterans within VHA, the ongoing risk warrants continued vigilance and monitoring. Additional resources for decentralized influenza A subtyping at local VA clinical laboratories are needed as centralized subtyping and WGS at PHRL was not comprehensive.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of H5N1 detection and the reliance on centralized subtyping, which was not comprehensive. The study design limitations include the need for more decentralized subtyping resources to enhance surveillance coverage.

与哺乳动物适应高致病性 H5N1 禽流感 病毒相关的 PB2 突变 的鉴定和表征

Identification and characterization of PB2 mutations associated with mammalian adaptation of highly pathogenic H5N1 avian influenza viruses
作者： Simin Cui, Xiaoning Hou, Siyu Pu, Junhao Luo, Haijun Zhu, Peiqing He, Chen Gao, Rongbao Gao, Jun Han
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 亚型 H5N1 一直在 野生鸟类 人群和家禽中持续传播。它的持续传播导致家禽和美国奶牛群体出现 暴发,以及从事家禽和奶牛养殖的个人出现零星严重感染。这些事件引起了人们对该病毒可能成为大流行毒株的担忧。为了阐明促进 H5N1 跨物种适应的分子决定因素并评估其对公共卫生的影响,我们对病毒聚合酶亚基 PB2 进行了一系列序列分析和特定位点 突变,以确定其对聚合酶活性和病毒感染性的影响。结果表明,PB2蛋白中的三个突变(E362G、D441N和M631L)在哺乳动物细胞中呈现出与增强病毒复制相关的协同效应。与 2.3.4.4b 分支的原始分离菌株 A/chicken/NL/FAV-0033/2021 相比,这三个 突变 主要在 2021 年至 2024 年期间从牛和其他哺乳动物 宿主 获得的分离株中被鉴定。M631L 突变 被鉴定为哺乳动物中聚合酶活性增加的主要决定因素细胞中,显着增强了 PB2 与 ANP32A 的结合亲和力。突变 E362G和D441N没有显着增加聚合酶活性和病毒复制,但增强了PB2与ANP32A的结合亲和力。突变与E362G、D441N和M631L的组合导致H5N1病毒的聚合酶活性和病毒复制显着增加,并显着升高H5N1感染小鼠肺部的病毒载量并加重肺部病理。这些发现表明,PB2-M631L 突变 构成了 H5N1 适应哺乳动物 宿主 的关键分子标记,而 E362G 和 D441N 突变 可能作为支持性调节因子,优化了这种 宿主 适应过程。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定促进 H5N1 禽流感 病毒适应哺乳动物 宿主 的分子决定因素,因为该病毒在鸟类种群中持续传播,并且存在演变成大流行毒株的风险。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 H5N1 病毒 PB2 蛋白的序列分析和特定位点 突变,检查了 2021 年至 2024 年间来自牛和其他哺乳动物 宿主 的分离株,以及原始 2.3.4.4b 分支毒株 A/chicken/NL/FAV-0033/2021。
核心方法
研究人员对PB2蛋白进行了系列序列分析和特定位点突变,以评估其对聚合酶活性和病毒感染性的影响,使用体外测定和小鼠模型来评估复制和发病机制。
主要结果
三个 PB2 突变(E362G、D441N 和 M631L)被发现具有协同作用,增强哺乳动物细胞中的病毒复制。M631L 突变 显着增加聚合酶活性和病毒复制,而 E362G 和 D441N 也增强 PB2 与 ANP32A 的结合亲和力。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,M631L 突变 是 H5N1 适应哺乳动物 宿主 的关键分子标记,E362G 和 D441N 充当优化这一过程的支持因子。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 H5N1 适应的分子机制提供了重要的见解,可以为 监测 策略和潜在人畜共患 传播 和大流行出现的风险评估提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和小鼠模型分析,可能无法完全反映体内动力学。然而,由于在哺乳动物 宿主 分离株中直接鉴定出 突变 及其功能验证,证据是强有力的。

Identification and characterization of PB2 mutations associated with mammalian adaptation of highly pathogenic H5N1 avian influenza viruses

Authors: Simin Cui, Xiaoning Hou, Siyu Pu, Junhao Luo, Haijun Zhu, Peiqing He, Chen Gao, Rongbao Gao, Jun Han
Original abstract (English)
The highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) subtype H5N1 has been continuously circulating among wild bird populations and domestic poultry. It’s ongoing circulation has led to outbreaks in poultry and U.S. dairy cattle populations, as well as sporadic severe infections in individuals engaged in poultry and dairy farming. These occurrences have raised concerns about the potential evolution of this virus into a pandemic strain. To elucidate the molecular determinants facilitating H5N1 cross-species adaptation and to evaluate its implications for public health, we conducted serials of sequence analysis and specific-site mutations on the viral polymerase subunit PB2 to determine its effect on polymerase activity and viral infectivity. The results showed that three mutations in the PB2 protein (E362G, D441N and M631L) were presented cooperative effects associated with enhanced viral replication in mammalian cells. Compared to the original isolated strain of the 2.3.4.4b clade, A/chicken/NL/FAV-0033/2021, these three mutations were predominantly identified in isolates obtained from cattle and other mammalian hosts between 2021 and 2024. The M631L mutation, identified as the primary determinant of increased polymerase activity in mammalian cells, significantly enhanced the binding affinity of PB2 to ANP32A. The mutation E362G and D441N did not increased polymerase activity and viral replication significantly but enhanced binding affinity of PB2 to ANP32A. The combined mutations with E362G, D441N and M631L resulted in a significantly increased polymerase activity and viral replication in H5N1 virus, and significantly elevated viral loads and aggravated pulmonary pathology in lungs of mice with H5N1 infection. These findings indicate that the PB2-M631L mutation constitutes a crucial molecular marker for the adaptation of H5N1 to mammalian hosts, whereas the E362G and D441N mutations likely function as supportive modulatory factors that optimize this host-adaptation process.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify molecular determinants that facilitate H5N1 avian influenza virus adaptation to mammalian hosts, given its continuous circulation among bird populations and the risk of evolving into a pandemic strain.
Design and population
The study involved sequence analysis and specific-site mutations on the PB2 protein of H5N1 viruses, examining isolates from cattle and other mammalian hosts between 2021 and 2024, as well as the original 2.3.4.4b clade strain A/chicken/NL/FAV-0033/2021.
Core methods
The researchers performed serial sequence analysis and specific-site mutations on the PB2 protein to assess its effect on polymerase activity and viral infectivity, using in vitro assays and mouse models to evaluate replication and pathogenesis.
Main results
Three PB2 mutations (E362G, D441N, and M631L) were found to have cooperative effects, enhancing viral replication in mammalian cells. The M631L mutation significantly increased polymerase activity and viral replication, while E362G and D441N also enhanced binding affinity of PB2 to ANP32A.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the M631L mutation is a key molecular marker for H5N1 adaptation to mammalian hosts, with E362G and D441N acting as supportive factors that optimize this process.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide critical insights into the molecular mechanisms of H5N1 adaptation, which can inform surveillance strategies and risk assessments for potential zoonotic transmission and pandemic emergence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and mouse model analyses, which may not fully reflect in vivo dynamics. However, the evidence is strong due to the direct identification of mutations in isolates from mammalian hosts and their functional validation.

2025年从中国江苏省鸡中分离的禽流感(H9N2)病毒的遗传和致病性研究

Genetic and Pathogenicity Studies of Avian Influenza (H9N2) Virus Isolated from Chickens in Jiangsu Province, China, in 2025
作者： Yue Li, Xingdong Song, Tengfei Gao, Guangyuan Wang, Hongyin Wei, Jiajun Wang, Wenjun Jiang, Ruihua Zhang, Yu Meng, Shijin Jiang
摘要中文翻译本研究于2025年3月在中国江苏省分离到1株H9N2亚型禽流感病毒(AIV)。根据系统发育分析,H9N2分离物的HA基因片段被归类为B4.7谱系,其余7个片段属于欧亚谱系。这种H9N2病毒在进化期间在中国多个地区经历了复杂的基因重排,并确定了关键氨基酸取代,这可能会增强其病毒聚合酶活性和在小鼠中的复制效率。至关重要的是,小鼠实验证明 H9N2 病毒无需事先适应即可在 BALB/c 小鼠的肺部和鼻甲中有效复制。此外,家禽感染研究表明,H9N2病毒在鸡中的复制和传播效率比在鸭中更高。此外,病毒可以通过受感染的鸡传播给接触过的鸭子,其效率比鸭子传播给鸭子的效率更高。此外,在接种的鸡和鸭的肺部,模式识别受体(PRR)和细胞因子的mRNA表达水平上调,而鸭的总体倍数增幅低于鸡。总之,这些结果强调了鸡源性 H9N2 AIV 带来的潜在公共卫生风险,强调了对家禽 H9N2 AIV 加强常规 监测 的迫切需要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查2025年从中国江苏省鸡中分离的H9N2 禽流感病毒的遗传特征和致病潜力,以评估其公共卫生风险。
设计与对象
该研究于2025年3月从中国江苏省的鸡中分离出H9N2 进化病毒,并通过小鼠和家禽感染实验分析其基因进化和致病性。
核心方法
对 H9N2 病毒进行系统发育分析以确定其谱系,并在中国多个地区检查了基因重排。进行小鼠和家禽感染研究以评估复制效率和 传播 动态。
主要结果
小鼠实验表明,H9N2病毒无需事先适应即可在BALB/c小鼠的肺部和鼻甲中有效复制。家禽感染研究表明,该病毒在鸡中的复制和传播效率比在鸭中更高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,鸡源性 H9N2 病毒在小鼠体内的复制效率有所提高,并且由于其在家禽物种之间有效 传播 的潜力,可能会造成公共健康风险。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了在家禽中监测 H9N2 禽流感 病毒的重要性,以更好地了解其感染人畜共患 传播 的潜力,并为 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到动物模型使用的限制,可能无法完全反映人类感染动态。证据是温和的,因为研究结果是基于实验数据而不是现场观察。

Genetic and Pathogenicity Studies of Avian Influenza (H9N2) Virus Isolated from Chickens in Jiangsu Province, China, in 2025

Authors: Yue Li, Xingdong Song, Tengfei Gao, Guangyuan Wang, Hongyin Wei, Jiajun Wang, Wenjun Jiang, Ruihua Zhang, Yu Meng, Shijin Jiang
Original abstract (English)
In this study, one H9N2 subtype avian influenza virus (AIV) was isolated in Jiangsu Province, China, in March 2025. According to phylogenetic analysis, the HA gene fragment of the H9N2 isolate was classified as the B4.7 lineage, with the remaining seven segments falling into the Eurasian lineage. This H9N2 virus underwent complex genetic reassortment across multiple regions in China during its evolution, and key amino acid substitutions were identified, which may enhance its viral polymerase activity and replication efficiency in mice. Critically, a mouse experiment demonstrated that the H9N2 virus replicated effectively in the lungs and nasal turbinates of BALB/c mice without requiring prior adaptation. In addition, poultry infection studies showed that the H9N2 virus could replicate and spread more efficiently in chickens than in ducks. Moreover, the virus could be transmitted to contacted ducks through infected chickens with greater efficiency than from ducks to ducks. Furthermore, in the lungs of inoculated chickens and ducks, the mRNA expression levels of pattern-recognition receptors (PRRs) and cytokines were upregulated, while the overall fold increase in ducks was lower than that in chickens. In conclusion, these results highlighted the potential public health risk presented by chicken-derived H9N2 AIV, emphasizing the critical need for enhanced routine surveillance of H9N2 AIVs from poultry.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the genetic characteristics and pathogenic potential of an H9N2 avian influenza virus isolated from chickens in Jiangsu Province, China, in 2025, to assess its public health risk.
Design and population
The study involved isolating an H9N2 avian influenza virus from chickens in Jiangsu Province, China, in March 2025, and analyzing its genetic evolution and pathogenicity through mouse and poultry infection experiments.
Core methods
The H9N2 virus was subjected to phylogenetic analysis to determine its lineage, and genetic reassortment was examined across multiple regions in China. Mouse and poultry infection studies were conducted to assess replication efficiency and transmission dynamics.
Main results
A mouse experiment showed that the H9N2 virus replicated effectively in the lungs and nasal turbinates of BALB/c mice without prior adaptation. Poultry infection studies revealed that the virus replicated and spread more efficiently in chickens than in ducks.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the chicken-derived H9N2 virus has enhanced replication efficiency in mice and may pose a public health risk due to its potential for efficient transmission between poultry species.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of monitoring H9N2 avian influenza viruses in poultry to better understand their potential for zoonotic transmission and to inform surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of animal models, which may not fully reflect human infection dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on experimental data rather than field observations.

RfxCas13d 介导 高致病性禽流感 病毒的强大跨毒株抑制

RfxCas13d mediates robust cross-strain suppression of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses
作者： Sudip Dhakal, Aaron J. Smith, Emiliana Weiss, Md Zohorul Islam, Lynn Nazareth, Terence Lee, Tamara Gough, Kiran Krishnankutty Nair, Laurence Wilson, James W. Wynne
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 继续在鸟类和哺乳动物中引起严重疾病,H5N1 跨物种溢出 的反复出现凸显了对广泛保护性 抗病毒药物 策略的需求。在这里,我们开发了基于 RfxCas13d 的可编程 RNA 靶向 抗病毒药物 平台,并评估其在鸟类细胞中的活性。对鸡 DF1 成纤维细胞中五种 Cas13 直向同源物的筛选表明,RfxCas13d 是最有效且耐受性最好的效应子。针对 A/WSN/033 (H1N1) 和多种 HPAIV 分离株(包括分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 的成员)测试了针对正义和负义流感 RNA 保守区域的病毒特异性 CRISPR RNA (crRNA)。靶向正义 RNA 具有优异的流感抑制活性,并且通过多重 crRNA 表达进一步增强。这些发现将 RfxCas13d 确立为多功能 RNA 引导的 抗病毒药物 平台,并为通过保守 RNA 靶向控制跨毒株流感奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过制定广泛保护的 抗病毒药物 策略来解决 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 的持续威胁。背景强调了 H5N1 的重复 跨物种溢出 以及对可针对多种菌株的 抗病毒药物 方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在禽类细胞中使用 RfxCas13d 开发和评估可编程 RNA 靶向 抗病毒药物 平台。它专注于 禽流感 病毒,包括 H5N1,并针对多种 HPAIV 分离株测试了该平台。
核心方法
研究人员在鸡 DF1 成纤维细胞中筛选了 5 个 Cas13 直向同源物,以确定最有效和耐受性最好的效应子,即 RfxCas13d。然后,他们测试了针对 A/WSN/033 (H1N1) 和各种 HPAIV 分离株(包括分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 成员)的流感 RNA 保守区域的病毒特异性 CRISPR RNA (crRNA)。
主要结果
对鸡 DF1 成纤维细胞中五种 Cas13 直向同源物的筛选表明,RfxCas13d 是最有效且耐受性最好的效应子。该研究还表明,靶向正义 RNA 具有卓越的流感抑制活性,而多重 crRNA 表达可进一步增强这种活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RfxCas13d 是一种多功能 RNA 引导的 抗病毒药物 平台,能够抑制交叉菌株,为通过保守 RNA 靶向控制流感提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发 抗病毒药物 策略奠定了基础,该策略可以针对 禽流感 的多种菌株,潜在地改善 监测、诊断 以及对禽类和哺乳动物 宿主 中 HPAIV 的控制。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于禽类细胞的体外测试,由于缺乏体内验证和长期疗效数据,证据强度中等。

RfxCas13d mediates robust cross-strain suppression of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses

Authors: Sudip Dhakal, Aaron J. Smith, Emiliana Weiss, Md Zohorul Islam, Lynn Nazareth, Terence Lee, Tamara Gough, Kiran Krishnankutty Nair, Laurence Wilson, James W. Wynne
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) continue to cause substantial disease in birds and mammals, with repeated H5N1 spillovers highlighting the need for broadly protective antiviral strategies. Here, we develop a programmable RNA-targeting antiviral platform based on RfxCas13d and evaluate its activity in avian cells. Screening of five Cas13 orthologs in chicken DF1 fibroblasts revealed RfxCas13d as the most potent and well tolerated effector. Virus-specific CRISPR RNAs (crRNAs) targeting conserved regions of positive- and negative-sense influenza RNA were tested against A/WSN/033 (H1N1) and multiple HPAIV isolates, including a member of clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1. Targeting positive-sense RNA conferred superior influenza inhibitory activity and was further enhanced by multiplexed crRNA expression. These findings establish RfxCas13d as a versatile RNA-guided antiviral platform and provide a foundation for cross-strain influenza control through conserved RNA targeting.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the ongoing threat of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) by developing a broadly protective antiviral strategy. The background highlights the repeated spillovers of H5N1 and the need for antiviral approaches that can target multiple strains.
Design and population
The study involved developing and evaluating a programmable RNA-targeting antiviral platform using RfxCas13d in avian cells. It focused on avian influenza viruses, including H5N1, and tested the platform against multiple HPAIV isolates.
Core methods
The researchers screened five Cas13 orthologs in chicken DF1 fibroblasts to identify the most potent and well-tolerated effector, which was RfxCas13d. They then tested virus-specific CRISPR RNAs (crRNAs) targeting conserved regions of influenza RNA against A/WSN/033 (H1N1) and various HPAIV isolates, including a clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 member.
Main results
Screening of five Cas13 orthologs in chicken DF1 fibroblasts revealed RfxCas13d as the most potent and well-tolerated effector. The study also demonstrated that targeting positive-sense RNA conferred superior influenza inhibitory activity, which was further enhanced by multiplexed crRNA expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish RfxCas13d as a versatile RNA-guided antiviral platform capable of cross-strain suppression, offering a new approach for controlling influenza through conserved RNA targeting.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for developing antiviral strategies that can target multiple strains of avian influenza, potentially improving surveillance, diagnosis, and control of HPAIVs in both avian and mammalian hosts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro testing in avian cells, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of in vivo validation and long-term efficacy data.

使用微流控非竞争性荧光偏振免疫分析检测禽类口咽拭子样本中的 H5 亚型 禽流感 病毒。

Detection of H5 subtype avian influenza virus in avian oropharyngeal swab samples using a microfluidic non-competitive fluorescence polarization immunoassay.
作者： Kazuki Takahashi, Yohei Takeda, Keine Nishiyama, Mao Fukuyama, Masatoshi Maeki, Akihiko Ishida, Hirofumi Tani, Ayuko Imai, Koji Shigemura, Akihide Hibara
摘要中文翻译快速、简单的 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 现场检测方法对于有效现场 监测 至关重要。In this study,a microfluidic device-based non-competitive fluorescence polarization immunoassay (NC-FPIA) using an ATTO 647N-labeled hemagglutinin fragment of H5 subtype AIV (H5-AIV) as a tracer was developed for the quantification of H5-AIV in avian oropharyngeal swab samples.拭子基质的自发荧光显着干扰荧光偏振测量。然而,H5-AIV的定量检测是通过80倍稀释抑制基质效应来实现的。检测限为186.4 μg mL -1。该测定可在 20 分钟内完成,无需复杂的样品制备,使其适合快速现场应用,包括 野生鸟类 和家禽中 AIV 的现场 监测。此外,通过设计适当的示踪剂,该方法可以扩展到其他 AIV 亚型。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
快速、简单的 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 现场检测对于 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的有效现场检测至关重要,但传统方法往往复杂或缓慢。本研究旨在满足对快速、可现场部署的检测来量化禽类口咽拭子样本中 H5 亚型 AIV 的需求。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种基于微流体装置的非竞争性荧光偏振免疫分析 (NC-FPIA),使用 H5 亚型 AIV (H5-AIV) 的 ATTO 647N 标记血凝素片段作为示踪剂,用于禽类口咽拭子样本的定量。
核心方法
该测定采用具有荧光偏振检测功能的微流体装置,并利用 ATTO 647N 标记的 H5 血凝素片段示踪剂。通过 80 倍稀释步骤减轻了拭子样品的基质干扰,并且无需复杂的样品制备即可在 20 分钟内完成测定。
主要结果
H5-AIV的检出限为186.4 μg·mL−1,通过将拭子样品稀释80倍抑制基质干扰后实现定量检测。
解释与创新
作者证明,微流控 NC-FPIA 能够以最少的样品制备快速定量检测禽类口咽拭子中的 H5-AIV,解决了 监测 领域能力的关键差距。该方法通过示踪剂设计对其他 AIV 亚型的适应性被强调为一个新颖的特征。
科研与公卫意义
该检测可以通过提供快速、便携式和定量检测工具来增强 野生鸟类 和家禽中的实时 AIV 暴发,从而有可能改善 暴发 的响应和控制措施。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象水平的数据,缺乏临床或现场验证,并且没有解决低致病性菌株的敏感性或与其他 AIV 亚型的交叉反应性。因此,证据的强度较低。

Detection of H5 subtype avian influenza virus in avian oropharyngeal swab samples using a microfluidic non-competitive fluorescence polarization immunoassay.

Authors: Kazuki Takahashi, Yohei Takeda, Keine Nishiyama, Mao Fukuyama, Masatoshi Maeki, Akihiko Ishida, Hirofumi Tani, Ayuko Imai, Koji Shigemura, Akihide Hibara
Original abstract (English)
Rapid and simple methods for on-site detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) are essential for effective field surveillance. In this study, a microfluidic device-based non-competitive fluorescence polarization immunoassay (NC-FPIA) using an ATTO 647N-labeled hemagglutinin fragment of H5 subtype AIV (H5-AIV) as a tracer was developed for the quantification of H5-AIV in avian oropharyngeal swab samples. Autofluorescence from the swab matrix significantly interfered with fluorescence polarization measurements. However, quantitative detection of H5-AIV was achieved by suppressing the matrix effect through 80-fold dilution. The limit of detection was 186.4 μg mL -1 . The assay can be completed within 20 minutes without complex sample preparation, making it suitable for rapid on-site applications, including field surveillance of AIV in wild birds and poultry. Furthermore, by designing appropriate tracers, the method can be extended to other AIV subtypes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid and simple on-site detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) is critical for effective field surveillance, yet conventional methods are often complex or slow. This study aimed to address the need for a rapid, field-deployable assay to quantify H5 subtype AIV in avian oropharyngeal swab samples.
Design and population
The study developed a microfluidic device-based non-competitive fluorescence polarization immunoassay (NC-FPIA) using an ATTO 647N-labeled hemagglutinin fragment of H5 subtype AIV (H5-AIV) as a tracer for quantification in avian oropharyngeal swab samples.
Core methods
The assay employed a microfluidic device with fluorescence polarization detection, utilizing an ATTO 647N-labeled H5 hemagglutinin fragment tracer. Matrix interference from swab samples was mitigated by an 80-fold dilution step, and the assay was completed within 20 minutes without complex sample preparation.
Main results
The limit of detection for H5-AIV was 186.4 μg mL−1, and quantitative detection was achieved after suppressing matrix interference through 80-fold dilution of the swab samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors demonstrated that the microfluidic NC-FPIA enables rapid, quantitative detection of H5-AIV in avian oropharyngeal swabs with minimal sample preparation, addressing a key gap in field surveillance capabilities. The method’s adaptability to other AIV subtypes via tracer design was highlighted as a novel feature.
Scientific and public-health significance
This assay could enhance real-time AIV surveillance in wild birds and poultry by providing a rapid, portable, and quantitative detection tool, potentially improving outbreak response and control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level data, lacks clinical or field validation, and does not address sensitivity in low-pathogenicity strains or cross-reactivity with other AIV subtypes. The strength of evidence is therefore low.
