使用新型细菌酶组合化学 (BECC) TLR4 佐剂优化 禽流感 疫苗

Optimizing an avian influenza vaccine using a novel Bacterial Enzymatic Combinatorial Chemistry (BECC) TLR4 adjuvant
作者： Devon V. Riley, Lauren Baracco, Sayan Das, Brandon M. Tenaglia, Sydney Speed, Carly Dillen, Juliahna Hayes, Samanta Del Veliz, Haye Nijhuis, Valerie Le Sage
摘要中文翻译摘要 开发具有广泛保护性和剂量节约型流感 疫苗 仍然是一个严峻的挑战,特别是对于具有大流行潜力的人畜共患 H5N1 病毒株。本研究评估了 BECC470s(一种合成 TLR4 佐剂)在小鼠模型中增强重组 H5 血凝素 (rHA) 疫苗接种 的免疫原性和保护功效的能力。在初免-加强模型中,在同源 2004 H5N1 攻击后,BECC470s 佐剂的 rHA 引发了强大的 IgG1/IgG2a 抗体反应并完全存活。尽管通过 ELISA,BECC470 扩大了抗体与可变 HA 头和保守茎结构域的结合,但功能性中和抗体反应仅限于匹配的 2004 年 H5N1 分离株,没有检测到对 2022 年或 2024 年分离的 H5N1 病毒的中和作用。这些数据表明,BECC470 增强了结合抗体反应的幅度和明显的交叉反应性,同时保持了毒株特异性中和活性,支持其作为佐剂的潜力。下一代流感 疫苗,同时强调需要进一步优化以实现真正的交叉中和保护。重要性 BECC470s 是一种合成 TLR4 激动剂佐剂,可增强重组 H5 血凝素 疫苗接种 在小鼠体内的广度和效力。通过驱动平衡的抗体反应、针对同源攻击的完全保护以及针对 HA 头部和茎部的中和活性,BECC470 的性能优于比较剂 TLR4 佐剂。这些初步研究结果表明,BECC470 能够节省抗原,同时增加抗原免疫原性,支持其对当前 H5N1 疫苗 的潜在转化应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
开发具有广泛保护性和剂量节约型流感 疫苗 仍然是一个严峻的挑战,特别是对于具有大流行潜力的人畜共患 H5N1 毒株。
设计与对象
本研究评估了 BECC470s(一种合成 TLR4 佐剂)在小鼠模型中增强重组 H5 血凝素 (rHA) 疫苗接种 的免疫原性和保护功效的能力。
核心方法
在初免-加强模型中,在同源 2004 H5N1 攻击后,BECC470s 佐剂的 rHA 引发了强大的 IgG1/IgG2a 抗体反应并完全存活。
主要结果
尽管通过 ELISA,BECC470 扩大了抗体与可变 HA 头和保守茎结构域的结合,但功能性中和抗体反应仅限于匹配的 2004 年 H5N1 分离株,没有检测到对 2022 年或 2024 年分离的 H5N1 病毒的中和作用。
解释与创新
这些数据表明,BECC470s 增强了结合抗体反应的强度和明显的交叉反应性,同时保持毒株特异性中和活性,支持其作为下一代流感 疫苗 佐剂的潜力,同时强调需要进一步优化以实现真正的交叉中和保护。
科研与公卫意义
BECC470s 是一种合成的 TLR4 激动剂佐剂,可增强小鼠中重组 H5 血凝 疫苗接种 的广度和效力,在增加抗原免疫原性的同时实现抗原保留,支持其对当前 H5N1 疫苗 的潜在转化应用。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于小鼠模型和初步发现,中和活性仅限于匹配的 2004 年 H5N1 分离株,并且没有检测到较新的 H5N1 分离株的中和作用,表明转化潜力中等强度,但需要进一步优化交叉中和保护。

Optimizing an avian influenza vaccine using a novel Bacterial Enzymatic Combinatorial Chemistry (BECC) TLR4 adjuvant

Authors: Devon V. Riley, Lauren Baracco, Sayan Das, Brandon M. Tenaglia, Sydney Speed, Carly Dillen, Juliahna Hayes, Samanta Del Veliz, Haye Nijhuis, Valerie Le Sage
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The development of broadly protective and dose-sparing influenza vaccines remains a critical challenge, particularly for zoonotic H5N1 strains with pandemic potential. This study evaluates BECC470s, a synthetic TLR4 adjuvant, for its ability to enhance the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of recombinant H5 hemagglutinin (rHA) vaccination in murine models. BECC470s-adjuvanted rHA elicited robust IgG1/IgG2a antibody responses and complete survival following homologous 2004 H5N1 challenge in a prime–boost model. Although BECC470s broadened antibody binding to both variable HA head and conserved stalk domains by ELISA, functional neutralizing antibody responses were restricted to the matched 2004 H5N1 isolate, with no detectable neutralization of H5N1 viruses isolated in 2022 or 2024. These data indicate that BECC470s enhances the magnitude and apparent cross-reactivity of binding antibody responses while maintaining strain-specific neutralizing activity, supporting its potential as an adjuvant for next-generation influenza vaccines while underscoring the need for further optimization to achieve true cross-neutralizing protection. IMPORTANCE BECC470s is a synthetic TLR4 agonist adjuvant that enhances the breadth and potency of recombinant H5 hemagglutinin vaccination in mice. By driving balanced antibody responses, complete protection against homologous challenge, and neutralizing activity targeting both HA head and stalk, BECC470s outperforms the comparator TLR4 adjuvant. These preliminary findings indicate that BECC470s enables antigen sparing while increasing antigen immunogenicity, supporting its potential translational application to current H5N1 vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The development of broadly protective and dose-sparing influenza vaccines remains a critical challenge, particularly for zoonotic H5N1 strains with pandemic potential.
Design and population
This study evaluates BECC470s, a synthetic TLR4 adjuvant, for its ability to enhance the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of recombinant H5 hemagglutinin (rHA) vaccination in murine models.
Core methods
BECC470s-adjuvanted rHA elicited robust IgG1/IgG2a antibody responses and complete survival following homologous 2004 H5N1 challenge in a prime–boost model.
Main results
Although BECC470s broadened antibody binding to both variable HA head and conserved stalk domains by ELISA, functional neutralizing antibody responses were restricted to the matched 2004 H5N1 isolate, with no detectable neutralization of H5N1 viruses isolated in 2022 or 2024.
Interpretation and novelty
These data indicate that BECC470s enhances the magnitude and apparent cross-reactivity of binding antibody responses while maintaining strain-specific neutralizing activity, supporting its potential as an adjuvant for next-generation influenza vaccines while underscoring the need for further optimization to achieve true cross-neutralizing protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
BECC470s is a synthetic TLR4 agonist adjuvant that enhances the breadth and potency of recombinant H5 hemagglutination vaccination in mice, enabling antigen sparing while increasing antigen immunogenicity, supporting its potential translational application to current H5N1 vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to murine models and preliminary findings, with neutralizing activity restricted to the matched 2004 H5N1 isolate and no detectable neutralization of more recent H5N1 isolates, indicating moderate strength for translational potential but requiring further optimization for cross-neutralizing protection.

使用多标准决策模型绘制哈萨克斯坦家禽 高致病性禽流感 的区级风险图

District-Level Risk Mapping of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza in Poultry in Kazakhstan Using a Multi-Criteria Decision Model
作者： Aizada A. Mukhanbetkaliyeva, Irene Iglesias Martín, Fedor I. Кorennoy, Alimzhan S. Kadyrov, Yersyn Y. Mukhanbetkaliyev, Saidulla I. Ruzmatov, Asem Zh. Abenova, Temirlan G. Bakishev, Andres M. Perez, Sarsenbay K. Abdrakhmanov
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 是一种具有人畜共患潜力的跨界鸟类疾病。Since 2021,H5 HPAI virus variants have spread globally,with migratory waterfowl playing a key role in transcontinental dissemination.哈萨克斯坦位于主要迁徙路线的交汇处,造成病毒传入家禽的持续风险。虽然哈萨克斯坦已探讨了 野生鸟类 的高致病性禽流感风险,但家禽的空间风险仍未得到充分评估。在此,我们使用多标准决策分析 (TOPSIS) 绘制了哈萨克斯坦 174 个行政区家禽高致病性禽流感风险地图,该分析整合了与 野生鸟类 栖息地、环境中病毒存活和家禽养殖场普查相关的五个定量风险指标。获得的区级风险指数与暴发历史数据(2005-2025年)吻合较好,受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线下面积为0.899(95% CI:0.846-0.952)。大多数地区(56%)的风险可以忽略不计,而中风险和高风险的集群发生在北部、中部和南部地区以及里海附近。这些发现为针对高风险地区的 监测 和生物安全措施提供了一个可操作的、空间明确的工具,最终有助于减轻中亚最具破坏性的跨境家禽疾病之一的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 对具有人畜共患潜力的家禽构成跨境威胁,而哈萨克斯坦沿主要迁徙路线的位置造成了病毒传入家禽的持续风险。尽管之前对 野生鸟类 的高致病性禽流感风险进行了评估,但哈萨克斯坦家禽的空间风险仍然被低估。
设计与对象
该研究利用多标准决策分析 (TOPSIS) 综合了与 野生鸟类 栖息地、环境中病毒存活和家禽养殖场普查相关的五个定量风险指标,绘制了哈萨克斯坦 174 个行政区家禽高致病性禽流感的区级风险地图。
核心方法
采用多标准决策分析 (TOPSIS) 将五个定量风险指标(野生鸟类 栖息地、环境中的病毒存活率和家禽养殖场普查)整合为区级家禽高致病性禽流感风险指数。
主要结果
区级风险指数与2005-2025年暴发历史数据高度吻合,ROC曲线下面积为0.899(95% CI:0.846-0.952)。大多数地区(56%)的风险可以忽略不计,而中风险和高风险的集群出现在北部、中部和南部地区以及里海附近。
解释与创新
作者将风险图解释为一种可操作的、空间明确的工具,用于针对高风险地区的 监测 和生物安全措施,代表了哈萨克斯坦家禽业 HPAI 风险评估的一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为高风险地区有针对性的 监测 和生物安全干预措施提供信息,从而有可能减轻高致病性禽流感对中亚家禽种群的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖历史 暴发 数据进行验证,并未解决实时 监测 或干预有效性问题,限制了其即时操作适用性。由于依赖建模指标和回顾性验证,证据强度中等。

District-Level Risk Mapping of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza in Poultry in Kazakhstan Using a Multi-Criteria Decision Model

Authors: Aizada A. Mukhanbetkaliyeva, Irene Iglesias Martín, Fedor I. Кorennoy, Alimzhan S. Kadyrov, Yersyn Y. Mukhanbetkaliyev, Saidulla I. Ruzmatov, Asem Zh. Abenova, Temirlan G. Bakishev, Andres M. Perez, Sarsenbay K. Abdrakhmanov
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) is a transboundary disease of birds with zoonotic potential. Since 2021, H5 HPAI virus variants have spread globally, with migratory waterfowl playing a key role in transcontinental dissemination. Kazakhstan lies at the intersection of major flyways, creating a persistent risk of virus introduction into domestic poultry. While HPAI risk in wild birds has been explored in Kazakhstan, the spatial risk for poultry has remained underassessed. Here, we present a risk map for HPAI in poultry across 174 administrative districts of Kazakhstan, using a multi-criteria decision analysis (TOPSIS) that integrates five quantitative risk indicators that relate to wild bird habitat, virus survival in the environment and poultry farm census. The obtained district-level risk index agreed well with historical outbreak data (2005–2025), with an area under the receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve of 0.899 (95% CI: 0.846–0.952). Most districts (56%) were at negligible risk, whereas medium- and high-risk clusters occurred in the north, central, and southern regions, and near the Caspian Sea. These findings provide an operational, spatially explicit tool for targeting surveillance and biosecurity measures to high-risk areas, ultimately helping to mitigate the impact of one of the most devastating transboundary poultry diseases in Central Asia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) poses a transboundary threat to poultry with zoonotic potential, and Kazakhstan’s position along major migratory flyways creates persistent risk of virus introduction into domestic poultry. Despite prior assessments of HPAI risk in wild birds, the spatial risk for poultry in Kazakhstan remained underassessed.
Design and population
The study produced a district-level risk map for HPAI in poultry across 174 administrative districts of Kazakhstan using a multi-criteria decision analysis (TOPSIS) integrating five quantitative risk indicators related to wild bird habitat, virus survival in the environment, and poultry farm census.
Core methods
A multi-criteria decision analysis (TOPSIS) was used to integrate five quantitative risk indicators—wild bird habitat, virus survival in the environment, and poultry farm census—into a district-level risk index for HPAI in poultry.
Main results
The district-level risk index showed strong agreement with historical outbreak data from 2005–2025, with an area under the ROC curve of 0.899 (95% CI: 0.846–0.952). Most districts (56%) were at negligible risk, while medium- and high-risk clusters occurred in the north, central, and southern regions, and near the Caspian Sea.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the risk map as an operational, spatially explicit tool for targeting surveillance and biosecurity measures to high-risk areas, representing a novel approach to HPAI risk assessment in Kazakhstan’s poultry sector.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform targeted surveillance and biosecurity interventions in high-risk districts, potentially mitigating the impact of HPAI in Central Asia’s poultry populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on historical outbreak data for validation and does not address real-time surveillance or intervention effectiveness, limiting its immediate operational applicability. The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on modeled indicators and retrospective validation.

冲突阻碍了对动物运动的研究。

Conflicts hinder research into animal movements.
作者： Víctor Martín-Vélez, Joan Navarro, Isabel Afán, Tomas Montalvo, Andy J Green
摘要中文翻译卫星追踪彻底改变了我们对动物迁徙的理解,但其可靠性越来越依赖于野生动物经常出没地区的地缘政治稳定性。在这里,我们表明,在东欧持续冲突期间,军事对全球导航卫星系统(GNSS)的干扰严重影响了基于全球定位系统(GPS)的黑头鸥(Chroicocephalus rodibundus)跟踪数据的准确性。2024-2025 年,GPS 轨迹显示出不稳定、低质量且在地理上不可信的位置,与已知的电子战区域重合。这些不准确之处阻碍了定位繁殖群、确定关键中途停留栖息地以及评估候鸟带来的疾病 传播 风险的工作,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。我们的研究结果说明了现代冲突如何将其影响扩展到生态研究基础设施,呼吁采取系统的纠正方法和国际协调,以在地缘政治不稳定的世界中保障运动生态学研究和同一个健康模型的稳健性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
卫星追踪彻底改变了我们对动物迁徙的理解,但其可靠性越来越依赖于野生动物经常出没地区的地缘政治稳定性。
设计与对象
在这里,我们表明,在东欧持续冲突期间,军事对全球导航卫星系统(GNSS)的干扰严重影响了基于全球定位系统(GPS)的黑头鸥(Chroicocephalus rodibundus)跟踪数据的准确性。
核心方法
2024-2025 年,GPS 轨迹显示出不稳定、低质量且在地理上不可信的位置,与已知的电子战区域重合。
主要结果
这些不准确之处阻碍了定位繁殖群、确定关键中途停留栖息地以及评估候鸟带来的疾病 传播 风险的工作,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果说明了现代冲突如何将其影响扩展到生态研究基础设施,呼吁采取系统的纠正方法和国际协调,以在地缘政治不稳定的世界中保障运动生态学研究和同一个健康模型的稳健性。
科研与公卫意义
受损的跟踪数据影响了关键的生态和公共卫生研究,包括对候鸟疾病 传播 风险的评估,特别是 HPAI H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于特定冲突地区的 GPS 轨迹分析,限制了对其他地区或时间段的普遍适用性;然而,对干扰的直接观察为报告的影响提供了有力的证据。

Conflicts hinder research into animal movements.

Authors: Víctor Martín-Vélez, Joan Navarro, Isabel Afán, Tomas Montalvo, Andy J Green
Original abstract (English)
Satellite tracking has revolutionized our understanding of animal migration, yet its reliability increasingly depends on the geopolitical stability of the regions frequented by wildlife. Here, we show that military-induced interference with global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) during ongoing conflicts in Eastern Europe has severely compromised the accuracy of global positioning systems (GPS)-based tracking data for black-headed gulls (Chroicocephalus ridibundus). In 2024-2025, GPS trajectories revealed erratic, low-quality, and geographically implausible positions coinciding with known zones of electronic warfare. These inaccuracies hinder efforts to locate breeding colonies, identify key stopover habitats, and assess disease transmission risks posed by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1. Our findings illustrate how modern conflicts now extend their impact into ecological research infrastructures, calling for systematic correction methods and international coordination to safeguard the robustness of movement ecology studies and One Health models in a geopolitically unstable world.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Satellite tracking has revolutionized our understanding of animal migration, yet its reliability increasingly depends on the geopolitical stability of the regions frequented by wildlife.
Design and population
Here, we show that military-induced interference with global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) during ongoing conflicts in Eastern Europe has severely compromised the accuracy of global positioning systems (GPS)-based tracking data for black-headed gulls (Chroicocephalus ridibundus).
Core methods
In 2024-2025, GPS trajectories revealed erratic, low-quality, and geographically implausible positions coinciding with known zones of electronic warfare.
Main results
These inaccuracies hinder efforts to locate breeding colonies, identify key stopover habitats, and assess disease transmission risks posed by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings illustrate how modern conflicts now extend their impact into ecological research infrastructures, calling for systematic correction methods and international coordination to safeguard the robustness of movement ecology studies and One Health models in a geopolitically unstable world.
Scientific and public-health significance
The compromised tracking data affects critical ecological and public health research, including the assessment of disease transmission risks by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses like HPAI H5N1.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on GPS trajectory analysis during specific conflict zones, limiting generalizability to other regions or time periods; however, the direct observation of interference provides strong evidence for the reported impact.

无帽自扩增 mRNA 疫苗 在小鼠中诱导针对 HPAI 分支 2.3.4.4 H5 病毒的剂量节省保护性免疫。

Capless self-amplifying mRNA vaccine induces dose-sparing protective immunity against HPAI clade 2.3.4.4 H5 virus in mice.
作者： Beom Kyu Kim, Ji-Hyun Park, Won-Suk Choi, Sol Oh, Se Hee An, Gi Chan Lee, Santosh Chokkakula, Chang-Ung Kim, Ju Hwan Jeong, Seong Cheol Min
摘要中文翻译自我扩增mRNA(samRNA)疫苗可以比常规非复制mRNA 疫苗更低的剂量诱导强效免疫应答;然而,大 RNA 尺寸和与 5' 封端相关的制造考虑因素仍然是重要的挑战。在这里,我们开发了一种源自柯萨奇病毒 B5 复制子的无帽 samRNA (CLsamRNA) 疫苗 平台,该平台使用 IRES 介导的帽独立翻译。关键遗传元件的系统优化增强了 CLsamRNA 主链的抗原表达。使用报告 RNA,LNP 配制的 CLsamRNA 显示出快速的早期表达和 RNA 扩增,其表达动力学与基于 VEEV 的 saRNA 和核苷修饰的 mRNA 比较剂不同。当编码高致病性禽流感进化枝2.3.4.4 H5病毒的血凝素抗原时,CLsamRNA在LNP配制后诱导有效的免疫应答。在小鼠模型中,CLsamRNA 诱导有效的中和抗体反应、针对 clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒的交叉反应活性、Th1 偏向体液免疫和强抗原特异性细胞 免疫应答。CLsamRNA 还引发了与抗原呈递、共刺激和细胞免疫启动相关的平台特异性早期炎症和淋巴结免疫基因特征。值得注意的是,最低 0.01 μg 剂量的 CLsamRNA 可以为 BALB/c 小鼠提供针对致命 H5N8 流感病毒攻击的完全保护。这些发现支持 CLsamRNA 作为一种独特的帽独立 RNA 疫苗 平台,具有针对 HPAI H5 病毒的剂量节省保护功效。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决自扩增 mRNA (samRNA) 疫苗 的挑战,特别是需要较低剂量以及与 RNA 大小和与 5' 加帽相关的制造注意事项相关的问题。该研究试图开发一种新颖的 疫苗 平台来克服这些限制。
设计与对象
该研究在小鼠模型中进行,特别是 BALB/c 小鼠,以评估无帽 samRNA (CLsamRNA) 疫苗 平台对抗 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 进化枝 2.3.4.4 H5 病毒的功效。疫苗 在不同剂量下进行了测试,包括最小 0.01 μg 剂量。
核心方法
CLsamRNA 疫苗 平台是使用具有 IRES 介导的帽独立翻译的柯萨奇病毒 B5 复制子开发的。系统地优化关键遗传元件以增强抗原表达。疫苗 采用脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 配制,并在小鼠中进行了 免疫应答 测试。
主要结果
LNP 配制的 CLsamRNA 显示出快速的早期表达和 RNA 扩增,与基于 VEEV 的 saRNA 和核苷修饰的 mRNA 相比,具有不同的动力学。最低 0.01 μg 剂量的 CLsamRNA 可为 BALB/c 小鼠提供针对致命 H5N8 流感病毒攻击的完全保护。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CLsamRNA 是一种独特的帽独立 RNA 疫苗 平台,可以诱导有效的 免疫应答 并提供针对 HPAI H5 病毒的剂量节省保护功效。该平台可能在制造和免疫诱导方面具有优势。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于开发针对 禽流感(特别是 HPAI H5 病毒)的更有效和更具成本效益的 疫苗 具有影响。这可能有助于 监测、人畜共患流感 暴发 的预防和控制策略。
局限与证据强度
这项研究是在小鼠身上进行的,虽然结果很有希望,但它们可能无法直接转化为人类应用。在研究设计的背景下,证据是强有力的,但需要进一步的研究来在更大的动物模型和人类中验证这些发现。

Capless self-amplifying mRNA vaccine induces dose-sparing protective immunity against HPAI clade 2.3.4.4 H5 virus in mice.

Authors: Beom Kyu Kim, Ji-Hyun Park, Won-Suk Choi, Sol Oh, Se Hee An, Gi Chan Lee, Santosh Chokkakula, Chang-Ung Kim, Ju Hwan Jeong, Seong Cheol Min
Original abstract (English)
Self-amplifying mRNA (samRNA) vaccines can induce potent immune responses at lower doses than conventional non-replicating mRNA vaccines; however, large RNA size and manufacturing considerations associated with 5' capping remain important challenges. Here, we developed a capless samRNA (CLsamRNA) vaccine platform derived from a Coxsackievirus B5 replicon that uses IRES-mediated cap-independent translation. Systematic optimization of key genetic elements enhanced antigen expression from the CLsamRNA backbone. Using reporter RNAs, LNP-formulated CLsamRNA showed rapid early expression and RNA amplification, with expression kinetics distinct from VEEV-based saRNA and nucleoside-modified mRNA comparators. When encoding the hemagglutinin antigen of highly pathogenic avian influenza clade 2.3.4.4 H5 viruses, CLsamRNA induced potent immune responses after LNP formulation. In mouse models, CLsamRNA induced potent neutralizing antibody responses, cross-reactive activity against clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 virus, Th1-skewed humoral immunity, and strong antigen-specific cellular immune responses. CLsamRNA also elicited platform-specific early inflammatory and lymph-node immune gene signatures associated with antigen presentation, costimulation, and cellular immune priming. Notably, a minimal 0.01 μg dose of CLsamRNA conferred complete protection against lethal H5N8 influenza virus challenge in BALB/c mice. These findings support CLsamRNA as a distinct cap-independent RNA vaccine platform with dose-sparing protective efficacy against HPAI H5 viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges of self-amplifying mRNA (samRNA) vaccines, particularly the need for lower doses and the issues related to RNA size and manufacturing considerations associated with 5' capping. The research sought to develop a novel vaccine platform that could overcome these limitations.
Design and population
The study was conducted in mouse models, specifically BALB/c mice, to evaluate the efficacy of the capless samRNA (CLsamRNA) vaccine platform against highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) clade 2.3.4.4 H5 viruses. The vaccine was tested at various doses, including a minimal 0.01 μg dose.
Core methods
The CLsamRNA vaccine platform was developed using a Coxsackievirus B5 replicon with IRES-mediated cap-independent translation. Key genetic elements were systematically optimized to enhance antigen expression. The vaccine was formulated with lipid nanoparticles (LNP) and tested for immune responses in mice.
Main results
The LNP-formulated CLsamRNA showed rapid early expression and RNA amplification, with distinct kinetics compared to VEEV-based saRNA and nucleoside-modified mRNA. A minimal 0.01 μg dose of CLsamRNA conferred complete protection against lethal H5N8 influenza virus challenge in BALB/c mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CLsamRNA is a distinct cap-independent RNA vaccine platform that can induce potent immune responses and provide dose-sparing protective efficacy against HPAI H5 viruses. This platform may offer advantages in terms of manufacturing and immune induction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have implications for the development of more efficient and cost-effective vaccines against avian influenza, particularly HPAI H5 viruses. This could aid in surveillance, prevention, and control strategies for zoonotic influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in mice, and while the results are promising, they may not directly translate to human applications. The evidence is strong within the context of the study design, but further research is needed to validate these findings in larger animal models and humans.

酸、pH 值和孵育时间对 禽流感 病毒在原奶中持久性的影响

The impact of acids, pH, and incubation time on avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk
作者： Harrell TL, Shwani A, Suarez DL
摘要中文翻译2024 年 3 月,高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 进化枝 2.3.4.4。b 在奶牛中检测到 H5N1。自检测以来,该病毒已在 17 个州传播,感染了 1000 多个奶牛群,引起了乳制品行业对其对奶牛的影响、与牛奶质量和生产相关的风险以及最终对人类的风险的担忧。研究表明,巴氏灭菌足以灭活牛奶中存在的 HPAIV,使其可供人类安全食用。然而,经常食用并用于制作奶酪、酸奶和开菲尔的未经巴氏消毒的生奶是一个公共健康问题。我们用乙酸、丙酸、乳酸或柠檬酸酸化原料奶,这一过程类似于生产发酵乳产品的初始阶段,将 pH 值调至 5;或用乙酸将其酸化至 pH 6、5 或 4。随后将每个样品掺入低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV),并在室温下孵育长达 24 小时,然后接种到 10 日龄的无特定病原体的胚胎鸡蛋中。每天评估胚胎的活力,并使用尿囊液的血凝测定来确认存活的 LPAIV 的存在。活病毒的减少与时间和 pH 值显着相关,但与测试的特定酸类型无关。pH 4 和 5 的酸 治疗 随着时间的推移逐渐降低活 LPAIV 水平,在 24 小时观察到最明显的失活。pH 值为 6 的样品病毒活力几乎没有降低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 在 17 个州的奶牛中传播,引发了人们对未经高温消毒的原料奶的牛奶质量、生产和人类健康风险的担忧,因此需要进行这项研究。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用各种酸将生奶酸化至不同的 pH 值,添加低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV),并在室温下孵育长达 24 小时,然后接种到 10 日龄的无特定病原体的胚胎鸡蛋中。
核心方法
将样品用乙酸、丙酸、乳酸或柠檬酸酸化至 pH 5,或用乙酸酸化至 pH 6、5 或 4,加入 LPAIV,孵育,然后接种到含胚鸡蛋中,每天通过血凝测定评估活力。
主要结果
活病毒的减少与时间和 pH 值显着相关,但与测试的酸的具体类型无关;pH 6 的样品显示病毒活力几乎没有降低,而 pH 4 和 5 的酸 治疗 随着时间的推移逐渐降低活的 LPAIV 水平。
解释与创新
作者解释说,酸 治疗,特别是在较低 pH 水平下,可以有效灭活原料奶中的 LPAIV,为降低与未经巴氏消毒的乳制品相关的公共卫生风险提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为减少原奶中 禽流感 病毒持久性的潜在干预措施提供了信息,从而减轻发酵食品中使用的未经高温消毒的乳制品对人类健康的风险。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在受控实验室环境中使用胚胎鸡蛋进行的,这可能无法完全复制乳制品加工或人类消费的现实条件。

The impact of acids, pH, and incubation time on avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk

Authors: Harrell TL, Shwani A, Suarez DL
Original abstract (English)
In March 2024, highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4. b H5N1 was detected in dairy cattle. Since detection, the virus has spread across 17 states, infecting more than 1000 dairy herds, causing concern for the dairy industry regarding the effects on dairy cattle, the risk associated with milk quality and production, and ultimately its risk to humans. It has been shown that pasteurization is sufficient to inactivate HPAIV, if present in milk, making it safe for human consumption. However, unpasteurized raw milk that is routinely consumed and used to make cheese, yogurt, and kefir is a public health concern. We acidified raw milk, a process that is analogous to the preliminary phase of generating fermented milk products, with acetic, propionic, lactic, or citric acid to pH 5; or acidified it with acetic acid to pH 6, 5, or 4. Each sample was subsequently spiked with low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) and incubated at room temperature for up to 24 h before being inoculated into 10-day old specific-pathogen-free embryonating chicken eggs. Embryos were assessed daily for viability and hemagglutination assays on the allantoic fluid was used to confirm the presence of viable LPAIV. The reduction of viable virus was significantly correlated with both time and pH but not specific type of acid tested. An acid treatment at pH 4 and 5 progressively reduced viable LPAIV levels over time, with the most pronounced inactivation observed at 24 h. Samples at pH 6 had little reduction in virus viability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed due to the spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 in dairy cattle across 17 states, raising concerns about milk quality, production, and human health risks from unpasteurized raw milk.
Design and population
The study involved acidifying raw milk with various acids to different pH levels, spiking it with low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV), and incubating it at room temperature for up to 24 hours before inoculating into 10-day-old specific-pathogen-free embryonating chicken eggs.
Core methods
Samples were acidified with acetic, propionic, lactic, or citric acid to pH 5 or with acetic acid to pH 6, 5, or 4, spiked with LPAIV, incubated, and then inoculated into embryonating chicken eggs, with viability assessed daily via hemagglutination assays.
Main results
The reduction of viable virus was significantly correlated with both time and pH but not the specific type of acid tested; samples at pH 6 showed little reduction in virus viability, while acid treatment at pH 4 and 5 progressively reduced viable LPAIV levels over time.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that acid treatment, particularly at lower pH levels, can effectively inactivate LPAIV in raw milk, providing a novel approach to reduce public health risks associated with unpasteurized dairy products.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform potential interventions for reducing avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk, thereby mitigating risks to human health from unpasteurized dairy products used in fermented foods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in a controlled laboratory setting using embryonating chicken eggs, which may not fully replicate real-world conditions in dairy processing or human consumption.

NanoSTING 的治疗性鼻内给药可针对季节性和高致病性流感病毒株提供广泛的保护 2258688

Therapeutic intranasal delivery of NanoSTING provides broad protection against seasonal and highly pathogenic influenza strains 2258688
作者： Kate Reichel, Ankita Leekha, Brett Hurst, Navin Varadarajan
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 季节性和高致病性流感病毒株给公共卫生带来了重大挑战。保护弱势群体、加速康复并减少 死亡率 的广谱疗法是流感长期追求的目标。我们开发了 NanoSTING,这是一种 2’3’-cGAMP(内源性 STING 激活剂)的脂质体制剂,可激活粘膜先天免疫,作为 宿主 定向疗法。方法 通过 DLS、zeta 电位和 HPLC 评估 NanoSTING 稳定性。单次鼻内剂量 NanoSTING 后,对 BALB/c 小鼠的药代动力学 (PK)、药效学 (PD) 和安全性进行了评估。通过 qPCR 分析鼻/肺组织的 IFN 基因(Ifnb1 和 Cxcl10),并通过 H&E 分析安全性。为了模拟脆弱人群,我们测试了老年(18-20 个月)、环磷酰胺淋巴细胞去除和地塞米松治疗(免疫抑制)小鼠的 NanoSTING 诱导的粘膜 IFN 反应、安全性和病毒攻击中的保护作用。针对流感(A/H1N1pdm、B/布里斯班和 H5N1)攻击模型测试了治疗效果。结果 NanoSTING 在 4oC 下稳定 12 个月,封装的 cGAMP 没有损失。单次鼻内剂量在鼻和肺组织中诱导出强大的 Ifnb1 和 Cxcl10,而没有组织学损伤。在甲型/H1N1 和乙型流感攻击模型中,一剂 NanoSTING 减少了体重减轻,加速了恢复,并且优于十剂奥司他韦。在 高致病性禽流感(HPAI、H5N1)模型中,即使在感染后 48-72 小时内施用,NanoSTING 也能降低病毒滴度和疾病严重程度。NanoSTING 诱导的鼻先天免疫在淋巴细胞去除、地塞米松治疗和老年小鼠中得以保留,支持其在脆弱的 宿主 中的使用。结论 单剂量鼻内 NanoSTING 激活保护性 I 型 IFN 程序,并为 A/B 型流感(包括 H5N1)提供广泛的治疗益处,具有延长的 治疗 窗口、冷藏稳定性和节省剂量的给药,突出了细胞、宿主 导向的持久呼吸道 抗病毒药物 防御的途径。资金来源 NIH、AuraVax 主题类别 粘膜和区域免疫学 (MUC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性和高致病性流感毒株构成了重大的公共卫生挑战,保护弱势群体、加速康复和减少 死亡率 的广谱疗法是流感长期追求的目标。
设计与对象
该研究在 BALB/c 小鼠中进行,包括老年(18-20 个月)、环磷酰胺淋巴细胞去除和地塞米松治疗(免疫抑制)小鼠,以模拟易感人群,并测试了针对流感(A/H1N1pdm、B/布里斯班和 H5N1)攻击模型的治疗效果。
核心方法
通过 DLS、zeta 电位和 HPLC 评估 NanoSTING 稳定性;单次鼻内给药后评估药代动力学(PK)、药效学(PD)和安全性;通过 qPCR 分析鼻/肺组织中的 IFN 基因(Ifnb1 和 Cxcl10),并通过 H&E 分析安全性。
主要结果
NanoSTING 在 4°C 下稳定 12 个月,封装的 cGAMP 没有损失;单次鼻内剂量在鼻和肺组织中诱导出强大的 Ifnb1 和 Cxcl10,而没有组织学损伤;在甲型/H1N1 和乙型流感攻击模型中,一剂 NanoSTING 减少了体重减轻,加速了恢复,并且优于十剂奥司他韦。
解释与创新
作者解释说,单剂量鼻内 NanoSTING 可激活保护性 I 型 IFN 程序,并为 A/B 型流感(包括 H5N1)提供广泛的治疗益处,具有延长的 治疗 窗口、冷藏稳定性和节省剂量的给药,突出了细胞、宿主 导向的持久呼吸道 抗病毒药物 防御的途径。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发一种新型 宿主 定向疗法提供了信息,该疗法可以针对季节性和高致病性流感病毒株提供广泛的保护,特别是在弱势群体中,对 监测、治疗 和预防策略具有潜在影响。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在小鼠模型中进行的,可能无法完全转化为人类;局限性包括缺乏人体数据以及需要进一步评估不同人群的长期安全性和有效性。

Therapeutic intranasal delivery of NanoSTING provides broad protection against seasonal and highly pathogenic influenza strains 2258688

Authors: Kate Reichel, Ankita Leekha, Brett Hurst, Navin Varadarajan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Seasonal and highly pathogenic influenza strains pose major public health challenges. Broad-spectrum therapies that protect vulnerable populations, accelerate recovery, and reduce mortality are a long-sought goal for influenza. We developed NanoSTING, a liposomal formulation of 2’3’-cGAMP, the endogenous STING-activator, to activate mucosal innate immunity as a host-directed therapy. Methods NanoSTING stability was assessed by DLS, zeta potential, and HPLC. Pharmacokinetics (PK), pharmacodynamics (PD), and safety were assessed in BALB/c mice after a single intranasal dose of NanoSTING. The nasal/lung tissues were analyzed by qPCR for IFN genes (Ifnb1 and Cxcl10) and by H&E for safety. To model vulnerable populations, we tested aged (18—20 mo), cyclophosphamide-lymphodepleted, and dexamethasone-treated (immune-suppressed) mice for NanoSTING-induced mucosal IFN responses, safety, and protection in viral challenge. Therapeutic efficacy was tested against influenza (A/H1N1pdm, B/Brisbane, and H5N1) challenge models. Results NanoSTING was stable for 12 months at 4oC with no loss of encapsulated cGAMP. A single intranasal dose induced robust Ifnb1 and Cxcl10 in nasal and lung tissues without histologic injury. In influenza A/H1N1 and B challenge models, one dose of NanoSTING reduced weight loss, accelerated recovery, and outperformed ten doses of oseltamivir. In models with highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI, H5N1), NanoSTING reduced viral titers and disease severity even when administered 48—72 h post-infection. NanoSTING-induced nasal innate immunity was preserved in lymphodepleted, dexamethasone-treated, and aged mice, supporting its use in vulnerable hosts. Conclusion Single-dose intranasal NanoSTING activates protective type I IFN programs and delivers broad therapeutic benefit across influenza A/B, including H5N1, with an extended treatment window, refrigeration stability, and dose-sparing administration—highlighting a cellular, host-directed path to durable respiratory antiviral defense. Funding Source NIH, AuraVax Topic Categories Mucosal and Regional Immunology (MUC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal and highly pathogenic influenza strains pose major public health challenges, and broad-spectrum therapies that protect vulnerable populations, accelerate recovery, and reduce mortality are a long-sought goal for influenza.
Design and population
The study was conducted in BALB/c mice, including aged (18—20 mo), cyclophosphamide-lymphodepleted, and dexamethasone-treated (immune-suppressed) mice to model vulnerable populations, with therapeutic efficacy tested against influenza (A/H1N1pdm, B/Brisbane, and H5N1) challenge models.
Core methods
NanoSTING stability was assessed by DLS, zeta potential, and HPLC; pharmacokinetics (PK), pharmacodynamics (PD), and safety were evaluated after a single intranasal dose; nasal/lung tissues were analyzed by qPCR for IFN genes (Ifnb1 and Cxcl10) and by H&E for safety.
Main results
NanoSTING was stable for 12 months at 4°C with no loss of encapsulated cGAMP; a single intranasal dose induced robust Ifnb1 and Cxcl10 in nasal and lung tissues without histologic injury; in influenza A/H1N1 and B challenge models, one dose of NanoSTING reduced weight loss, accelerated recovery, and outperformed ten doses of oseltamivir.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that single-dose intranasal NanoSTING activates protective type I IFN programs and delivers broad therapeutic benefit across influenza A/B, including H5N1, with an extended treatment window, refrigeration stability, and dose-sparing administration, highlighting a cellular, host-directed path to durable respiratory antiviral defense.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of a novel host-directed therapy that could provide broad protection against seasonal and highly pathogenic influenza strains, particularly in vulnerable populations, with potential implications for surveillance, treatment, and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in mouse models and may not fully translate to humans; limitations include the absence of human data and the need for further evaluation of long-term safety and efficacy in diverse populations.

来自韩国和蒙古 野生鸟类 以及家鸭的 H6N1 禽流感 病毒的基因组特征(2022-2024 年)

Genomic characterization of H6N1 avian influenza viruses from wild birds and domestic ducks in the republic of Korea and Mongolia (2022–2024)
作者： Kwang-Nyeong Lee, Se-Hee An, Gyeong-Beom Heo, Youn-Jeong Lee, Yong-Myung Kang, Kyuyoung Lee
摘要中文翻译在本研究中,我们表征了从蒙古10个关键繁殖地(2022年4月至2024年10月)的9,206份野生鸟类粪便样本中分离的七株H6N1病毒的HA(血凝素)和NA(神经氨酸酶)基因片段,并使用贝叶斯系统发育分析评估了它们与2023年9月至2024年4月间从韩国(ROK)的野生鸟类(n = 7,419)和家鸭(n = 79,386)粪便样本中分离的七株H6N1菌株之间的遗传关系。数据可用性 在当前研究期间未生成或分析任何数据集。J Clin Microbiol 46(5):1769–1773 - Kim G-S et al (2019) 使用不同方法检测禽流感病毒在野生鸟类 监测中的检测率差异。Methods Mol Biol 2123:69–79 - Shu Y,McCauley J (2017) GISAID:全球共享所有流感数据倡议——从愿景到现实。Nat Methods 22(8):1653–1656 - Yu G et al (2017) ggtree:一个用于可视化和注释系统发育树及其协变量和其他相关数据的R包。Methods Ecol Evol 8(1):28–36 - Billera LJ,Holmes SP,Vogtmann K (2001) 系统发育树空间的几何结构。摘要 禽流感病毒(AIVs)对全球家禽生产和动物健康构成持续威胁,主要在野生鸟类 储存宿主中维持,并沿着迁徙飞行路线传播。其中,低致病性H6N1亚型在东亚广泛传播,是基因组监测的关键研究对象。所有分析分离株均属于东亚谱系,主要在2016-2017年左右分化为两个区域亚分支(最近共同祖先时间[tMRCA]:HA,2017年12月;NA,2016年11月)。HA和NA系统发育均呈现出梯状拓扑结构,分布在不同的地理区域...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 对全球家禽生产和动物健康构成持续威胁,主要存在于 野生鸟类 储存宿主 内并沿迁徙路线传播。其中,低致病性H6N1亚型在东亚地区广泛流行,是基因组监测的重点研究对象。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们对从蒙古 10 个主要繁殖地(2022 年 4 月至 10 月)的 9,206 个 野生鸟类 粪便样本中分离的 7 种 H6N1 病毒的血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 基因片段进行了特征分析,并评估了它们与从 野生鸟类 粪便样本中分离的 7 种 H6N1 病毒株的遗传关系 (n = 2023 年 9 月至 2024 年 4 月期间,使用贝叶斯系统发育分析对韩国 (ROK) 的家鸭 (n = 7,419) 和家鸭 (n = 79,386) 进行了研究。
核心方法
该研究采用贝叶斯系统发育分析来评估蒙古和韩国的 H6N1 分离株之间的遗传关系,方法包括使用下一代测序和比对软件 MAFFT 对甲型流感病毒进行完整的 基因组 测序。
主要结果
所有分析的分离株均属于东北亚谱系,主要在 2016 年至 2017 年左右分为两个区域分支(tMRCA:HA,2017 年 12 月;NA,2016 年 11 月)。尽管蒙古和韩国之间地理距离遥远,但这些 H6N1 分离株在东北亚谱系内表现出密切的遗传连通性(贝叶斯因子 > 100)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明局部变异的产生和更新,HA 受体结合和 NA 酶活性之间的功能平衡驱动了强烈的共同适应。新颖之处在于证明了东北亚谱系内地理上相距遥远的 H6N1 分离株之间存在密切的遗传连通性。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于表面糖蛋白的持续进化动态,有必要对野生水禽和家禽种群进行持续的跨境监测,以追踪区域性 H6N1 变异。该 监测 对于早期检测潜在危险的重配或适应至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括仅关注 HA 和 NA 基因片段,没有分析内部基因或功能测定。证据强度中等,因为系统发育分析提供了稳健的遗传关系,但缺乏表型特征的实验验证。

Genomic characterization of H6N1 avian influenza viruses from wild birds and domestic ducks in the republic of Korea and Mongolia (2022–2024)

Authors: Kwang-Nyeong Lee, Se-Hee An, Gyeong-Beom Heo, Youn-Jeong Lee, Yong-Myung Kang, Kyuyoung Lee
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we characterized the hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) gene segments of seven H6N1 viruses isolated from 9,206 wild bird fecal samples across 10 key breeding habitats in Mongolia (April–October, 2022–2024) and evaluated their genetic relationships with seven H6N1 strains isolated from fecal samples of wild birds (n = 7,419) and domestic ducks (n = 79,386) in the Republic of Korea (ROK) between September 2023 and April 2024 using Bayesian phylogenetic analyses. Data availability No datasets were generated or analysed during the current study. Abstract Avian influenza viruses (AIVs) pose a continuous threat to global poultry production and animal health, maintained largely within wild bird reservoirs and dispersed along migratory flyways. Among these, the low-pathogenic H6N1 subtype circulates widely across East Asia and is a key subject of genomic surveillance. All analyzed isolates belonged to the Northeast Asian lineage, primarily diverging into two regional subclades around 2016–2017 (time to the most recent common ancestor [tMRCA]: HA, Dec 2017; NA, Nov 2016). Both HA and NA phylogenies exhibited a ladder-like topology structured across distinct geographical regi...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza viruses (AIVs) pose a continuous threat to global poultry production and animal health, maintained largely within wild bird reservoirs and dispersed along migratory flyways. Among these, the low-pathogenic H6N1 subtype circulates widely across East Asia and is a key subject of genomic surveillance.
Design and population
In this study, we characterized the hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) gene segments of seven H6N1 viruses isolated from 9,206 wild bird fecal samples across 10 key breeding habitats in Mongolia (April–October, 2022–2024) and evaluated their genetic relationships with seven H6N1 strains isolated from fecal samples of wild birds (n = 7,419) and domestic ducks (n = 79,386) in the Republic of Korea (ROK) between September 2023 and April 2024 using Bayesian phylogenetic analyses.
Core methods
The study employed Bayesian phylogenetic analyses to evaluate genetic relationships between H6N1 isolates from Mongolia and the ROK, with methods including complete genome sequencing of influenza A viruses using next-generation sequencing and alignment software MAFFT.
Main results
All analyzed isolates belonged to the Northeast Asian lineage, primarily diverging into two regional subclades around 2016–2017 (tMRCA: HA, Dec 2017; NA, Nov 2016). Despite the substantial geographical distance between Mongolia and the ROK, these H6N1 isolates demonstrated close genetic connectivity within the Northeast Asian lineage (Bayes factor > 100).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating localized variant generation and turnover, with strong co-adaptation driven by functional balance between HA receptor binding and NA enzymatic activity. The novelty lies in the demonstration of close genetic connectivity between geographically distant H6N1 isolates within the Northeast Asian lineage.
Scientific and public-health significance
Given the continuous evolutionary dynamics of surface glycoproteins, sustained transboundary monitoring across wild waterfowl and poultry populations is warranted to track regional H6N1 variants. This surveillance is critical for early detection of potentially dangerous reassortants or adaptations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the focus only on HA and NA gene segments, with no analysis of internal genes or functional assays. The evidence strength is moderate, as the phylogenetic analyses provide robust genetic relationships but lack experimental validation of phenotypic characteristics.

亚型特异性中和抗体在流感病毒共感染期间促进抗原转变。

Subtype-specific neutralizing antibodies promote antigenic shift during influenza virus co-infection.
作者： Mengnan Liu, Xiaoran Gao, Xueyang Li, Jinyang Hao, Christine Light, Yu Tian, Siqin Wang, Wenyu Yan, Rong Hai, Wei Hu
摘要中文翻译当不同流感毒株共同感染相同的 宿主 细胞时,就会发生不同流感毒株之间的重配。重配病毒的出现取决于其内在适应性和外在因素,包括预先存在的体液免疫。鸟类中 H2N2 和 H3N2 等大流行毒株以及 H5N6、H5N8 和 H7N9 等人畜共患甲型流感病毒的产生被认为是先前存在的抗体广泛选择的结果。为了进一步探讨体液免疫在重配中的作用,我们生成了两种不同的表达荧光蛋白的病毒,并使用毒株特异性和交叉反应性单克隆抗体(mAb)来评估交叉免疫对重配的影响。我们的结果表明,所有单克隆抗体都改变了基因型多样性,并在体外和体内显着减少了共感染细胞中子代病毒粒子的释放。此外,小鼠体内的抗体转移研究揭示了针对不同致病性特征的攻击的保护作用。值得注意的是,菌株特异性 mAb 驱动的选择取决于其中和特异性,而广泛反应性 mAb 驱动的选择则独立于中和特异性。我们的研究结果表明,预先存在的中和抗体会形成重配,并且毒株特异性中和抗体会在共感染期间促进抗原转变,而识别不同亚型流感病毒的广泛交叉反应抗体则并非如此。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨体液免疫在流感病毒重配中的作用,特别是先前存在的抗体如何影响具有大流行或人畜共患潜力的重配病毒的出现。
设计与对象
该研究在体外和体内的共感染细胞中使用了表达荧光蛋白的病毒和单克隆抗体,以评估交叉免疫对重配的影响。
核心方法
产生了表达不同荧光蛋白的病毒,并使用毒株特异性和交叉反应性单克隆抗体来评估它们在共感染期间对基因型多样性和子代病毒粒子释放的影响。
主要结果
所有单克隆抗体都改变了基因型多样性,并在体外和体内显着减少了共感染细胞中子代病毒粒子的释放;小鼠体内的抗体转移提供了针对不同致病性特征的保护。
解释与创新
作者解释说,预先存在的中和抗体形成重配,与识别不同亚型的广泛交叉反应抗体不同,菌株特异性抗体在共感染期间促进抗原转变。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了预先存在的免疫力如何影响流感病毒 进化 以及大流行或人畜共患毒株的出现,为 进化 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于单克隆抗体的体外和体内模型,可能无法完全复制自然感染动态。

Subtype-specific neutralizing antibodies promote antigenic shift during influenza virus co-infection.

Authors: Mengnan Liu, Xiaoran Gao, Xueyang Li, Jinyang Hao, Christine Light, Yu Tian, Siqin Wang, Wenyu Yan, Rong Hai, Wei Hu
Original abstract (English)
Reassortment between different influenza strains occurs when they co-infect the same host cell. The emergence of a reassortant virus depends on both its intrinsic fitness and extrinsic factors, including preexisting humoral immunity. The generation of pandemic strains, such as H2N2 and H3N2, and zoonotic influenza A viruses, such as H5N6, H5N8, and H7N9, in birds is suggested to be the result of extensive selection by preexisting antibodies. To further explore the role of humoral immunity in reassortment, we generated two divergent fluorescent protein-expressing viruses and used strain-specific and cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) to assess the impact of cross-immunity on reassortment. Our results indicate that all mAbs altered the genotypic diversity and significantly reduced the release of progeny virions in co-infected cells both in vitro and in vivo . Moreover, antibody transfer studies in mice revealed protection from challenge with divergent pathogenicity profiles. Notably, selection driven by a strain-specific mAb depended on its neutralizing specificity, whereas the selection driven by broadly reactive mAbs was independent of neutralization specificity. Our findings demonstrate that preexisting neutralizing antibodies shape reassortment and that strain-specific neutralizing antibodies promote antigenic shift during co-infection, which is not the case for broadly cross-reactive antibodies that recognize influenza viruses from different subtypes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the role of humoral immunity in influenza virus reassortment, particularly how preexisting antibodies influence the emergence of reassortant viruses with pandemic or zoonotic potential.
Design and population
The study used fluorescent protein-expressing viruses and monoclonal antibodies in co-infected cells, both in vitro and in vivo, to assess the impact of cross-immunity on reassortment.
Core methods
Divergent fluorescent protein-expressing viruses were generated, and strain-specific and cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies were used to evaluate their effects on genotypic diversity and progeny virion release during co-infection.
Main results
All monoclonal antibodies altered genotypic diversity and significantly reduced progeny virion release in co-infected cells both in vitro and in vivo; antibody transfer in mice provided protection against divergent pathogenicity profiles.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that preexisting neutralizing antibodies shape reassortment, with strain-specific antibodies promoting antigenic shift during co-infection, unlike broadly cross-reactive antibodies that recognize different subtypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance and prevention strategies by highlighting how preexisting immunity can influence influenza virus evolution and the emergence of pandemic or zoonotic strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relied on in vitro and in vivo models with monoclonal antibodies, which may not fully replicate natural infection dynamics.

具有广泛抗 H5N1 神经氨酸酶活性的多供体血浆抗体类 2309455

A Multidonor Plasma Antibody Class with Broad Activity Against H5N1 Neuraminidase 2309455
作者： Gregory Ippolito, Dalton Towers, Sara Mahmoud, Alvaro Padrón, Emily Yemington, Yao Ma, Jeremy Glenn, Michael Moody, Jason Lavinder, Luis Martínez‐Sobrido
摘要中文翻译摘要 引言 高致病性 H5N1 流感病毒仍然是重大的大流行威胁,强调需要定义针对保守病毒表位的抗体反应。在这里,我们表征了一组血浆 IgG 衍生的人神经氨酸酶 (NA) 特异性单克隆抗体 (mAb),它们共享一个会聚的 CDR-H3“DR 基序”,该基序先前与 NA 活性位点的底物拟态有关。方法 通过 IgG 蛋白质组学发现、ELISA、免疫荧光测定、酶抑制和微中和测定,我们证明多种 DR 基序 mAb 能够高效结合 H5N1 NA 并直接抑制 NA 活性。结果 几种抗体与不同的甲型流感毒株表现出交叉反应性,并靶向 NA 催化位点内的重叠表位。重要的是,两种 DR 基序 mAb(41508 和 125308)为 C57BL/6 小鼠提供保护,使其免受 A/Texas/37/2024(H5N1;进化枝 2.3.4.4b,基因型 B3.13)的致命攻击。结论 总之,这些数据定义了具有广泛 抗病毒药物 活性的多供体类循环功能性人 NA 导向抗体,并强调 NA 活性位点是下一代流感 疫苗 和基于抗体的对策的可接近且易受攻击的靶点。资金来源 不适用 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性 H5N1 流感病毒构成持续的大流行威胁,因此需要识别人类抗体针对的保守病毒表位。
设计与对象
该研究表征了一组血浆 IgG 衍生的人神经氨酸酶 (NA) 特异性单克隆抗体 (mAb),它们共享一个会聚的 CDR-H3“DR 基序”,该基序与 NA 活性位点的底物拟态有关。
核心方法
使用 IgG 蛋白质组学发现、ELISA、免疫荧光测定、酶抑制和微中和测定来评估 DR 基序 mAb 的结合、NA 活性抑制和交叉反应性。
主要结果
几种 DR 基序 mAb 能够高效结合 H5N1 NA,直接抑制 NA 活性,并与不同的甲型流感病毒株表现出交叉反应性,靶向 NA 催化位点内的重叠表位。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为定义了一类多供体循环、功能性人 NA 导向抗体,具有广泛的 抗病毒药物 活性,强调 NA 活性位点是下一代流感 疫苗 和基于抗体的对策的易受攻击的目标。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据表明 NA 活性位点靶向在通用流感 疫苗 设计和针对 H5N1 及相关毒株的治疗性抗体开发中的潜力。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外和小鼠模型数据,因此对人类的有效性和安全性存在不确定性;由于缺乏临床验证,证据强度中等。

A Multidonor Plasma Antibody Class with Broad Activity Against H5N1 Neuraminidase 2309455

Authors: Gregory Ippolito, Dalton Towers, Sara Mahmoud, Alvaro Padrón, Emily Yemington, Yao Ma, Jeremy Glenn, Michael Moody, Jason Lavinder, Luis Martínez‐Sobrido
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Highly pathogenic H5N1 influenza viruses remain a significant pandemic threat, underscoring the need to define antibody responses that target conserved viral epitopes. Here, we characterize a panel of plasma IgG—derived human neuraminidase (NA)—specific monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) that share a convergent CDR-H3 “DR motif,” previously implicated in substrate mimicry at the NA active site. Methods Using IgG proteomics discovery, ELISA, immunofluorescence assays, enzymatic inhibition, and microneutralization assays, we show that multiple DR-motif mAbs bind H5N1 NA with high potency and directly inhibit NA activity. Results Several antibodies exhibit cross-reactivity with divergent influenza A strains and target overlapping epitopes within the NA catalytic site. Importantly, two DR-motif mAbs (41508 and 125308) confer protection in C57BL/6 mice against lethal challenge with A/Texas/37/2024 (H5N1; clade 2.3.4.4b, genotype B3.13). Conclusion Together, these data define a multidonor class of circulating, functional human NA-directed antibodies with broad antiviral activity and highlight the NA active site as an accessible and vulnerable target for next-generation influenza vaccines and antibody-based countermeasures. Funding Source n/a Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic H5N1 influenza viruses pose a persistent pandemic threat, creating a need to identify conserved viral epitopes targeted by human antibodies.
Design and population
The study characterized a panel of plasma IgG-derived human neuraminidase (NA)-specific monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) sharing a convergent CDR-H3 “DR motif” implicated in substrate mimicry at the NA active site.
Core methods
IgG proteomics discovery, ELISA, immunofluorescence assays, enzymatic inhibition, and microneutralization assays were used to assess binding, NA activity inhibition, and cross-reactivity of DR-motif mAbs.
Main results
Several DR-motif mAbs bound H5N1 NA with high potency, directly inhibited NA activity, and exhibited cross-reactivity with divergent influenza A strains, targeting overlapping epitopes within the NA catalytic site.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as defining a multidonor class of circulating, functional human NA-directed antibodies with broad antiviral activity, highlighting the NA active site as a vulnerable target for next-generation influenza vaccines and antibody-based countermeasures.
Scientific and public-health significance
These data suggest potential for NA active-site targeting in universal influenza vaccine design and therapeutic antibody development against H5N1 and related strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro and mouse model data, leaving uncertainty about efficacy and safety in humans; the strength of evidence is moderate due to the absence of clinical validation.

用于同时检测 禽流感 A 病毒和新城疫病毒的多重实时 RT-PCR 检测的开发和分析验证

Development and Analytical Validation of a Multiplex Real-Time RT-PCR Assay for Simultaneous Detection of Avian Influenza A Virus and Newcastle Disease Virus
作者： Yerbol Burashev, Saken Khaidarov, Nurdos A. Aubakir, Zamira D. Omarova, Ali B. Tulendibayev, Takhmina U. Argimbayeva, Tangat T. Yermekbay, Khairulla B. Abeuov, Rashida A. Rystaeva, Kulyaisan T. Sultankulova
摘要中文翻译禽流感 A 病毒 (AIV) 和新城疫病毒 (NDV) 是对家禽最具破坏性的病原体之一,AIV 还具有人畜共患病的潜力,因此将其纳入“同一个健康”议程。这两种感染经常产生重叠的临床症状,因此在单一反应中检测这两种感染的方法对于 监测 具有明显的实用价值。我们开发了一种双工一步式实时逆转录 PCR (RT-qPCR),可检测 FAM 通道中的 AIV 和 ROX 通道中的 NDV。对从不同地理区域的 GenBank 检索到的每种病毒的 500 个序列进行比对后,针对 AIV 核蛋白和基质基因以及 NDV 基质、磷蛋白和核蛋白基因的保守区域设计引物和水解探针。退火温度、引物和探针浓度均经过优化,携带目标片段的重组质粒既可作为阳性对照,又可作为定量标准品。该检测对 AIV 和 NDV 具有特异性,与传染性支气管炎病毒无交叉反应,通道间无信号交叉;其分析灵敏度达到每个反应约 10-20 个 RNA 拷贝。参考分离株的定性结果与两种商业兽医试剂盒完全一致(Cohen’s κ = 1.00),并且该测定通过了实验室间委员会测试。这项研究确立了实验室开发的双工检测的设计和核心分析性能;在常规 监测 部署之前,需要在计算机上扩展当代序列的包容性、更广泛的特异性面板、包含抑制剂的基质、内部扩增控制和前瞻性临床验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A 病毒 (AIV) 和新城疫病毒 (NDV) 是对家禽最具破坏性的病原体之一,AIV 还具有人畜共患病的潜力,因此将其纳入“同一个健康”议程。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种双重一步式实时逆转录 PCR (RT-qPCR) 检测方法,用于同时检测 AIV 和 NDV。
核心方法
在对来自 GenBank 的每种病毒 500 个序列进行比对后,针对 AIV 核蛋白和基质基因以及 NDV 基质、磷蛋白和核蛋白基因的保守区域设计引物和水解探针;优化退火温度、引物和探针浓度,并以重组质粒作为阳性对照和定量标准品。
主要结果
该检测对 AIV 和 NDV 具有特异性,与传染性支气管炎病毒无交叉反应,通道间无信号交叉;其分析灵敏度达到每个反应约 10-20 个 RNA 拷贝,参考分离株的定性结果与两种商业兽医试剂盒完全一致(Cohen's κ = 1.00)。
解释与创新
该研究确定了实验室开发的同时检测 AIV 和 NDV 的双重检测的设计和核心分析性能。
科研与公卫意义
由于家禽中 AIV 和 NDV 感染的临床症状重叠,双重检测对于 监测 具有明显的实用价值。
局限与证据强度
在常规 监测 部署之前,需要扩大当代序列的计算机包容性、更广泛的特异性组、包含抑制剂的基质、内部扩增控制和前瞻性临床验证;由于需要进一步验证,证据强度中等。

Development and Analytical Validation of a Multiplex Real-Time RT-PCR Assay for Simultaneous Detection of Avian Influenza A Virus and Newcastle Disease Virus

Authors: Yerbol Burashev, Saken Khaidarov, Nurdos A. Aubakir, Zamira D. Omarova, Ali B. Tulendibayev, Takhmina U. Argimbayeva, Tangat T. Yermekbay, Khairulla B. Abeuov, Rashida A. Rystaeva, Kulyaisan T. Sultankulova
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A virus (AIV) and Newcastle disease virus (NDV) are among the most damaging pathogens of poultry, and AIV also carries zoonotic potential that places it within the One Health agenda. The two infections frequently produce overlapping clinical signs, so a method that detects both in a single reaction has clear practical value for surveillance. We developed a duplex one-step real-time reverse-transcription PCR (RT-qPCR) that detects AIV in the FAM channel and NDV in the ROX channel. Primers and hydrolysis probes were designed against conserved regions of the AIV nucleoprotein and matrix genes and the NDV matrix, phosphoprotein and nucleoprotein genes, after alignment of 500 sequences per virus retrieved from GenBank from different geographic regions. Annealing temperature, primer and probe concentrations were optimised, and recombinant plasmids carrying the target fragments served both as positive controls and as quantitative standards. The assay was specific for AIV and NDV, with no cross-reactivity with infectious bronchitis virus and no signal crossover between channels; its analytical sensitivity reached approximately 10–20 RNA copies per reaction. Qualitative results on reference isolates were fully concordant with two commercial veterinary kits (Cohen’s κ = 1.00), and the assay passed inter-laboratory commission testing. This study establishes the design and core analytical performance of a laboratory-developed duplex assay; expanded in silico inclusivity on contemporary sequences, a broader specificity panel, inhibitor-containing matrices, an internal amplification control, and prospective clinical validation are required before routine surveillance deployment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A virus (AIV) and Newcastle disease virus (NDV) are among the most damaging pathogens of poultry, and AIV also carries zoonotic potential that places it within the One Health agenda.
Design and population
The study developed a duplex one-step real-time reverse-transcription PCR (RT-qPCR) assay for simultaneous detection of AIV and NDV.
Core methods
Primers and hydrolysis probes were designed against conserved regions of the AIV nucleoprotein and matrix genes and the NDV matrix, phosphoprotein and nucleoprotein genes after alignment of 500 sequences per virus from GenBank; annealing temperature, primer and probe concentrations were optimised, and recombinant plasmids served as positive controls and quantitative standards.
Main results
The assay was specific for AIV and NDV with no cross-reactivity with infectious bronchitis virus and no signal crossover between channels; its analytical sensitivity reached approximately 10–20 RNA copies per reaction, and qualitative results on reference isolates were fully concordant with two commercial veterinary kits (Cohen's κ = 1.00).
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The duplex assay has clear practical value for surveillance due to overlapping clinical signs of AIV and NDV infections in poultry.
Limitations and evidence strength
Expanded in silico inclusivity on contemporary sequences, a broader specificity panel, inhibitor-containing matrices, an internal amplification control, and prospective clinical validation are required before routine surveillance deployment; the evidence strength is moderate due to the need for further validation.

使用进化流行病学模型估计具有大流行潜力的人畜共患 禽流感 病毒的感染死亡率

Estimating the infection fatality ratio of zoonotic avian influenza viruses with pandemic potential using an evolutionary epidemiological model
作者： Mack, J., Li, M., Hurford, A.
摘要中文翻译人畜共患 禽流感 (AIV) 感染对人类的风险难以估计,因为许多人类 禽流感 病毒感染未被发现,因为感染可能无症状、有症状但未经测试,并且很难通过接触者追踪来识别,因为人与人之间的 传播 很罕见。我们推导出的方程考虑了引起大流行的进化机制,并参数化以与过去大流行的记录保持一致。我们估计,全球平均每年发生数以千计的人类感染具有大流行潜力的 AIV。将这些估计值与 H5N1 死亡数据相结合,我们估计历史平均感染死亡率为每 10,000 名感染者 32 人死亡(95% 不确定性区间:9.6-75 人)。这一估计值与最近大流行期间的 SARS-CoV-2 相当,并且高于季节性人类流感。我们估计,预防动物传染人类的流感 跨物种溢出 将使大流行的出现延迟几年。鉴于个人遭受严重后果的高风险,预防人类感染 AIV 是必要的,并且可以降低未来发生大流行的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了估计人类感染人畜共患 禽流感 (AIV) 风险的挑战,因为许多感染由于无症状或未经检测的病例以及罕见的人传人 传播 而未被发现。
设计与对象
该研究使用以过去流行病记录为参数的进化流行病学模型来估计具有流行潜力的人类感染AIV。
核心方法
该研究推导了考虑引发大流行的进化机制的方程,并将这些估计值与 H5N1 死亡数据相结合,以计算感染死亡率。
主要结果
该研究估计,全球每年发生数千例 AIV 人类感染,历史平均感染死亡率为每 10,000 例感染中有 32 人死亡(95% 不确定区间:9.6-75 人)。
解释与创新
作者将这一估计值解释为与最近大流行期间的 SARS-CoV-2 相当,并且高于季节性人类流感,突显了严重后果的显着风险。
科研与公卫意义
预防动物传染人类的流感 跨物种溢出 对于降低个人发生严重后果的风险并将大流行的出现延迟几年是必要的。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于进化模型和历史数据,这在捕获 AIV 感染当前和未来的动态方面可能存在局限性。

Estimating the infection fatality ratio of zoonotic avian influenza viruses with pandemic potential using an evolutionary epidemiological model

Authors: Mack, J., Li, M., Hurford, A.
Original abstract (English)
The risk of zoonotic avian influenza (AIV) infection to humans is challenging to estimate as many human avian influenza virus infections are undetected because infections may be asymptomatic, symptomatic but not tested, and difficult to identify through contact tracing, as human-to-human transmission is rare. We derive equations that consider the evolutionary mechanisms that give rise to pandemics and are parameterized to be consistent with records of past pandemics. We estimate that thousands of human infections with AIVs possessing pandemic potential occur worldwide in an average year. Combining these estimates with H5N1 fatality data, we estimate a historical average infection fatality ratio of 32 (95% uncertainty interval: 9.6-75) deaths per 10,000 infections. This estimate is comparable to SARS-CoV-2 during the recent pandemic and higher than seasonal human influenza. We estimate that preventing animal-to-human influenza spillovers would delay pandemic emergence by several years. Preventing human infections with AIVs is necessary given the high risk of severe outcomes to individuals and to reduce the risk of pandemics occurring in the future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of estimating the risk of zoonotic avian influenza (AIV) infection to humans, as many infections are undetected due to asymptomatic or untested cases and rare human-to-human transmission.
Design and population
The study uses an evolutionary epidemiological model parameterized with records of past pandemics to estimate human infections with AIVs possessing pandemic potential.
Core methods
The study derives equations considering evolutionary mechanisms that give rise to pandemics and combines these estimates with H5N1 fatality data to calculate the infection fatality ratio.
Main results
The study estimates that thousands of human infections with AIVs occur worldwide annually, with a historical average infection fatality ratio of 32 (95% uncertainty interval: 9.6-75) deaths per 10,000 infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this estimate as comparable to SARS-CoV-2 during the recent pandemic and higher than seasonal human influenza, highlighting the significant risk of severe outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
Preventing animal-to-human influenza spillovers is necessary to reduce individual risk of severe outcomes and delay pandemic emergence by several years.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on evolutionary models and historical data, which may have limitations in capturing current and future dynamics of AIV infections.

H3N2 犬流感病毒细胞和卵优先复制的遗传决定因素。

Genetic determinants of cell- and egg-preferred replication in H3N2 canine influenza virus.
作者： Rang Wang, Yaohui Zhu, Min Zhu, Xiaolong Li, Huabo Zhou, Linying Peng, Shiqi Li, Haodong Zhang, Shanshan Dong, Yifeng Qin
摘要中文翻译H3N2 犬流感病毒 (CIV) 起源于 禽流感 病毒,已在狗中传播了二十多年,但对其 宿主 适应机制和细胞趋向性仍知之甚少。在这项研究中,我们从犬呼吸道疾病 暴发 中分离出两种表型不同的 H3N2 CIV 毒株(CX3 和 CX12)。尽管两者都是使用 MDCK 细胞分离的,但 CX3 在 MDCK 细胞中表现出更高的复制效率和噬斑形成能力,而 CX12 在鸡胚胎中复制效率更高。利用反向遗传学,我们确定了 PB1 基因是病毒聚合酶活性的关键增强子,而 HA 基因是病毒稳定性的关键贡献者,这两者共同介导细胞偏好的复制。这些发现为 H3N2 CIV 宿主 适应提供了机制见解,并为未来的 疫苗 开发奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解H3N2犬流感病毒的宿主适应机制和细胞向性,该病毒起源于禽流感病毒,已在狗中传播了二十多年,但在这些方面仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,从犬呼吸道疾病 暴发 中分离出两种表型不同的 H3N2 CIV 毒株(CX3 和 CX12)。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学,确定了 PB1 基因是病毒聚合酶活性的关键增强子,而 HA 基因是病毒稳定性的关键贡献者,以研究它们在病毒复制中的作用。
主要结果
CX3 在 MDCK 细胞中表现出更高的复制效率和噬斑形成能力,而 CX12 在鸡胚中复制效率更高,其中 PB1 和 HA 基因共同介导细胞偏好的复制。
解释与创新
这些发现为 H3N2 CIV 宿主 适应提供了机制见解,并为未来的 疫苗 开发奠定了基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解病毒的趋向性和适应性,这可能为犬流感病毒的 监测 策略和 疫苗 设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于有限菌株的体外实验,由于调查范围狭窄,证据强度中等。

Genetic determinants of cell- and egg-preferred replication in H3N2 canine influenza virus.

Authors: Rang Wang, Yaohui Zhu, Min Zhu, Xiaolong Li, Huabo Zhou, Linying Peng, Shiqi Li, Haodong Zhang, Shanshan Dong, Yifeng Qin
Original abstract (English)
The H3N2 canine influenza virus (CIV), which originated from avian influenza viruses and has circulated in dogs for over two decades, remains poorly understood in terms of its host adaptation mechanisms and cellular tropism. In this study, we isolated two phenotypically distinct H3N2 CIV strains (CX3 and CX12) from a canine respiratory disease outbreak. Although both were isolated using MDCK cells, CX3 showed higher replication efficiency and plaque-forming ability in MDCK cells, whereas CX12 replicated more efficiently in chicken embryos. Using reverse genetics, we identified the PB1 gene as a critical enhancer of viral polymerase activity and the HA gene as a key contributor to viral stability, both of which collectively mediate cell-preferred replication. These findings provide mechanistic insights into H3N2 CIV host adaptation and offer a foundation for future vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the host adaptation mechanisms and cellular tropism of H3N2 canine influenza virus, which originated from avian influenza viruses and has circulated in dogs for over two decades but remains poorly understood in these aspects.
Design and population
In this study, two phenotypically distinct H3N2 CIV strains (CX3 and CX12) were isolated from a canine respiratory disease outbreak.
Core methods
Using reverse genetics, the researchers identified the PB1 gene as a critical enhancer of viral polymerase activity and the HA gene as a key contributor to viral stability to investigate their roles in viral replication.
Main results
CX3 showed higher replication efficiency and plaque-forming ability in MDCK cells, whereas CX12 replicated more efficiently in chicken embryos, with the PB1 and HA genes collectively mediating cell-preferred replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide mechanistic insights into H3N2 CIV host adaptation and offer a foundation for future vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results contribute to understanding viral tropism and adaptation, which may inform surveillance strategies and vaccine design for canine influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro experiments with limited strains, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the narrow scope of the investigation.

美国流感中人与动物界面的一个健康盲点 监测。

The One Health Blind Spot at the Human‒Animal Interface in US Influenza Surveillance.
作者： Cole King, A Paige Adams
摘要中文翻译新型甲型流感病毒在 21 世纪造成了数千人的生命和数十亿美元的经济损失,过去 4 次大流行甲型流感病毒中有 2 次可能起源于北美大陆的动物种群。尽管如此,美国的人类和兽用流感 监测 系统连接较差,并且未达到国会对数据共享和互操作性的要求。这使美国处于一种被动的姿态,重点是遏制已经发生的暴发,而不是积极主动的暴发和预防。COVID-19 大流行推动了对 监测 基础设施和基因组技术的重大投资,以及新的国家统一健康框架,为实现流感 监测 现代化提供了独特的机会,同时更加强调人类和动物健康的相互联系。然而,随着大流行时代资金的退出,增强型 监测 的资金将大幅减少。对新型 监测 技术的持续投资对于解决最近的 H5N1 流感 暴发 至关重要,对于防止未来的经济损失以及人类和动物生命的损失也至关重要。(《美国公共卫生杂志》。2026;116(8):1220-1228。https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308445)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
新型甲型流感病毒在21世纪造成了重大的人类和经济损失,过去4次大流行甲型流感病毒中有2次可能源自北美的动物种群。
设计与对象
该研究调查了美国人类和兽用流感 监测 系统之间的脱节,重点是它们未能满足国会对数据共享和互操作性的要求。
核心方法
该分析评估了美国流感 监测 基础设施和基因组技术的现状,特别是在 COVID-19 大流行和国家统一健康框架的背景下。
主要结果
尽管在流感大流行期间进行了大量投资,但流感 暴发 系统的连接较差,使该国处于被动状态,专注于 暴发 遏制而不是主动预防。
解释与创新
作者解释说,缺乏集成的 监测 代表了人与动物界面的一个关键盲点,资金的减少威胁着最近在增强的 监测 功能方面取得的进展。
科研与公卫意义
对新型 监测 技术的持续投资对于解决最近的 H5N1 暴发 问题并防止未来的经济和人类/动物生命损失至关重要,这强调了相互关联的“同一个健康”方法的必要性。
局限与证据强度
尽管通过明确识别系统性差距和政策影响来加强评估,但证据基于抽象层面的分析,没有详细的方法或统计数据。

The One Health Blind Spot at the Human‒Animal Interface in US Influenza Surveillance.

Authors: Cole King, A Paige Adams
Original abstract (English)
Novel influenza A viruses have cost thousands of lives and billions of dollars of economic losses in the 21st century, and 2 of the past 4 pandemic influenza A viruses likely originated in animal populations on the North American continent. Despite this, the human and veterinary influenza surveillance systems in the United States are poorly connected and have fallen short of congressional mandates for data sharing and interoperability. This places the United States in a reactive posture that is focused on containment of outbreaks that have already occurred rather than proactive surveillance and prevention. The significant investments in surveillance infrastructure and genomic technologies spurred by the COVID-19 pandemic, along with the new National One Health Framework, present a unique opportunity to modernize influenza surveillance with a greater emphasis on the interconnectedness of human and animal health. However, funding for enhanced surveillance is set to decrease significantly as pandemic-era funding sunsets. Continuing investments into novel surveillance technologies have been critical in addressing the recent H5N1 influenza outbreaks and will be critical to prevent future economic losses and losses of both human and animal life. ( Am J Public Health . 2026;116(8):1220-1228. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308445).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Novel influenza A viruses have caused significant human and economic losses in the 21st century, with 2 of the past 4 pandemic influenza A viruses likely originating from animal populations in North America.
Design and population
The study examines the disconnect between human and veterinary influenza surveillance systems in the United States, focusing on their failure to meet congressional mandates for data sharing and interoperability.
Core methods
The analysis evaluates the current state of influenza surveillance infrastructure and genomic technologies in the U.S., particularly in the context of the COVID-19 pandemic and the National One Health Framework.
Main results
influenza surveillance systems are poorly connected, placing the country in a reactive posture focused on outbreak containment rather than proactive prevention, despite significant pandemic-era investments.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the lack of integrated surveillance represents a critical blind spot at the human-animal interface, with decreasing funding threatening recent gains in enhanced surveillance capabilities.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continued investment in novel surveillance technologies is essential to address recent H5N1 outbreaks and prevent future economic and human/animal life losses, emphasizing the need for interconnected One Health approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on abstract-level analysis without detailed methodological or statistical data, though the assessment is strengthened by clear identification of systemic gaps and policy implications.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

在瑞士家庭 队列研究 中研究呼吸道感染 传播 的可行性:BEready(“伯尔尼,做好准备”)研究的结果。

Feasibility of studying respiratory infection transmission in a Swiss household cohort: Findings from the BEready ("Bern, get ready") study.
作者： Grimm K, E M Hodel, Gasser L, Schuller S, Wegmüller S, Magyar U, Iff F, Lim E, Furrer L, Bittel P
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
COVID-19 大流行凸显了做好大流行准备的必要性,包括建立 队列研究,以便快速收集数据和生物样本。我们在瑞士“伯尔尼,准备好”(BEready)家庭 队列研究 中调查了进行呼吸道病毒自我采样、在线问卷调查和远程医疗的可行性。
设计与对象
在这项试点研究中,我们招募了 108 个家庭:161 名成人、32 名儿童、29 只猫和 15 只狗。一周后,指示病例的家庭成员收集了拭子,包括他们的宠物的拭子,以识别继发病例。
核心方法
我们指示参与者如果出现呼吸道感染症状,则收集鼻拭子。使用市售多重 PCR 对样本进行了 13 种呼吸道病毒检测,包括 SARS-CoV-2、流感、RSV 和鼻病毒。
主要结果
From 26/05/2023 to 16/09/2024, 78 households reported 152 disease events. We obtained valid results from 131/188 (70%) expected samples from index cases, 105/152 (69%) from household contacts, and 24/47 (51%) from pets. The positivity rate of the swabs was 82/131 (63%) among index cases and 24/105 (23%) among human household contacts.
解释与创新
分散式呼吸道病毒监测在我们的家用队列研究中是可行的。我们的研究将为未来新出现的疾病(例如 H5N1 大流行)的调查提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果证明了分散式呼吸道病毒 监测 在家庭中的可行性 队列研究,它可以适应未来新出现的疾病,如 H5N1。这种方法通过实现快速数据和样本收集来支持大流行病的防范。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项试点,样本量和持续时间有限,这可能会影响普遍性。由于该研究的可行性性质和参与的家庭数量相对较少,证据强度中等。

Feasibility of studying respiratory infection transmission in a Swiss household cohort: Findings from the BEready ("Bern, get ready") study.

Authors: Grimm K, E M Hodel, Gasser L, Schuller S, Wegmüller S, Magyar U, Iff F, Lim E, Furrer L, Bittel P
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: The COVID-19 pandemic highlighted the need for pandemic preparedness, including the establishment of cohorts allowing for the rapid collection of data and biological specimens. We investigated the feasibility of performing self-sampling for respiratory viruses, online questionnaires and telemedicine in the "Bern, get ready" (BEready) household-based cohort in Switzerland. METHODS: In this pilot study, we enrolled 108 households: 161 adults, 32 children, 29 cats and 15 dogs. We instructed participants to collect a nasal swab if they experienced symptoms of a respiratory infection. Household members of index cases collected swabs, including from their pets, a week later to identify secondary cases. Samples were tested for 13 respiratory viruses, including SARS-CoV-2, Influenza, RSV and rhinovirus, using a commercially available multiplex PCR. RESULTS: From 26/05/2023 to 16/09/2024, 78 households reported 152 disease events. We obtained valid results from 131/188 (70%) expected samples from index cases, 105/152 (69%) from household contacts, and 24/47 (51%) from pets. The positivity rate of the swabs was 82/131 (63%) among index cases and 24/105 (23%) among human household contacts. CONCLUSIONS: Decentralized respiratory virus surveillance was feasible in our household-based cohort. Our study will inform the investigation of future emerging diseases, such as an H5N1 pandemic.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The COVID-19 pandemic highlighted the need for pandemic preparedness, including the establishment of cohorts allowing for the rapid collection of data and biological specimens. We investigated the feasibility of performing self-sampling for respiratory viruses, online questionnaires and telemedicine in the "Bern, get ready" (BEready) household-based cohort in Switzerland.
Design and population
In this pilot study, we enrolled 108 households: 161 adults, 32 children, 29 cats and 15 dogs. Household members of index cases collected swabs, including from their pets, a week later to identify secondary cases.
Core methods
We instructed participants to collect a nasal swab if they experienced symptoms of a respiratory infection. Samples were tested for 13 respiratory viruses, including SARS-CoV-2, Influenza, RSV and rhinovirus, using a commercially available multiplex PCR.
Main results
From 26/05/2023 to 16/09/2024, 78 households reported 152 disease events. We obtained valid results from 131/188 (70%) expected samples from index cases, 105/152 (69%) from household contacts, and 24/47 (51%) from pets. The positivity rate of the swabs was 82/131 (63%) among index cases and 24/105 (23%) among human household contacts.
Interpretation and novelty
Decentralized respiratory virus surveillance was feasible in our household-based cohort. Our study will inform the investigation of future emerging diseases, such as an H5N1 pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings demonstrate the feasibility of decentralized respiratory virus surveillance in a household cohort, which can be adapted for future emerging diseases like H5N1. This approach supports pandemic preparedness by enabling rapid data and specimen collection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a pilot with a limited sample size and duration, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the feasibility nature of the study and the relatively small number of households enrolled.

来自塞尔维亚共和国珍珠鸡的甲型流感病毒 H1N3 亚型的完整编码序列。

Complete coding sequence of influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from guinea fowl in Republic of Serbia.
作者： Dejan Vidanović, Jelena Maletić, Milanko Šekler, Bojana Tešović, Nikola Vasković, Marko Dmitrić, Biljana Đurđević, Edoardo Giussani, Gianpiero Zamperin
摘要中文翻译在这里,我们报告了在 Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION 设备上结合基于扩增子的方案获得的珍珠鸡 禽流感 A 病毒亚型 H1N3 [A/Guinea fowl/Serbia/Vinca_household/2025(H1N3)] 的完整编码序列。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在报告来自塞尔维亚共和国珍珠鸡的 禽流感 A 病毒亚型 H1N3 的完整编码序列。
设计与对象
该研究涉及从塞尔维亚的珍珠鸡样本中获取 禽流感 A 病毒 H1N3 亚型的完整编码序列。
核心方法
完整的编码序列是使用 Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION 设备结合基于扩增子的方案获得的。
主要结果
成功报道了珍珠鸡禽流感 A亚型H1N3病毒[A/Guinea fowl/Serbia/Vinca_household/2025(H1N3)]的完整编码序列。
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为对来自塞尔维亚珍珠鸡的新型 禽流感 A 病毒亚型 H1N3 进行了成功测序。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解 禽流感 A 病毒多样性,并可能为 禽流感 和禽类种群的监测工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究报告了单个病毒序列,而没有进一步分析或验证。

Complete coding sequence of influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from guinea fowl in Republic of Serbia.

Authors: Dejan Vidanović, Jelena Maletić, Milanko Šekler, Bojana Tešović, Nikola Vasković, Marko Dmitrić, Biljana Đurđević, Edoardo Giussani, Gianpiero Zamperin
Original abstract (English)
Here, we report the complete coding sequence of the avian influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from guinea fowl [A/Guinea fowl/Serbia/Vinca_household/2025(H1N3)] obtained on the Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION device in combination with an amplicon-based protocol.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to report the complete coding sequence of an avian influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from guinea fowl in the Republic of Serbia.
Design and population
The study involved obtaining the complete coding sequence of the avian influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from a guinea fowl sample in Serbia.
Core methods
The complete coding sequence was obtained using the Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION device in combination with an amplicon-based protocol.
Main results
The complete coding sequence of the avian influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from guinea fowl [A/Guinea fowl/Serbia/Vinca_household/2025(H1N3)] was successfully reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as the successful sequencing of a novel avian influenza A virus subtype H1N3 from guinea fowl in Serbia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings contribute to the understanding of avian influenza A virus diversity and may inform surveillance and monitoring efforts in avian populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study reports a single viral sequence without further analysis or validation.

受 HPAI 影响的奶牛在连续泌乳期的长期产奶量影响和恢复及其与抗体反应的关系。

Long-term milk-yield impact and recovery in HPAI-affected cows across consecutive lactations and its association with antibody response.
作者： Zelmar Rodriguez, Valentina Macchi, Lucia Martinez, Nora Wineland, Kimberly Dodd, Annette O'Connor, Catalina Picasso-Risso
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在量化 HPAI H5N1 对连续哺乳期产奶量和恢复的长期影响,并评估现场条件下抗体反应与受影响状态之间的关系。
设计与对象
对 队列研究 中受 HPAI H5N1 感染的商业奶牛群中的 1,845 头哺乳期荷斯坦奶牛进行了当前和后续哺乳期的监测。
核心方法
HPAI 受影响状态定义为每日产奶量相对 7 天基线下降 ≥ 30%。使用广义加法混合模型和基于样条的平滑时间项来分析产奶量轨迹,包括奶牛的随机截距和胎次、泌乳阶段和 HPAI 影响状态的固定效应。使用多变量逻辑和线性回归模型评估抗体反应和受影响状态之间的关联,并根据胎次和哺乳阶段进行调整。
主要结果
受影响的动物 (n = 772) 每 305 天平均累积产奶损失为 9.6% (1,109 公斤),每头受影响的奶牛平均损失 569 美元。经产奶牛 (-12.4%) 的损失率高于初产奶牛 (-3.9%)。受影响奶牛和未受影响奶牛的后续泌乳保留率相似(分别为 69.8% 和 69.2%),没有证据表明存在残留效应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,暴发 后更强的抗体反应与 暴发 期间受影响的状态相关,将产奶量下降定义为临床相关体征。新颖之处在于量化受 HPAI 影响的奶牛在连续哺乳期的长期产奶量影响和恢复模式。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为奶牛群中高致病性禽流感的 监测 和经济影响评估提供了信息,强调了巨大的经济负担和有限的恢复,特别是在多产奶牛中。这支持有针对性的干预措施,以减轻 暴发 期间的损失。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个商业奶牛群,可能会降低普遍性。尽管如此,由于样本量大、纵向设计和稳健的统计方法,证据强度很高。

Long-term milk-yield impact and recovery in HPAI-affected cows across consecutive lactations and its association with antibody response.

Authors: Zelmar Rodriguez, Valentina Macchi, Lucia Martinez, Nora Wineland, Kimberly Dodd, Annette O'Connor, Catalina Picasso-Risso
Original abstract (English)
This cohort study quantifies the long-term impact of HPAI H5N1 on cumulative milk yield and milk-yield trajectories during the affected lactation, and carryover effects in the subsequent lactation; and evaluates the relationship between antibody response and affected status under field conditions. A cohort of 1,845 lactating Holstein cows from a commercial dairy herd affected by HPAI H5N1 was monitored across current and subsequent lactations. HPAI affected status was defined as ≥ 30% decline in daily milk yield relative to a 7-d baseline. Milk yield trajectories were analyzed using generalized additive mixed models with spline-based smooth terms for time, including a random intercept for cow and fixed effects for parity, stage of lactation, and HPAI affected status. Associations between antibody response and affected status were evaluated using multivariable logistic and linear regression models, adjusted for parity and stage of lactation. Affected animals (n = 772) had an average cumulative milk loss of 9.6% (1,109 kg) per 305 d, representing an average loss of $569 per affected cow. Higher losses occurred in multiparous (-12.4%) than primiparous cows (-3.9%). Retention into the subsequent lactation was similar between affected and unaffected cows (69.8% vs. 69.2%, respectively), and no evidence of carryover effects was detected. Based on post-outbreak serologic testing (n = 121), multiparous cows had higher odds of testing ELISA-positive for antibodies to HPAI than primiparous cows (OR = 2.48, 95% CI: 1.07-6.28). The adjusted predicted proportions of cows classified as affected based on the milk-yield decline criterion during the HPAI outbreak were 63.2% among ELISA-positive cows, 42.1% among ELISA-suspect cows, and 33.2% among ELISA-negative cows. ELISA-positive cows had higher odds of experiencing severe milk-yield decline than ELISA-negative cows (OR = 3.45, 95% CI:1.44-8.69). Cows with stronger antibody response had higher odds of being classified as affected based on the milk-yield decline (OR = 0.51 per 0.05 S/N increase; 95% CI:0.35-0.75). Overall, cows affected during the HPAI outbreak had substantial milk-yield and economic losses during the affected lactation. Limited recovery was most evident among multiparous cows and among cows affected in early lactation, regardless of parity. No evidence of carryover effects was observed in the subsequent lactation. Stronger post-outbreak antibody responses were associated with affected status during the outbreak, defined by milk-yield decline as a clinically relevant sign.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to quantify the long-term impact of HPAI H5N1 on milk yield and recovery across consecutive lactations and evaluate the relationship between antibody response and affected status under field conditions.
Design and population
A cohort of 1,845 lactating Holstein cows from a commercial dairy herd affected by HPAI H5N1 was monitored across current and subsequent lactations.
Core methods
HPAI affected status was defined as ≥ 30% decline in daily milk yield relative to a 7-d baseline. Milk yield trajectories were analyzed using generalized additive mixed models with spline-based smooth terms for time, including random intercepts for cows and fixed effects for parity, stage of lactation, and HPAI affected status. Associations between antibody response and affected status were evaluated using multivariable logistic and linear regression models, adjusted for parity and stage of lactation.
Main results
Affected animals (n = 772) had an average cumulative milk loss of 9.6% (1,109 kg) per 305 d, representing an average loss of $569 per affected cow. Higher losses occurred in multiparous (-12.4%) than primiparous cows (-3.9%). Retention into the subsequent lactation was similar between affected and unaffected cows (69.8% vs. 69.2%, respectively), with no evidence of carryover effects.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that stronger post-outbreak antibody responses were associated with affected status during the outbreak, defined by milk-yield decline as a clinically relevant sign. The novelty lies in quantifying long-term milk-yield impacts and recovery patterns in HPAI-affected cows across consecutive lactations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance and economic impact assessments for HPAI in dairy herds, highlighting the substantial economic burden and limited recovery, particularly in multiparous cows. This supports targeted interventions to mitigate losses during outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single commercial dairy herd, potentially reducing generalizability. Despite this, the evidence strength is high due to the large sample size, longitudinal design, and robust statistical methods.
