使用共振识别模型分析甲型流感病毒

Analysis of Influenza A viruses using the resonant recognition model
作者： Irena Cosic, D. Cosic, Ivan Loncarevic
摘要中文翻译禽流感(禽流感)和人流感均由甲型流感病毒引起,但它们在 宿主 适应性、传播 和严重程度方面存在显着差异。在这里,我们使用了共振识别模型 (RRM),这是一种利用量子物理、生物化学和数学知识的创新方法,能够分析蛋白质与其目标(包括其他蛋白质、DNA、RNA 或小分子)之间的相互作用。使用RRM模型,我们识别了不同类型流感病毒的不同特征,并发现三组流感病毒与宿主细胞受体的相互作用完全不同。这一发现可以解释为什么不同的流感病毒只能与特定的 宿主 相互作用,以及为什么不同物种之间,特别是鸟类和人类之间的交叉感染极低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用共振识别模型分析甲型流感病毒,以了解 宿主 适应性、传播 以及禽流感病毒和人类流感病毒之间的严重性差异。
设计与对象
该研究利用共振识别模型,这是一种结合量子物理、生物化学和数学的创新方法,用于分析蛋白质与 宿主 细胞受体的相互作用。
核心方法
应用共振识别模型来识别不同流感病毒类型的特征及其与 宿主 细胞受体的相互作用。
主要结果
鉴定出与 宿主 细胞受体具有不同相互作用的三组不同的流感病毒,解释了 宿主 特异性和低跨物种感染。
解释与创新
这些发现为为什么不同的流感病毒仅与特定的 宿主 相互作用以及为什么物种之间(特别是鸟类和人类)之间的交叉感染极低提供了新颖的解释。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果增强了对 宿主 与 禽流感 中病毒相互作用的理解,可能为 监测 和人畜共患 传播 的预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它依赖于未经实验验证的计算模型,尽管该方法具有创新性且有理论依据。

Analysis of Influenza A viruses using the resonant recognition model

Authors: Irena Cosic, D. Cosic, Ivan Loncarevic
Original abstract (English)
Bird flu (Avian influenza) and human flu are both caused by Influenza A viruses, but they differ significantly in their host adaptation, transmission and severity. Here, we have used the Resonant Recognition Model (RRM), which is innovative approach using knowledge from quantum physics, biochemistry and mathematics, capable to analyse interactions between proteins and their targets, including other proteins, DNA, RNA, or small molecules. Using the RRM model we have identified different characteristics for different types of flu viruses, and we have found that there are three groups of flu viruses with completely different interactions with host cells receptors. Such finding can provide explanation why different flu viruses can interact only with specific hosts and why there is extremely low cross infection between different species, in particular between birds and humans.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze Influenza A viruses using the Resonant Recognition Model to understand host adaptation, transmission, and severity differences between avian and human flu viruses.
Design and population
The study utilized the Resonant Recognition Model, an innovative approach combining quantum physics, biochemistry, and mathematics to analyze protein interactions with host cell receptors.
Core methods
The Resonant Recognition Model was applied to identify characteristics of different flu virus types and their interactions with host cell receptors.
Main results
Three distinct groups of flu viruses with different interactions with host cell receptors were identified, explaining host specificity and low cross-species infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a novel explanation for why different flu viruses interact only with specific hosts and why cross-infection between species, particularly birds and humans, is extremely low.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results enhance understanding of host-virus interactions in avian influenza, potentially informing surveillance and prevention strategies for zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate as it relies on a computational model without experimental validation, though the approach is innovative and theoretically grounded.

整合计算机人源化和 DNA 递送来设计抗流感单克隆抗体,以改善体内表达 2267274

Integrating in silico Humanization and DNA Delivery to engineer anti-Influenza Monoclonal Antibodies for improved in vivo expression 2267274
作者： Martina Tomirotti, Ebony Gary, Elizabeth Parzych, Casey Hojecki, Madison McCanna, David Weiner
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 H5 亚型的 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 对人类和动物健康构成持续的人畜共患威胁。广泛中和抗体 (bnAb) 为大流行防范提供了强大的工具,但转化为临床通常需要人源化,以避免针对鼠抗体的免疫反应,这通常会影响其在不同病毒株中的功能。方法 我们开发了一个计算管道,将 BioPhi 与内部改进相结合,以生成 5C2 的人源化变体,5C2 是一种针对进化枝 2.3.2.1 H5N1 病毒的鼠抗体。为了评估人源化抗体是否可以保留亲本克隆的广度和效力,我们设计了一组独特的人源化构建体,并将它们与 VH/VL 嵌合体和鼠野生型 mAb 进行比较,这些构建体都针对体内表达进行了遗传优化。结果我们确认了所有构建体的体外表达,随后通过注射和体内电穿孔 (EP) 将它们作为 DNA 编码的单克隆抗体 (DMAb) 递送给野生型小鼠。DMAb 处理小鼠的血清表现出与多个 H5N1 进化枝的强结合和假病毒中和作用,包括目前流行的泛动物进化枝 2.3.4.4b 病毒。体内 DMAb 递送支持跨构建体的持续表达数月。结论 这些数据表明,优化的计算机人源化可以保留关键的功能特性,包括针对多个进化枝的交叉反应性。我们的结果支持使用这种计算工作流程来快速生成具有广泛活性的人源化抗体,突出了其针对新出现的季节性或大流行威胁的潜在应用。资金来源 NIH/NIAID CIVIC (75N93019C00051) 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫治疗 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5 病毒对人类和动物健康构成持续的人畜共患威胁,因此需要在新出现的 暴发 期间快速部署广泛的保护性对策。
设计与对象
该研究使用计算流程设计了鼠抗 H5 单克隆抗体 (5C2) 的人源化变体,并在将 DNA 递送至野生型小鼠后评估了它们的体内表达和功能。
核心方法
将 BioPhi 与内部精炼相结合的计算管道生成了鼠 5C2 抗体的人源化变体,然后针对体内表达进行了优化,并通过注射和体内电穿孔将其作为 DNA 编码的单克隆抗体 (DMAb) 递送给小鼠。
主要结果
DMAb 处理小鼠的血清对多个 H5N1 进化枝(包括当前流行的进化枝 2.3.4.4b 病毒)表现出强烈的结合和假病毒中和作用,并且在构建体中观察到持续的抗体表达数月。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,优化的计算机人源化保留了关键的功能特性,包括针对不同的 H5N1 进化枝的交叉反应性,并且计算工作流程能够快速生成具有针对新兴威胁的广泛活性的人源化抗体。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持 DNA 编码的人源化抗体作为大流行防范平台的潜力,能够通过持续的交叉反应免疫快速应对季节性或大流行性流感威胁。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外结合和假病毒中和测定以及小鼠体内表达,这使得针对人类不同循环毒株的临床疗效、安全性和性能存在不确定性。

Integrating in silico Humanization and DNA Delivery to engineer anti-Influenza Monoclonal Antibodies for improved in vivo expression 2267274

Authors: Martina Tomirotti, Ebony Gary, Elizabeth Parzych, Casey Hojecki, Madison McCanna, David Weiner
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Highly pathogenic avian influenzas (HPAI) of the H5 subtype represent an ongoing zoonotic threat to human and animal health. Broadly neutralizing antibodies (bnAbs) offer a powerful tool for pandemic preparedness, yet translation to the clinic often requires humanization to avoid immune reactions against murine antibodies, often impacting its function across divergent viral strains. Methods We developed a computational pipeline integrating BioPhi with in-house refinement to generate humanized variants of 5C2, a murine antibody elicited against a clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 virus. To assess whether humanized antibodies could retain the breadth and potency of the parental clone, we designed a panel of unique humanized constructs and compared them to a VH/VL chimera and the murine wild-type mAb, which were all genetically optimized for in vivo expression. Results We confirmed in vitro expression of all constructs and subsequently delivered them as DNA-encoded monoclonal antibody (DMAbs) via injection and in vivo electroporation (EP) to wild-type mice. Sera from DMAb treated mice exhibited strong binding and pseudovirus neutralization to multiple H5N1 clades, including the currently circulating panzootic clade 2.3.4.4b virus. In vivo DMAb delivery supported sustained expression across constructs for several months. Conclusion These data demonstrate that optimized in silico humanization can preserve key functional properties, including cross-reactivity against multiple clades. Our results support the use of this computational workflow for rapid generation of humanized antibodies with broad activity, highlighting its potential application against emerging seasonal or pandemic threats. Funding Source NIH/NIAID CIVICs (75N93019C00051) Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5 viruses pose an ongoing zoonotic threat to human and animal health, creating a need for broadly protective countermeasures that can be rapidly deployed during emerging outbreaks.
Design and population
The study engineered humanized variants of a murine anti-H5 monoclonal antibody (5C2) using a computational pipeline and assessed their expression and function in vivo after DNA delivery to wild-type mice.
Core methods
A computational pipeline integrating BioPhi with in-house refinement generated humanized variants of the murine 5C2 antibody, which were then optimized for in vivo expression and delivered as DNA-encoded monoclonal antibodies (DMAbs) via injection and in vivo electroporation to mice.
Main results
Sera from DMAb-treated mice showed strong binding and pseudovirus neutralization against multiple H5N1 clades, including the currently circulating clade 2.3.4.4b virus, and sustained antibody expression was observed across constructs for several months.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that optimized in silico humanization preserved key functional properties, including cross-reactivity against divergent H5N1 clades, and that the computational workflow enables rapid generation of humanized antibodies with broad activity against emerging threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the potential of DNA-encoded humanized antibodies as a platform for pandemic preparedness, enabling rapid response to seasonal or pandemic influenza threats through sustained, cross-reactive immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro binding and pseudovirus neutralization assays and in vivo expression in mice, leaving uncertainty about clinical efficacy, safety, and performance against diverse circulating strains in humans.

Question and background
The study aimed to promote avian physiology research led by women, particularly those early in their careers, by compiling a series of papers focused on various aspects of avian biology.

鼻内施用泛反应性流感抗体揭示了 Fc 独立的保护模式 2259232

Intranasal administration of a pan-reactive influenza antibody reveals Fc-independent mode of protection 2259232
作者： Faez Amokrane Nait Mohamed, Anna Beukenhorst, Jacopo Frallicciardi, Jaap Goudsmit, Daniel Lingwood, Keira Rice
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 单克隆抗体通过两个核心机制提供保护:其抗原结合片段 (Fab) 直接结合并中和病毒抗原,而片段可结晶结构域 (Fc) 与 Fcγ 受体结合以激活免疫效应功能。我们通过比较针对流感病毒的抗体预防小鼠模型中的外周/全身与局部/粘膜递送,研究并分离了每个抗体结构域对免疫保护的相对贡献。方法我们进行了CR9114的鼻内与静脉内递送,CR9114是已知最广泛的人类流感抗体,其与血凝素茎上的保守表位结合,以在临床前挑战模型中提供针对甲型流感和乙型流感病毒的保护。在用 A/H1N1、A/H3N2、A/H5N1、B/Yamagata 或 B/Victoria 病毒致死攻击前 24 小时,小鼠接受野生型 CR9114、Fc 沉默突变体(LALA、N297Q)或单独的二价抗原结合 F(ab’)2 片段(无 Fc 结构域)。测量存活率和体重变化以确定保护作用和中位有效剂量 (ED50)。结果 与静脉内给药相比,鼻内给药 CR9114 的抗致命性流感 A/H1N1、A/H5N1、A/H3N2、B/Yam 和 B/Vic 的效力提高了 50 倍。全身保护需要完整的 Fc 结构域,而即使采用 Fc 沉默或 Fc 耗尽的形式,鼻内保护仍然很强。当局部递送时,单独的 F(ab’)2 片段可以提供充分的预防作用,但全身无效。结论这些发现揭示了抗体递送途径决定了其保护机制。局部粘膜递送实现了有效的不依赖于 Fc 的免疫,这在缺乏常规中和活性的情况下也可见到,为基于抗体的流感和其他呼吸道感染的预防提供了新策略。资金来源 Leyden Laboratories B.V. 主题类别 粘膜和区域免疫学 (MUC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过比较小鼠模型中的外周/全身与局部/粘膜递送,研究和分离抗体结构域对流感病毒免疫保护的相对贡献。
设计与对象
该研究是在针对流感病毒的抗体预防的小鼠模型中进行的,比较了针对多种流感病毒株的最广泛已知的人类流感抗体 CR9114 的鼻内和静脉内递送。
核心方法
小鼠在甲型/H1N1、A/H3N2、A/H5N1、B/Yamagata 或 B/Victoria 流感病毒致死攻击前 24 小时单独接受野生型 CR9114、Fc 沉默突变体(LALA、N297Q)或二价抗原结合 F(ab’)2 片段;测量存活率和体重变化以确定保护作用和中位有效剂量(ED50)。
主要结果
与静脉内给药相比,鼻内给药 CR9114 导致抗致命性流感 A/H1N1、A/H5N1、A/H3N2、B/Yamagata 和 B/Victoria 的效力增加高达 50 倍;全身保护需要完整的 Fc 结构域,而即使采用 Fc 沉默或 Fc 耗尽的形式,鼻内保护仍然很强。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,抗体递送途径决定了其保护机制,即使在缺乏常规中和活性的情况下,局部粘膜递送也能实现有效的 Fc 独立免疫。
科研与公卫意义
局部粘膜递送为基于抗体的流感和其他呼吸道感染的预防提供了一种新策略,可能增强对包括 禽流感 A/H5N1 在内的多种毒株的保护。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于小鼠的临床前挑战模型,虽然在这方面的研究结果是可靠的,但还需要进一步的研究来证实对人类的功效;由于翻译范围有限,证据强度中等。

Intranasal administration of a pan-reactive influenza antibody reveals Fc-independent mode of protection 2259232

Authors: Faez Amokrane Nait Mohamed, Anna Beukenhorst, Jacopo Frallicciardi, Jaap Goudsmit, Daniel Lingwood, Keira Rice
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Monoclonal antibodies protect through two core mechanisms: their antigen-binding fragment (Fab) directly binds and neutralizes viral antigens, while the fragment crystallizable domain (Fc) engages Fcγ receptors to activate immune effector functions. We investigated and segregated the relative contribution of each antibody domain to immune protection by comparing peripheral/systemic versus local/mucosal delivery within a mouse model of antibody prophylaxis against influenza virus Methods We performed intranasal versus intravenous delivery of CR9114, the broadest known human influenza antibody, which engages a conserved epitope on the hemagglutinin stem to provide protection against both influenza A and influenza B viruses within pre-clinical challenge models. Mice received either wild-type CR9114, Fc-silenced mutants (LALA, N297Q), or the bivalent antigen-binding F(ab’)2 fragment alone (no Fc domain) 24 hours before lethal challenge with influenza A/H1N1, A/H3N2, A/H5N1, B/Yamagata, or B/Victoria viruses. Survival and weight change were measured to determine protection and median effective doses (ED50). Results Intranasal delivery of CR9114 resulted in an increase in potency against lethal influenza A/H1N1, A/H5N1, A/H3N2, B/Yam and B/Vic compared to intravenous administration, up to 50-fold. Systemic protection required an intact Fc domain, whereas intranasal protection remained strong even with Fc-silenced or Fc-depleted formats. The F(ab’)2 fragment alone conferred full prophylaxis when delivered locally but was ineffective systemically. Conclusion These findings reveal that the route of antibody delivery determines its mechanism of protection. Local mucosal delivery achieves potent Fc-independent immunity, which was also seen in the absence of conventional neutralization activity, offering a new strategy for antibody-based prevention of influenza and other respiratory infections. Funding Source Leyden Laboratories B.V. Topic Categories Mucosal and Regional Immunology (MUC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate and segregate the relative contribution of antibody domains to immune protection against influenza virus by comparing peripheral/systemic versus local/mucosal delivery in a mouse model.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a mouse model of antibody prophylaxis against influenza virus, comparing intranasal versus intravenous delivery of the broadest known human influenza antibody CR9114 against multiple influenza strains.
Core methods
Mice received wild-type CR9114, Fc-silenced mutants (LALA, N297Q), or the bivalent antigen-binding F(ab’)2 fragment alone 24 hours before lethal challenge with influenza A/H1N1, A/H3N2, A/H5N1, B/Yamagata, or B/Victoria viruses; survival and weight change were measured to determine protection and median effective doses (ED50).
Main results
Intranasal delivery of CR9114 resulted in an increase in potency against lethal influenza A/H1N1, A/H5N1, A/H3N2, B/Yamagata, and B/Victoria compared to intravenous administration, up to 50-fold; systemic protection required an intact Fc domain, whereas intranasal protection remained strong even with Fc-silenced or Fc-depleted formats.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that the route of antibody delivery determines its mechanism of protection, with local mucosal delivery achieving potent Fc-independent immunity even in the absence of conventional neutralization activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
Local mucosal delivery offers a new strategy for antibody-based prevention of influenza and other respiratory infections, potentially enhancing protection against diverse strains including avian influenza A/H5N1.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on pre-clinical challenge models in mice, and while the findings are robust within this context, further studies are needed to confirm efficacy in humans; the evidence strength is moderate due to the limited translational scope.

使用 TLR4/9 组合佐剂优化 T 细胞启动,以实现强大的细胞介导的流感免疫 2307646

Optimizing T Cell Priming Using TLR4/9 Combination Adjuvant For Robust Cell-Mediated Immunity Against Influenza 2307646
作者： Dan Nguyen, David Davies, Jiin Felgner, Erwin Strahsburger, Angel Zavala, Jenny Hernandez-Davies, Shivashankar Othy, Amit Jairaman
摘要中文翻译摘要 介绍 亚基 疫苗 是安全的,但很少能诱导强大、持久的免疫,特别是细胞介导的免疫(CMI)。在这里,我们研究了 IVAX-1(在角鲨烯水包油乳液 (AddaVax) 中配制的 TLR4 (MPLA) 和 TLR9 (CpG 1018) 激动剂组合)介导保护和产生 CMI 的潜力。方法 我们利用具有命运图谱报告基因的鼠 H5N1 流感模型、条件敲除小鼠、多光子成像和流式细胞术来阐明 IVAX-1 驱动免疫的早期时空特征。通过 ELISpot 和回忆分析评估抗原特异性 CMI。结果 IVAX-1 免疫引发增强了树突状细胞 (DC) 的成熟和骨髓细胞向注射部位的募集,导致引流淋巴结 (DLN) 中中性粒细胞、单核细胞和抗原呈递 DC 的数量增加,与抗原清除动力学无关。这在 DLN 中诱导了 IFN-γ+ 细胞岛,表明克隆扩张。此外,IVAX-1 产生了强大的抗原特异性功能性 (IFN-γ+) T 细胞反应。删除 1 型常规 DC (XCR1+) 细胞可消除 IVAX-1 介导的 T 细胞反应。此外,使用 IVAX-1 进行免疫可以在肺和脾中产生显着更高数量的流感特异性记忆 T 细胞。IVAX-1 在免疫后长达 60 天提供持久保护和出色的肺部病毒清除能力。结论 通过多种 TLR 途径利用协同先天免疫激活,该组合可优化 T 细胞启动,而不依赖于抗原库效应。我们的研究结果确定了关键的早期先天免疫事件和细胞介质,这些事件和细胞介质可驱动强大的 CMI,并证明 TLR 激动剂组合具有引发持久 T 细胞免疫的潜力。资金来源 NIH/NIAID 拨款 U01AI160397 和 R01AI168063;佐剂比较和表征合同 BAA-DAIT-75N93020R00022;并获得国防威胁减少机构的拨款#HDTRA-18-1-0036;加州大学欧文分校 UROP 研究经验奖学金;NIH-MARC U-STAR trai 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
亚基 疫苗 是安全的,但很少诱导强大、持久的免疫,尤其是细胞介导的免疫 (CMI)。
设计与对象
我们使用鼠 H5N1 流感模型研究了 IVAX-1(在角鲨烯水包油乳液 (AddaVax) 中配制的 TLR4 (MPLA) 和 TLR9 (CpG 1018) 激动剂组合)介导保护和产生 CMI 的潜力。
核心方法
我们利用带有命运图谱报告基因的鼠 H5N1 流感模型、条件敲除小鼠、多光子成像和流式细胞术来阐明 IVAX-1 驱动免疫的早期时空特征。通过 ELISpot 和回忆分析评估抗原特异性 CMI。
主要结果
使用 IVAX-1 进行免疫引发可增强树突状细胞 (DC) 的成熟以及将骨髓细胞募集到注射部位,从而导致引流淋巴结 (DLN) 中出现更多数量的中性粒细胞、单核细胞和抗原呈递 DC,而与抗原清除动力学无关。这在 DLN 中诱导了 IFN-γ+ 细胞岛,表明克隆扩张。IVAX-1 产生了强大的抗原特异性功能性 (IFN-γ+) T 细胞反应,并在肺和脾中产生了显着更高数量的流感特异性记忆 T 细胞。IVAX-1 在免疫后长达 60 天提供持久保护和出色的肺部病毒清除能力。
解释与创新
通过利用多种 TLR 途径的协同先天免疫激活,该组合可优化 T 细胞启动,而不依赖于抗原库效应。我们的研究结果确定了关键的早期先天免疫事件和细胞介质,这些事件和细胞介质可驱动强大的 CMI,并证明 TLR 激动剂组合具有引发持久 T 细胞免疫的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,像 IVAX-1 这样的 TLR4/9 组合佐剂可以通过促进强大的细胞介导的免疫来增强亚基 疫苗 对抗流感的功效,这对于长期保护和病毒清除至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在鼠 H5N1 流感模型中进行的,这可能会限制对人类的直接推广。然而,多种先进技术的使用和多次检测的一致结果为研究结果提供了高度可信度。

Optimizing T Cell Priming Using TLR4/9 Combination Adjuvant For Robust Cell-Mediated Immunity Against Influenza 2307646

Authors: Dan Nguyen, David Davies, Jiin Felgner, Erwin Strahsburger, Angel Zavala, Jenny Hernandez-Davies, Shivashankar Othy, Amit Jairaman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Subunit vaccines are safe but rarely induce robust, long-lasting immunity, especially cell-mediated immunity (CMI). Here, we investigated the potential of IVAX-1, a combination of TLR4 (MPLA) and TLR9 (CpG 1018) agonists formulated in a squalene oil-in-water emulsion (AddaVax), to mediate protection and generate CMI. Methods We leveraged a murine H5N1 influenza model with fate-map reporters, conditional knock-out mice, multiphoton imaging, and flow cytometry to elucidate early spatiotemporal features of IVAX-1-driven immunity. Antigen-specific CMI was evaluated via ELISpot and recall assays. Results Immune priming with IVAX-1 enhanced dendritic cell (DC) maturation and recruitment of myeloid cells to the injection site, resulting in higher numbers of neutrophils, monocytes, and antigen-presenting DCs in the draining lymph node (DLN), independent of antigen clearance kinetics. This induced islands of IFN-γ+ cells in the DLN, suggesting clonal expansion. In addition, IVAX-1 generated a robust antigen-specific functional (IFN-γ+) T cell response. Deletion of Type 1 conventional DCs (XCR1+) cells abrogated IVAX-1-mediated T cell response. Furthermore, immunization with IVAX-1 generated significantly higher numbers of flu-specific memory T cells in the lungs and spleen. IVAX-1 conferred durable protection and superior viral clearance from the lungs up to 60 days after immunization. Conclusion By leveraging synergistic innate immune activation through multiple TLR pathways, the combination optimizes T cell priming without relying on antigen depot effects. Our findings identify key early innate immune events and cellular mediators that drive robust CMI and demonstrate the potential of TLR agonist combinations to elicit durable T-cell immunity. Funding Source NIH/NIAID grants U01AI160397 & R01AI168063; Adjuvant Comparison and Characterization contract BAA-DAIT-75N93020R00022; and grant from the Defense Threat Reduction Agency #HDTRA-18-1-0036; UC Irvine UROP Research Experience Fellowship; NIH-MARC U-STAR trai Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Subunit vaccines are safe but rarely induce robust, long-lasting immunity, especially cell-mediated immunity (CMI).
Design and population
We investigated the potential of IVAX-1, a combination of TLR4 (MPLA) and TLR9 (CpG 1018) agonists formulated in a squalene oil-in-water emulsion (AddaVax), to mediate protection and generate CMI using a murine H5N1 influenza model.
Core methods
We leveraged a murine H5N1 influenza model with fate-map reporters, conditional knock-out mice, multiphoton imaging, and flow cytometry to elucidate early spatiotemporal features of IVAX-1-driven immunity. Antigen-specific CMI was evaluated via ELISpot and recall assays.
Main results
Immune priming with IVAX-1 enhanced dendritic cell (DC) maturation and recruitment of myeloid cells to the injection site, resulting in higher numbers of neutrophils, monocytes, and antigen-presenting DCs in the draining lymph node (DLN), independent of antigen clearance kinetics. This induced islands of IFN-γ+ cells in the DLN, suggesting clonal expansion. IVAX-1 generated a robust antigen-specific functional (IFN-γ+) T cell response and significantly higher numbers of flu-specific memory T cells in the lungs and spleen. IVAX-1 conferred durable protection and superior viral clearance from the lungs up to 60 days after immunization.
Interpretation and novelty
By leveraging synergistic innate immune activation through multiple TLR pathways, the combination optimizes T cell priming without relying on antigen depot effects. Our findings identify key early innate immune events and cellular mediators that drive robust CMI and demonstrate the potential of TLR agonist combinations to elicit durable T-cell immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that TLR4/9 combination adjuvants like IVAX-1 can enhance the efficacy of subunit vaccines against influenza by promoting robust cell-mediated immunity, which is critical for long-term protection and viral clearance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a murine H5N1 influenza model, which may limit direct generalizability to humans. However, the use of multiple advanced techniques and consistent results across multiple assays provides high confidence in the findings.

流感 H5 头和茎结构域对粘膜 rH5-NE 疫苗接种 2333118 后功能性非中和抗体开发的贡献

Contribution of Influenza H5 Head and Stalk Domains to the Development of Functional Non-Neutralizing Antibodies Following Mucosal rH5-NE Vaccination 2333118
作者： Raymond Ash, Yuanyuan Liang, Jingping Hu, Gabrielle Russell, Juliahna Hayes, Lynda Coughlan, Meagan Deming, Chad Costley, Marcelo Sztein, Justin Ortiz
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 在之前的 1 期试验中,我们表明鼻内 (IN) 重组血凝素 (HA) H5 疫苗 (rH5;A/Indo/05) 纳米乳佐剂 (rH5 NE) 在第 1 天和第 29 天给予可有效启动免疫系统。参与者接受 rH5 NE(25-100 μg;IN;n = 24)、未佐剂的 rH5(100 μg;IN;n = 7)或安慰剂(n = 6)。在第 197 天异源肌内 (IM) H5N1 加强注射后,只有 rH5 NE 受体表现出 HAI 滴度显着增加,证实粘膜启动成功。抗 H5 抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 活性也证明了这种启动效应。在这里,我们评估了 rH5 NE 是否引发对不同 H5 HA 的交叉反应性 ADCC 和抗体依赖性细胞吞噬作用 (ADCP) 反应,并检查了 HA 头和茎结构域的贡献。方法 对第 1、57 和 225 天的血浆进行分析。使用包被有整个 H5 HA、仅头部或仅茎部的 H5 HA 的珠子来评估 ADCC,该 H5 HA 与血浆一起孵育并暴露于表达 CD16a 的报告细胞系。ADCP 通过抗原包被的 AF488 珠的 THP 1 摄取进行定量。ADCC 血清转化定义为增加≥ 4 倍;ADCP 血清转化增加 ≥ 2 倍。结果 到第 57 天,33% (8/24) rH5 NE 接受者显示 ADCC 血清转化为 A/Indo/05 全 HA,在第 197 天 IM 加强后上升至 50%。未佐剂的 rH5 和安慰剂仅在加强后才发生血清转化(分别为 4/7=57% 和 4/6=67%)。A/Viet/04 和 A/cow Tx/24 也出现了类似的趋势。rH5 NE 中的头部特异性 ADCC 血清转化在第 57 天达到 46% (11/24),在第 225 天达到 88% (21/24),而未佐剂的 rH5 仅在第 225 天有反应 (4/7=57%)。安慰剂没有显示任何反应。茎特异性 ADCC 在所有群体中基本上已存在,疫苗 诱导的增加极少。ADCP 趋势反映了 ADCC 的调查结果。结论 鼻内 rH5 NE 诱导交叉反应性 ADCC 和 ADCP 反应,主要由针对 H5 头的抗体驱动,支持其诱导有效粘膜启动的能力。资金来源来自以下资助的支持:NIH U01AI148081,BlueWillow Contract,主要作者是 UMB-ITP-Vax 学者 (NIH R25AI175011),主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估鼻内重组血凝素 H5 疫苗 (rH5 NE) 是否引发对不同 H5 血凝素的交叉反应性抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 和抗体依赖性细胞吞噬作用 (ADCP) 反应,并检查了 HA 头和茎结构域的贡献。
设计与对象
参与者接受 rH5 NE(25-100 μg;鼻内;n = 24)、未佐剂的 rH5(100 μg;鼻内;n = 7)或安慰剂(n = 6)。
核心方法
对第 1 天、第 57 天和第 225 天的血浆进行了分析。使用包被有整个 H5 HA、仅头部或仅茎部的 H5 HA 的珠子来评估 ADCC,该 H5 HA 与血浆一起孵育并暴露于表达 CD16a 的报告细胞系。ADCP 通过抗原包被的 AF488 珠的 THP-1 摄取进行定量。
主要结果
到第 57 天,33% (8/24) rH5 NE 接受者显示 ADCC 血清转化为 A/Indo/05 全 HA,在第 197 天肌内加强后上升至 50%。rH5 NE 中的头部特异性 ADCC 血清转化在第 57 天达到 46% (11/24),在第 225 天达到 88% (21/24)。茎特异性 ADCC 在所有组中基本上预先存在,疫苗 诱导的增加极少。
解释与创新
作者解释说,鼻内 rH5 NE 诱导交叉反应性 ADCC 和 ADCP 反应,主要由针对 H5 头的抗体驱动,支持其诱导有效粘膜启动的能力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,粘膜 rH5 NE 疫苗接种 可能是启动免疫系统对抗多种 H5 禽流感 病毒的有前途的策略,可能为广泛保护性 疫苗 的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
The study was limited to a small sample size and focused on immunological outcomes without clinical endpoints. The evidence strength is moderate due to the Phase 1 trial design and abstract-only data.

Contribution of Influenza H5 Head and Stalk Domains to the Development of Functional Non-Neutralizing Antibodies Following Mucosal rH5-NE Vaccination 2333118

Authors: Raymond Ash, Yuanyuan Liang, Jingping Hu, Gabrielle Russell, Juliahna Hayes, Lynda Coughlan, Meagan Deming, Chad Costley, Marcelo Sztein, Justin Ortiz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction In a prior Phase 1 trial we showed that an intranasal (IN) recombinant hemagglutinin (HA) H5 vaccine (rH5; A/Indo/05) nanoemulsion-adjuvanted (rH5 NE) and given on days 1 and 29 effectively primed the immune system. Participants received rH5 NE (25—100 μg; IN; n = 24), unadjuvanted rH5 (100 μg; IN; n = 7), or placebo (n = 6). Only rH5 NE recipients exhibited significant increases in HAI titers after a heterologous intramuscular (IM) H5N1 booster on day 197, confirming successful mucosal priming. Anti-H5 antibody-dependent cell cytotoxicity (ADCC) activity also demonstrated this priming effect. Here, we evaluated whether rH5 NE elicited cross reactive ADCC and antibody-dependent cell phagocytosis (ADCP) responses to diverse H5 HAs and examined contributions of HA head and stalk domains. Methods Plasma from days 1, 57, and 225 was analyzed. ADCC was assessed using beads coated with whole, head only, or stalk only H5 HAs incubated with plasma and exposed to a CD16a expressing reporter cell line. ADCP was quantified by THP 1 uptake of antigen-coated AF488—beads. ADCC seroconversion was defined as a ≥ 4 fold increase; ADCP seroconversion as a ≥ 2 fold increase. Results By day 57, 33% (8/24) rH5 NE recipients showed ADCC seroconversion to A/Indo/05 whole HA, rising to 50% after the day-197 IM boost. Unadjuvanted rH5 and placebos seroconverted only after boosting (4/7=57% & 4/6=67%, respectively). Similar trends were seen for A/Viet/04 and A/cow Tx/24. Head specific ADCC seroconversion in rH5 NE reached 46% (11/24) at day 57 and 88% (21/24) at day 225, whereas unadjuvanted rH5 responded only at day 225 (4/7=57%). Placebos showed no responses. Stalk specific ADCC was largely preexisting across all groups, with minimal vaccine induced increases. ADCP trends mirrored ADCC findings. Conclusion Intranasal rH5 NE induces cross reactive ADCC and ADCP responses driven primarily by antibodies targeting the H5-head, supporting its capacity to induce effective mucosal priming. Funding Source Support from the following Grants: NIH U01AI148081, BlueWillow Contract, lead author is a UMB-ITP-Vax scholar (NIH R25AI175011), Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate whether an intranasal recombinant hemagglutinin H5 vaccine (rH5 NE) elicited cross-reactive antibody-dependent cell cytotoxicity (ADCC) and antibody-dependent cell phagocytosis (ADCP) responses to diverse H5 hemagglutinins and examined the contributions of HA head and stalk domains.
Design and population
Participants received rH5 NE (25–100 μg; intranasal; n = 24), unadjuvanted rH5 (100 μg; intranasal; n = 7), or placebo (n = 6).
Core methods
Plasma from days 1, 57, and 225 was analyzed. ADCC was assessed using beads coated with whole, head only, or stalk only H5 HAs incubated with plasma and exposed to a CD16a expressing reporter cell line. ADCP was quantified by THP-1 uptake of antigen-coated AF488-beads.
Main results
By day 57, 33% (8/24) rH5 NE recipients showed ADCC seroconversion to A/Indo/05 whole HA, rising to 50% after the day-197 intramuscular boost. Head-specific ADCC seroconversion in rH5 NE reached 46% (11/24) at day 57 and 88% (21/24) at day 225. Stalk-specific ADCC was largely preexisting across all groups, with minimal vaccine-induced increases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that intranasal rH5 NE induces cross-reactive ADCC and ADCP responses driven primarily by antibodies targeting the H5-head, supporting its capacity to induce effective mucosal priming.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that mucosal rH5 NE vaccination could be a promising strategy for priming the immune system against diverse H5 avian influenza viruses, potentially informing the development of broadly protective vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small sample size and focused on immunological outcomes without clinical endpoints. The evidence strength is moderate due to the Phase 1 trial design and abstract-only data.

局部免疫网络促进粘膜后呼吸道 IgA 反应 疫苗接种 2260370

Local immune networks promote respiratory IgA responses after mucosal vaccination 2260370
作者： Jinyi Tang, Arka Sen Chaudhuri, Panke Qu, Yue Wu, You Xu, An Fang, Fangming Zhu, Takao Kobayashi, J. Taylor, Haitao Hu
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 粘膜免疫和呼吸道 IgA 在预防包括 SARS-CoV-2 在内的空气传播病原体方面具有重要前景。然而,粘膜 疫苗接种 后导致呼吸道 IgA 反应强烈诱导的条件和机制仍知之甚少。方法在本项目中,我们建立了SARS-CoV-2突破性感染和基于粘膜腺病毒的加强疫苗接种模型。使用遗传小鼠模型和各种体内外阻断或耗竭方法,我们进一步研究了调节呼吸 IgA 反应的机制。结果我们在小鼠模型中发现基于粘膜腺病毒的加强免疫 疫苗接种 表明,呼吸道加强免疫可引发明显更强、更持久的呼吸道 IgA、T 细胞反应和针对 SARS-CoV-2 的保护性免疫,支持了呼吸道粘膜 疫苗接种 的前景。从机制上讲,我们发现局部 CD4+ T 细胞通过促进 IgA+ B 细胞类别转换、增殖和原位存活来支持呼吸道 IgA 的产生。这种 T 细胞帮助取决于局部 Blimp-1+ T-bet+ Th1 细胞和 B 细胞之间的相互作用,通过 ICOS/CD40L/MHC-II 接合和 IL-21 信号传导介导。此外,肺巨噬细胞通过 TGF-β 的产生促进粘膜 IgA 反应。因此,通过粘膜给予封装在肺表面活性剂(PS)中的 SARS-CoV-2 Spike 编码 mRNA,该 mRNA 掺入了旨在靶向肺巨噬细胞的脂质纳米颗粒,从而引发了更强的粘膜 IgA 反应。结论 我们的结果揭示了支持增强粘膜 IgA 反应的局部细胞网络,这对于制定针对 SARS-CoV-2 和其他呼吸道病原体(如 H5N1 流感)的最佳粘膜免疫策略具有重要意义。资金来源 AI147394、AG069264、AI112844、HL170961、AI154598、AI176171 至 J.S.、AI67986、AI177467 至 Hui.H;U54CA260582 为 S.L.L;R01AI168684 至 G.Z.;S.L.L 还得到了一位匿名私人捐助者向俄亥俄州立大学提供的资金的支持。主题类别 粘膜和区域免疫学 (MUC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解粘膜 疫苗接种 后导致呼吸道 IgA 反应强烈诱导的条件和机制,因为粘膜免疫和呼吸道 IgA 在预防包括 SARS-CoV-2 在内的空气传播病原体方面提供了重要前景。
设计与对象
该研究使用遗传小鼠模型,建立了SARS-CoV-2突破性感染和基于粘膜腺病毒的加强疫苗接种模型,以研究调节呼吸道IgA反应的机制。
核心方法
研究人员采用各种体内和体外阻断或耗竭方法来检查调节呼吸道 IgA 反应的机制,包括局部 CD4+ T 细胞和肺巨噬细胞的作用。
主要结果
结果表明,基于粘膜腺病毒的加强剂 疫苗接种 在小鼠模型中引发了明显更强、更持久的呼吸道 IgA、T 细胞反应和针对 SARS-CoV-2 的保护性免疫。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为揭示了支持增强粘膜 IgA 反应的局部细胞网络,这对于制定针对 SARS-CoV-2 和其他呼吸道病原体(如 H5N1 流感)的最佳粘膜免疫策略具有新的意义。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 SARS-CoV-2 和其他呼吸道病原体的粘膜免疫策略的开发提供信息,从而有可能提高 疫苗 的功效和保护性免疫力。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到小鼠模型使用的限制,可能无法完全转化为人类反应,并且证据基于体内和体外实验,这可能会引入偏差或混杂因素。

Local immune networks promote respiratory IgA responses after mucosal vaccination 2260370

Authors: Jinyi Tang, Arka Sen Chaudhuri, Panke Qu, Yue Wu, You Xu, An Fang, Fangming Zhu, Takao Kobayashi, J. Taylor, Haitao Hu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Mucosal immunization and respiratory IgA offer significant promise in protecting against airborne pathogens, including SARS-CoV-2. However, the conditions and mechanisms that lead to the robust induction of respiratory IgA responses following mucosal vaccination remain poorly understood. Methods In this project, we established SARS-CoV-2 breakthrough infection and mucosal adenovirus-based booster vaccination models. Using genetic mouse models and various in vivo and in vitro blockade or depletion approaches, we further investigated the mechanisms regulating respiratory IgA responses. Results Here we found that a mucosal adenovirus-based booster vaccination in mouse models revealed that respiratory booster immunization elicited markedly stronger and more durable respiratory IgA, T cell response, and protective immunity against SARS-CoV-2, supporting the promise of respiratory mucosal vaccination. Mechanistically, we identified that local CD4+ T cells support respiratory IgA production by promoting IgA+ B cell class switching, proliferation, and survival in situ. This T cell help depended on interactions between local Blimp-1+ T-bet+ Th1 cells and B cells, mediated through ICOS/CD40L/MHC-II engagement and IL-21 signaling. Furthermore, lung macrophages contributed to the mucosal IgA response via TGF-β production. Consequently, mucosal administration of SARS-CoV-2 Spike-encoding mRNA encapsulated in pulmonary surfactant (PS)- incorporated lipid nanoparticles designed to target lung macrophages elicited a stronger mucosal IgA response. Conclusion Our results uncover a local cellular network supporting enhanced mucosal IgA responses, with implications for the development of optimal mucosal immunization strategies against SARS-CoV-2 and other respiratory pathogens such as H5N1 influenza. Funding Source AI147394, AG069264, AI112844, HL170961, AI154598, AI176171 to J.S., AI67986, AI177467 to Hui.H; U54CA260582 to S.L.L; R01AI168684 to G. Z.; S.L.L was also supported by a fund provided by an anonymous private donor to The Ohio State University. Topic Categories Mucosal and Regional Immunology (MUC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the conditions and mechanisms that lead to the robust induction of respiratory IgA responses following mucosal vaccination, as mucosal immunization and respiratory IgA offer significant promise in protecting against airborne pathogens, including SARS-CoV-2.
Design and population
The study used genetic mouse models and established SARS-CoV-2 breakthrough infection and mucosal adenovirus-based booster vaccination models to investigate the mechanisms regulating respiratory IgA responses.
Core methods
The researchers employed various in vivo and in vitro blockade or depletion approaches to examine the mechanisms regulating respiratory IgA responses, including the role of local CD4+ T cells and lung macrophages.
Main results
The results showed that mucosal adenovirus-based booster vaccination in mouse models elicited markedly stronger and more durable respiratory IgA, T cell response, and protective immunity against SARS-CoV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as uncovering a local cellular network that supports enhanced mucosal IgA responses, which has novel implications for the development of optimal mucosal immunization strategies against SARS-CoV-2 and other respiratory pathogens such as H5N1 influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of mucosal immunization strategies against SARS-CoV-2 and other respiratory pathogens, potentially improving vaccine efficacy and protective immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of mouse models, which may not fully translate to human responses, and the evidence is based on in vivo and in vitro experiments, which may introduce bias or confounding factors.

评估流感血凝素广泛中和抗体的 AlphaFold 3 免疫复合物结构预测和冷冻电镜分析 2309571

Evaluating AlphaFold 3 immune complex structure prediction and cryo-EM analysis of broadly neutralizing antibodies towards influenza hemagglutinin 2309571
作者： Morgan Gee, Pragati Sharma, Alesandra Rodriguez, Jordan Clark, Olivia Swanson, Florian Krammer, Andrew Ward, Julianna Han, Briney Bryan
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 表位:互补位相互作用定义了抗体对抗原的广度和亲和力。计算结构预测有助于对免疫复合物进行建模,作为计算机筛选广泛中和抗体 (bnAb) 的方法。然而,我们目前还不了解结构预测和置信度指标如何与实验数据相关联。在这里,我们评估了 AlphaFold 3 (AF3) 在预测已知类别 bnAb 对流感血凝素 (HA) 保守表位的免疫复合物方面的准确性。方法 我们使用单细胞 RNA 测序来生成 HA 反应性配对抗体序列文库。AF3 用于计算模拟抗体-HA 相互作用。通过 BLI、ELISA 和微量中和测定来表达和表征抗体。使用负染色电子显微镜 (nsEM) 和单粒子冷冻电子显微镜 (SPA Cryo-EM) 对免疫复合物进行结构表征。结果 对 23 名健康人类捐赠者的 PBMC 进行 B 细胞受体测序,生成了 1161 个 H5 HA 特异性配对抗体序列。这些序列包含已知类别抗体(VH1-69,其中先前的示例位于 AF3 训练集中)中的新示例,可能以保守的中央茎为目标。我们使用 AF3 对这些抗体序列与 H1N1 和 H5N1 HA 的复合物进行建模。高度可信的预测与具有高亲和力和广度的抗体相关。此外,计算机表位足迹与实验 nsEM 数据高度匹配。最后,我们使用高分辨率 SPA 冷冻电镜解决了原子细节,解释了 AF3 免疫复合物预测的挑战。结论 AF3 可用于筛选针对流感 HA 保守表位的 VH1-69 bnAb,且可信度较高。这种向下选择的方法可以应用于其他类别的抗体和其他表位。然而,仍然需要调整模型以准确预测新型或特征较少的抗体类别的免疫复合物。资金来源:国家过敏和传染病研究所 (NIAID) 斯卡格斯化学与生物科学研究生院的奖学金。主题类别 计算和系统免疫学 (COMP)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
表位:互补位相互作用定义了抗体对抗原的广度和亲和力,但结构预测和实验数据之间的相关性尚不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究使用来自 23 名健康人类捐赠者的序列评估了 AlphaFold 3 (AF3) 预测针对流感血凝素 (HA) 保守表位的广泛中和抗体 (bnAb) 免疫复合物的能力。
核心方法
单细胞 RNA 测序生成 HA 反应性配对抗体序列,使用 AF3 进行建模,并使用 BLI、ELISA 和微中和测定进行表征,并通过负染色电子显微镜和冷冻电镜进行结构验证。
主要结果
B 细胞受体测序产生了 1161 个 H5 HA 特异性配对抗体序列,以及与具有高亲和力和广度的抗体相关的高度可信的 AF3 预测,以及与实验 nsEM 数据相匹配的计算机表位足迹。
解释与创新
AF3 可以高置信度地筛选针对保守 HA 表位的 VH1-69 bnAb,但模型需要针对新型或特征较少的抗体类别进行调整。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可应用于其他抗体类别和表位,有助于开发针对流感病毒的广泛中和抗体。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究侧重于特定的抗体类别,需要进一步验证以实现更广泛的应用。

Evaluating AlphaFold 3 immune complex structure prediction and cryo-EM analysis of broadly neutralizing antibodies towards influenza hemagglutinin 2309571

Authors: Morgan Gee, Pragati Sharma, Alesandra Rodriguez, Jordan Clark, Olivia Swanson, Florian Krammer, Andrew Ward, Julianna Han, Briney Bryan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Epitope:paratope interactions define the breadth and affinity an antibody has towards an antigen. Computational structure prediction can help to model immune complexes as a method of in silico screening for broadly neutralizing antibodies (bnAbs). However, we do not currently understand how structural predictions and confidence metrics correlate with experimental data. Here, we evaluate the accuracy of AlphaFold 3 (AF3) in predicting immune complexes of a known class of bnAbs towards a conserved epitope on influenza hemagglutinin (HA). Methods We used single-cell RNA sequencing to generate a library of HA reactive, paired antibody sequences. AF3 was used to computationally model antibody-HA interactions. Antibodies were expressed and characterized with BLI, ELISA, and microneutralization assays. Negative stain electron microscopy (nsEM) and single particle cryo-electron microscopy (SPA cryo-EM) were used to structurally characterize immune complexes. Results B cell receptor sequencing of PBMCs from 23 healthy, human donors generated 1161 H5 HA-specific, paired antibody sequences. These sequences comprised new examples within a known class of antibodies (VH1-69, of which previous examples are in the AF3 training set) likely to target the conserved central stem. We modeled these antibody sequences in complex with H1N1 and H5N1 HAs using AF3. Highly confident predictions correlated with antibodies possessing high affinity and breadth. Moreover, in silico epitope footprints strongly matched experimental nsEM data. Finally, we resolved atomistic details with high-resolution SPA cryo-EM that explain challenges with AF3 immune complex prediction. Conclusion AF3 can be used to screen for VH1-69 bnAbs targeting a conserved epitope on influenza HA with high confidence. This method of down selection may be applied to other classes of antibodies and to other epitopes. However, models still need to be tuned to accurately predict immune complexes of novel or less characterized antibody classes. Funding Source Endowed Fellowship in the Skaggs Graduate School of Chemical and Biological Sciences, National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases (NIAID). Topic Categories Computational and Systems Immunology (COMP)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Epitope:paratope interactions define the breadth and affinity an antibody has towards an antigen, but the correlation between structural predictions and experimental data is not well understood.
Design and population
The study evaluated AlphaFold 3 (AF3) for predicting immune complexes of broadly neutralizing antibodies (bnAbs) targeting a conserved epitope on influenza hemagglutinin (HA) using sequences from 23 healthy human donors.
Core methods
Single-cell RNA sequencing generated HA-reactive paired antibody sequences, which were modeled with AF3 and characterized using BLI, ELISA, and microneutralization assays, with structural validation via negative stain electron microscopy and cryo-EM.
Main results
B cell receptor sequencing yielded 1161 H5 HA-specific paired antibody sequences, and highly confident AF3 predictions correlated with antibodies possessing high affinity and breadth, with in silico epitope footprints matching experimental nsEM data.
Interpretation and novelty
AF3 can screen for VH1-69 bnAbs targeting a conserved HA epitope with high confidence, though models require tuning for novel or less characterized antibody classes.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method may be applied to other antibody classes and epitopes, aiding in the development of broadly neutralizing antibodies against influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study focuses on a specific antibody class and requires further validation for broader applications.

Journal: International Journal of Infection  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-31  |  DOI: 10.5812/iji-169286  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

应对流行病准备的数字健康:对传染病创新、公平差距和治理的批判性回顾 监测

Digital Health for Pandemic Preparedness: A Critical Review of Innovations, Equity Gaps, and Governance in Infectious Disease Surveillance
作者： Abdolreza Babamahmoodi, F. Babamahmoodi, Majid Marjani
摘要中文翻译背景:最近的传染病紧急事件,包括SARS-CoV-2、猴痘(2022-2024年)、美国乳牛中的高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1)(2024-2025年)以及卢旺达的马尔堡病毒(2024年),已经使传统的监测系统不堪重负。数字健康已被提议作为一种范式转变;然而,大多数评论都集中在诊断准确性上,而忽视了公平性和治理。证据获取:我们进行了一项结构化的主题叙事综述,纳入了从2010年1月至2025年3月发表的同行评议文献和灰色文献,并按照PRISMA 2020和PRISMA-ScR进行报告。于2025年2月5日至18日检索了PubMed、Scopus和Web of Science。检索关键词结合了三个概念块:(1)数字健康技术(人工智能、机器学习、移动健康、电子健康、远程医疗、电子健康记录、可穿戴设备、废水监测、基因组监测、自然语言处理);(2)传染病(暴发、大流行、流行病、COVID-19、埃博拉、寨卡、猴痘、流感、抗菌素耐药性、马尔堡);(3)公共卫生功能(监测、检测、预测、接触者追踪、治理、公平性)。提供了完整的布尔语法。在EndNote X20中去重,并由2名盲法审稿人在Rayyan中进行筛选(Cohen κ = 0.81)。使用混合方法评估工具2018评估实证研究,使用Joanna Briggs Institute清单评估综述。综合分析遵循Braun和Clarke的反身主题分析,并通过社会技术系统和数据正义视角进行解释。结果:在1498条记录中,142项研究符合纳入标准;48%(69/142)被评为高质量。证据基础在区域分布上严重偏向(高收入国家[HICs],41%;撒哈拉以南非洲或南亚,19%)。没有技术具有普遍优越性。人工智能(AI)研究集中在高收入国家(32项中的18项,56%),而短信服务(SMS)移动健康研究集中在低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs;25项中的15项,60%)。反复出现的障碍包括算法偏见、碎片化的互操作性、依赖捐赠者的可持续性,以及与低收入和中等收入国家分享病原体数据的缓慢收益共享。结论:在142项纳入研究的综合分析支持下,我们得出一个谨慎的结论:数字健康对于大流行准备是必要但不充分的。证据中最一致观察到的模式——区域偏向、反复出现的治理失败、依赖捐赠者的可持续性,以及决定公平性结果的设计选择——共同表明,公平性、可持续性和问责制是有效性的组成部分,而非障碍。日益受到限制的是政治和制度意愿,而非分析不确定性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了大流行防范方面的数字健康创新,重点关注近期突发事件(如 SARS-CoV-2、mpox、禽流感 A(H5N1) 和马尔堡病毒)中的传染病 监测 系统。核心问题是数字医疗技术能否有效解决传统 监测 的局限性,同时确保公平和治理。
证据基础与方法
继 PRISMA 2020 和 PRISMA-ScR 之后,我们对 2010 年 1 月至 2025 年 3 月期间的同行评审文献和灰色文献进行了结构化主题叙述评审。使用结合数字医疗技术、传染病和公共卫生功能的布尔语法对 PubMed、Scopus 和 Web of Science 进行检索(2025 年 2 月 5 日至 18 日)。记录由两名盲审员进行重复数据删除和筛选(Cohen κ = 0.81),并通过混合方法评估工具 2018 和乔安娜·布里格斯研究所清单进行质量评估。综合使用了布劳恩和克拉克的反思性主题分析。
共识与结论
在 142 项纳入的研究(48% 为高质量)中,数字健康被认为是应对大流行病的必要条件,但还不够。没有一种技术是普遍优越的,人工智能研究集中在高收入国家(56%),短信移动医疗则集中在低收入和中等收入国家(60%)。证据表明,公平、可持续性和问责制对于有效性而言是不可或缺的,而政治和制度意愿是具有约束力的约束。
争议与缺口
证据基础存在区域偏差(41% 为高收入国家;19% 为撒哈拉以南非洲或南亚国家)。反复出现的障碍包括算法偏差、互操作性分散、依赖捐助者的可持续性以及与中低收入国家的病原体数据惠益分享缓慢。没有任何技术表现出普遍的优越性,而且治理失败的现象屡见不鲜。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括解决区域差异、提高互操作性、确保超越捐助者依赖的可持续资金以及公平的数据共享机制。必须加强治理框架,将公平和问责制纳入数字卫生系统,重点关注中低收入国家的需求。

Digital Health for Pandemic Preparedness: A Critical Review of Innovations, Equity Gaps, and Governance in Infectious Disease Surveillance

Authors: Abdolreza Babamahmoodi, F. Babamahmoodi, Majid Marjani
Original abstract (English)
Context: Recent infectious disease emergencies, including SARS-CoV-2, mpox (2022 - 2024), highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) in US dairy cattle (2024 - 2025), and the Marburg virus in Rwanda (2024), have strained traditional surveillance systems. Digital health has been proposed as a paradigm shift; however, most reviews have focused on diagnostic accuracy while neglecting equity and governance. Evidence Acquisition: We conducted a structured thematic narrative review of peer-reviewed and grey literature published from January 2010 to March 2025, reported in accordance with PRISMA 2020 and PRISMA-ScR. PubMed, Scopus, and Web of Science were searched from February 5 to 18, 2025. Search keywords combined three concept blocks: (1) Digital-health technologies (artificial intelligence, machine learning, mHealth, eHealth, telemedicine, electronic health records, wearables, wastewater surveillance, genomic surveillance, natural-language processing); (2) infectious diseases (outbreak, pandemic, epidemic, COVID-19, Ebola, Zika, mpox, influenza, antimicrobial resistance, Marburg); and (3) public-health functions (surveillance, detection, forecasting, contact tracing, governance, equity). Full Boolean syntax is provided. Records were deduplicated in EndNote X20 and screened by 2 blinded reviewers in Rayyan (Cohen κ = 0.81). Empirical studies were appraised using the Mixed Methods Appraisal Tool 2018, and reviews were appraised using Joanna Briggs Institute checklists. The synthesis followed Braun and Clarke's reflexive thematic analysis and was interpreted through sociotechnical systems and data justice lenses. Results: Of 1498 records, 142 studies met the inclusion criteria; 48% (69/142) were rated as high quality. The evidence base was heavily skewed regionally (high-income countries [HICs], 41%; sub-Saharan Africa or South Asia, 19%). No technology was universally superior. Artificial intelligence (AI) studies were concentrated in HICs (18/32, 56%), whereas short-message-service (SMS) mHealth studies were concentrated in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs; 15/25, 60%). Recurring barriers included algorithmic bias, fragmented interoperability, donor-dependent sustainability, and slow benefit-sharing of pathogen data with LMICs. Conclusions: Across 142 included studies the synthesis supports a measured conclusion: Digital health is necessary but not sufficient for pandemic preparedness. The patterns most consistently observed in the evidence — regional skew, recurring governance failures, donor-dependent sustainability, and design choices that determine equity outcomes — together indicate that equity, sustainability, and accountability are constituents of effectiveness rather than obstacles to it. The binding constraint is increasingly political and institutional will rather than analytic uncertainty.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines digital health innovations for pandemic preparedness, focusing on infectious disease surveillance systems in the context of recent emergencies like SARS-CoV-2, mpox, avian influenza A(H5N1), and Marburg virus. The central question is whether digital health technologies can effectively address traditional surveillance limitations while ensuring equity and governance.
Evidence base and method
A structured thematic narrative review was conducted of peer-reviewed and grey literature from January 2010 to March 2025, following PRISMA 2020 and PRISMA-ScR. PubMed, Scopus, and Web of Science were searched (February 5–18, 2025) using Boolean syntax combining digital-health technologies, infectious diseases, and public-health functions. Records were deduplicated and screened by two blinded reviewers (Cohen κ = 0.81), with quality appraisal via Mixed Methods Appraisal Tool 2018 and Joanna Briggs Institute checklists. Synthesis used Braun and Clarke's reflexive thematic analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
Across 142 included studies (48% high quality), digital health is deemed necessary but not sufficient for pandemic preparedness. No technology was universally superior, with AI studies concentrated in high-income countries (56%) and SMS mHealth in low- and middle-income countries (60%). The evidence supports that equity, sustainability, and accountability are integral to effectiveness, with political and institutional will as the binding constraint.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base was regionally skewed (41% high-income countries; 19% sub-Saharan Africa or South Asia). Recurring barriers included algorithmic bias, fragmented interoperability, donor-dependent sustainability, and slow benefit-sharing of pathogen data with LMICs. No technology demonstrated universal superiority, and governance failures were consistently observed.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include addressing regional disparities, improving interoperability, ensuring sustainable funding beyond donor dependence, and equitable data-sharing mechanisms. Governance frameworks must be strengthened to embed equity and accountability into digital health systems, with a focus on LMIC needs.

环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 作为在野外条件下检测 禽流感 病毒的方法(综述)

Loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) as a method for detecting avian influenza virus in field conditions (review)
作者： A. A. Glazunova, T. A. Sevskikh, I. P. Sindryakova, D. A. Kudryashov, O. L. Kolbasova, I. A. Titov
摘要中文翻译目前,禽流感 病毒继续构成全球生物威胁,影响野生和家养鸟类以及哺乳动物(包括人类)种群。该病毒的传播毒株不仅对自然生态系统产生负面影响,而且对农工业部门的经济可持续性产生负面影响。病毒持续的遗传变异需要有效、快速和可靠的实验室诊断工具。尽管诊断的“黄金标准”聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 具有很高的准确性,但由于需要复杂的设备和高素质的人员,因此无论是在设备资源有限的实验室还是在现场条件下,其适用性都有限。本综述探讨了环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 作为 禽流感 病毒监测的一种有前途的替代方案,无论是在资源基础有限的实验室还是在现场条件下。指定的方法允许在恒温(60-65°C)下扩增核酸,确保高速分析(15-40 分钟)、灵敏度和特异性。一个关键优势是可以使用比色指示器对结果进行视觉解释,从而最大限度地减少设备要求。特别关注现代修饰:RT-LAMP 用于 RNA、多重格式以及与微芯片平台的集成。该方法的实际验证证实了其与 RT-PCR 相当的分析特性以及监测研究的有效性。因此,LAMP 代表了一种技术成熟的快速筛查工具,能够提高流行病学 监测 的有效性。为了广泛实施,协议标准化并将其纳入官方兽医控制指南是必要的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述将 禽流感 A 病毒视为影响野生和家养鸟类以及哺乳动物(包括人类)的全球生物威胁,并询问环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 是否可以作为在资源有限和现场环境中检测 禽流感 病毒的有效、快速和可靠的诊断工具。
证据基础与方法
该综述评估了 LAMP 作为 禽流感 病毒检测 PCR 的诊断替代方案,强调了其恒温扩增(60-65 °C)、快速分析时间(15-40 分钟)、高灵敏度和特异性以及可减少设备需求的视觉比色解释。
共识与结论
LAMP 被认为是一种技术成熟的快速筛查工具,具有与 RT-PCR 相当的分析特性,并在监测研究中表现出有效性,为资源有限的实验室和现场条件下的 禽流感 病毒检测提供了可行的替代方案。
争议与缺口
审查指出,尽管 LAMP 具有优势,但其广泛实施需要协议标准化并融入官方兽医控制指南,这表明目前方法验证和标准化采用之间存在差距。
科研与实践启示
LAMP 方案的标准化及其纳入官方兽医控制指南对于广泛实施和增强 监测 病毒监测的流行病学 监测 有效性是必要的。

Loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) as a method for detecting avian influenza virus in field conditions (review)

Authors: A. A. Glazunova, T. A. Sevskikh, I. P. Sindryakova, D. A. Kudryashov, O. L. Kolbasova, I. A. Titov
Original abstract (English)
Currently, the avian influenza virus continues to pose a global biological threat, affecting populations of wild and domestic birds, as well as mammals (including humans). Circulating strains of the virus have a negative impact not only on natural ecosystems but also on the economic sustainability of the agro-industrial sector. The constant genetic variability of the virus requires effective, fast, and reliable laboratory diagnostic tools. Despite its high accuracy, the "gold standard" of diagnostics—polymerase chain reaction (PCR)—has limited applicability both in laboratories with limited resource equipment and in field conditions due to the need for complex equipment and high qualification of personnel. The presented review examines loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) as a promising alternative for avian influenza virus monitoring both in laboratories with a limited resource base and in field conditions. The specified method allows amplification of nucleic acids at a constant temperature (60–65 °C), ensuring high speed of analysis (15–40 minutes), sensitivity, and specificity. A key advantage is the possibility of visual interpretation of the results using colorimetric indicators, which minimizes equipment requirements. Special attention is paid to modern modifications: RT-LAMP for work with RNA, multiplex formats, and integration with microchip platforms. Practical validation of the method confirms its analytical characteristics, comparable to RT-PCR, and effectiveness in monitoring studies. Thus, LAMP represents a technologically mature tool for rapid screening, capable of increasing the effectiveness of epizootiological surveillance. For its widespread implementation, standardization of protocols and their integration into official guidelines for veterinary control are necessary.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review addresses avian influenza A viruses as a global biological threat affecting wild and domestic birds and mammals, including humans, and asks whether loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) can serve as an effective, rapid, and reliable diagnostic tool for avian influenza virus detection in resource-limited and field settings.
Evidence base and method
The review evaluates LAMP as a diagnostic alternative to PCR for avian influenza virus detection, highlighting its constant-temperature amplification (60–65 °C), rapid analysis time (15–40 minutes), high sensitivity and specificity, and visual colorimetric interpretation that reduces equipment needs.
Consensus and conclusions
LAMP is identified as a technologically mature, rapid screening tool with analytical characteristics comparable to RT-PCR and demonstrated effectiveness in monitoring studies, offering a viable alternative for avian influenza virus detection in laboratories with limited resources and in field conditions.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that despite LAMP's advantages, its widespread implementation requires standardization of protocols and integration into official veterinary control guidelines, indicating a current gap between method validation and standardized adoption.
Research and practice implications
Standardization of LAMP protocols and their integration into official veterinary control guidelines are necessary to enable widespread implementation and enhance epizootiological surveillance effectiveness for avian influenza virus monitoring.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

禽流感(禽流感):英格兰最新情况 - gov.uk

Bird flu (avian influenza): latest situation in England - gov.uk
新闻简报英国政府已宣布英国免受 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 影响,禽流感 预防区 (AIPZ) 于 2026 年 6 月 4 日解除。2025 年 10 月 11 日,英格兰确认了 2025-2026 季节的首例 HPAI H5N1 病例,苏格兰、威尔士和北爱尔兰也报告了更多病例。据评估,对公众健康的风险非常低,正确烹制的家禽产品仍然是安全的。政府继续监测全球局势,将疫苗接种限制为授权动物园鸟类,并提供生物安全和报告死亡野生鸟类的指导。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-27。英国于2026年5月18日宣布英国脱离高致病性禽流感 (HPAI),并于2026年6月4日解除禽流感预防区(AIPZ)。
地点与对象
英格兰,指的是大不列颠、苏格兰、威尔士和北爱尔兰。受影响的种群包括家禽、圈养鸟类、野生鸟类 和哺乳动物。
事件
英国政府已宣布英国免受 HPAI H5N1 感染,并解除了 AIPZ。2025 年 10 月 11 日,英格兰确认了 2025-2026 暴发 季节的首例 HPAI H5N1 病例。评估对公众健康的风险非常低。
规模、影响与风险
英格兰(2025年10月11日)、苏格兰(2025年11月12日)、威尔士(2025年10月25日)和北爱尔兰(2025年10月9日)报告了HPAI H5N1确诊病例。野生鸟类 和家禽暴露中 HPAI H5 的风险评估为低(事件很少发生)。对公众健康的风险非常低,英国消费者的食品安全风险也非常低。
应对、状态与不确定性
AIPZ 于 2026 年 6 月 4 日解除。政府继续监测全球局势并投资禽流感研究。疫苗接种仅限于经授权的动物园鸟类。英国健康安全局和食品标准局建议,正确煮熟的家禽和家禽产品可以安全食用。公共指导包括报告死亡 野生鸟类 和维护生物安全。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Bird flu (avian influenza): latest situation in England - gov.uk

News Brief
The UK government has declared freedom from highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) for Great Britain, with the avian influenza prevention zone (AIPZ) lifted on 4 June 2026. The first case of HPAI H5N1 in the 2025-2026 season was confirmed in England on 11 October 2025, with additional cases reported in Scotland, Wales, and Northern Ireland. The risk to the general public's health is assessed as very low, and properly cooked poultry products remain safe. The government continues to monitor the situation globally, restrict vaccination to authorized zoo birds, and provide guidance on biosecurity and reporting dead wild birds.
Original source text (English)
Bird flu (avian influenza): latest situation in England Find out about the latest bird flu situation in England and guidance for bird keepers and the public. Be vigilant for signs of disease and report it to keep your birds safe. Latest situation The UK has self-declared freedom from highly pathogenic avian influenza (bird flu) for Great Britain on 18 May 2026. The avian influenza prevention zone (AIPZ) for poultry and captive birds in England, Wales and Scotland was lifted on 4 June 2026. Read guidance on holding bird gatherings in England. Vaccines You cannot vaccinate poultry or most captive birds against bird flu in England. You can only vaccinate zoo birds in England if you meet eligibility criteria and get authorisation from APHA. Only zoos or collections holding a current zoo licence can apply for vaccination. Defra continues to invest in bird flu research and monitors the situation globally. We work with the Veterinary Medicines Directorate (VMD) to monitor the development of vaccines for birds. Find out more information on avian influenza (bird flu) vaccination and the work of the avian influenza vaccination taskforce. Latest GOV.UK news stories Risk levels and outbreak assessments The risk of HPAI H5 in wild birds in Great Britain is assessed as low (event occurs rarely). The risk of poultry exposure to HPAI H5 in Great Britain is assessed as: - low (event occurs rarely) (with low uncertainty) where there is suboptimal or poor biosecurity - low (event occurs rarely) (with low uncertainty) where stringent biosecurity is consistently applied at all times Find details of the evidence that supported these decisions in the: - risk and outbreak assessments - outbreak epidemiology reports - weekly reports of findings of bird flu in wild birds in Great Britain - findings of bird flu in non-avian wildlife - findings of bird flu in captive mammals The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) advise that bird flu is primarily a disease of birds and the risk to the general public’s health is very low. The Food Standards Agency (FSA) has said bird flu poses a very low food safety risk for UK consumers. Properly cooked poultry and poultry products, including eggs, are safe to eat. Wild birds Find out how to report dead wild birds. Defra has set out practical information to support land managers, the public and ornithological and environmental organisations in their response to the growing threat of bird flu. The mitigation strategy for avian influenza in wild birds in England and Wales explains how these groups, together with the government and its delivery partners, can reduce the impact on wild bird populations whilst protecting public health, the wider environment and the rural economy. Landowners, local authorities and other organisations can download and print bird flu posters to display on site when there is a risk of bird flu or if bird flu has been detected in the area. Check the: - interactive map of reported wild bird mortality and findings of bird flu in wild birds in Great Britain - interactive dashboard of findings of bird flu in wild birds in Great Britain - weekly reports of HPAI findings in wild birds in Great Britain You can find out more about: - the collection thresholds for dead wild birds with suspected bird flu - how bird flu spreads in wild birds and wild mammals, and how the government monitors it Feeding wild birds You can feed wild birds but always wash your hands with soap and water afterwards. Avoid areas that have premises where poultry or captive birds are kept. Wild birds can spread bird flu to captive birds. If you’re in an avian influenza prevention zone (AIPZ) you cannot feed wild gamebirds within 500m of a premises with more than 500 poultry or captive birds. Check if you’re in a zone on the bird flu zone map and find out more in the guidance on rules if you keep game birds. If you feed wild birds in your garden, read advice from the British Trust for Ornithology (BTO) to keep feeders and water baths clean. This will help to stop bird flu and other diseases spreading between different birds. The UK Health Security Agency has said that the risk to the public from this bird flu strain is very low. It mainly affects birds. Do not touch or move any dead or sick wild birds – find out how to report them. Wash your hands thoroughly with soap and water if you touch wild bird faeces or feathers. For more advice go to the NHS website. Mammals Avian influenza (bird flu) viruses can also infect wild and kept mammals. This is known as influenza of avian origin. Find out how we monitor the risk of influenza of avian origin in wild mammals. Check the: - report of findings of influenza of avian origin in non-avian wildlife - interactive map showing findings of influenza of avian origin in wild mammals in Great Britain - findings of influenza of avian origin in captive mammals Influenza of avian origin in mammals is notifiable in both wild and kept mammals. You must report it immediately if you examine or inspect any wild or kept mammal, or analyse samples from any wild or kept mammal or mammal carcase and: - suspect the presence of influenza of avian origin - detect evidence of influenza A virus or antibodies to influenza A virus Report it immediately by calling: - 03000 200 301 if you’re in England - 03003 038 268 if you’re in Wales - your local Field Services Office if you’re in Scotland If you do not report it, you’re breaking the law. Bird flu webinars Whether you have pet birds, commercial flocks or just a few birds in a backyard flock, the avian influenza ‘stop the spread’ webinars explain what you can do to protect your birds. Defra policies and contingency plans Defra controls cases of bird flu by following the: - contingency plan for exotic notifiable diseases - notifiable avian disease control strategy - mitigation strategy for avian influenza in wild birds in England and Wales Bird flu legislation The legislation in England includes: - The Avian Influenza and Influenza of Avian Origin in Mammals (England) (No.2) Order 2006 - The Avian Influenza and Influenza of Avian Origin in Mammals (Amendment) (England) Order 2022 - The Exotic Disease (Amendment) (England) (EU Exit) Regulations 2018 - The Exotic Animal Disease (Amendment) (England) Order 2021 - The Exotic Animal Disease (Amendment) (England) Order 2022 - The Exotic Disease (Amendment) (England) Order 2023 - The Exotic Disease (Amendment) (England) Order 2024 - The Avian Influenza (H5N1 in Wild Birds) (England) Order 2006 - The Avian Influenza (H5N1 in Wild Birds) (England) (Amendment) Order 2021 - The Avian Influenza (Preventive Measures) (England) Regulations 2006 - The Transport of Animals (Cleansing and Disinfection) (England) (No. 3) Order 2003 - The Diseases of Animals (Approved Disinfectants) (England) Order 2007 - The Avian Influenza (Vaccination) (England) Regulations 2006 For Scotland go to the Scottish Government website. For Wales go to the Welsh Government website. For Northern Ireland go to the DAERA-NI website. Image on this page shows a group of ducks flying. Credit: Jari Hytönen. View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-07-27. The UK declared freedom from highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) for Great Britain on 18 May 2026, and the avian influenza prevention zone (AIPZ) was lifted on 4 June 2026.
Location and population
England, with references to Great Britain, Scotland, Wales, and Northern Ireland. The affected population includes poultry, captive birds, wild birds, and mammals.
Event
The UK government has declared freedom from HPAI H5N1 for Great Britain and lifted the AIPZ. The first case of HPAI H5N1 in the 2025-2026 outbreak season was confirmed in England on 11 October 2025. The risk to the general public's health is assessed as very low.
Scale, impact and risk
Confirmed cases of HPAI H5N1 were reported in England (11 October 2025), Scotland (12 November 2025), Wales (25 October 2025), and Northern Ireland (9 October 2025). The risk of HPAI H5 in wild birds and poultry exposure is assessed as low (event occurs rarely). The risk to the general public's health is very low, and the food safety risk for UK consumers is also very low.
Response, status and uncertainty
The AIPZ was lifted on 4 June 2026. The government continues to monitor the situation globally and invest in bird flu research. Vaccination is restricted to zoo birds with authorization. The UK Health Security Agency and Food Standards Agency advise that properly cooked poultry and poultry products are safe to eat. Public guidance includes reporting dead wild birds and maintaining biosecurity.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

禽流感和保护您的鸟类|新西兰政府 - 第一产业部

Bird flu and protecting your birds | NZ Government - Ministry for Primary Industries
新闻简报新西兰第一产业部已确认在该国境内个体 野生鸟类 中检测到禽流感 (HPAI)。为此,当局建立了热线电话和在线报告系统,供公民报告三人或三人以上患病或死亡的群体 野生鸟类。政府正在向公众提供有关如何限制病毒影响并为潜在的进一步传播做好准备的指导。虽然在个别鸟类中证实了这一检测,但尚未披露具体的病例数、死亡人数或更广泛的风险评估。重点仍然是 监测、报告机制和保护本土物种的公共教育。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-23。证据中未指定事件日期。
地点与对象
受影响人群:野生鸟类。
事件
新西兰 野生鸟类 个体中确认检测到禽流感 (HPAI)。由第一产业部报告。
规模、影响与风险
案例:个人野生鸟类。没有报告具体病例数、死亡数或暴露数。风险:证据中未明确说明。
应对、状态与不确定性
公共卫生应对措施包括建立外来病虫害热线(0800 80 99 66)和在线报告门户网站(report.mpi.govt.nz),用于报告群体中 3 名或以上患病或死亡的 野生鸟类。提供了关于限制禽流感影响和做好准备的指南。没有有关调查、测试、隔离、接触者追踪或 治疗 状态的信息。来源限制:已审查完整的原始报告正文。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Bird flu and protecting your birds | NZ Government - Ministry for Primary Industries

News Brief
The New Zealand Ministry for Primary Industries has confirmed the detection of bird flu (HPAI) in individual wild birds within the country. In response, authorities have established a hotline and online reporting system for citizens to report groups of three or more sick or dead wild birds. The government is providing guidance to the public on how to limit the impact of the virus and be prepared for potential further spread. While the detection is confirmed in individual birds, no specific case numbers, deaths, or broader risk assessments have been disclosed. The focus remains on surveillance, reporting mechanisms, and public education to protect native species.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

野生鸟类 监测揭示H5Nx流感如何不断返回中国

Wild bird surveillance reveals how H5Nx flu keeps returning to China
新闻简报A 20-year nationwide surveillance study in China, published in Nature Communications, reveals how H5Nx highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) are repeatedly reintroduced through migratory birds rather than sustained local circulation. Researchers collected 219,445 wild bird samples from 2003 to 2023, isolating 1,344 avian influenza viruses (0.61% rate). After expanding surveillance in 2014, 368 H5Nx HPAIVs were detected, including H5N1, H5N2, H5N6, and H5N8 subtypes. Viral activity peaked seasonally between November and January, with a notable absence of H5Nx HPAIVs from mid-2017 to late 2020. Phylogenetic analyses showed that most recent viruses in China were genetically closer to European and North Asian strains, indicating repeated reintroductions via migratory flyways. The study underscores the need for enhanced international surveillance and genomic monitoring to track viral evolution and spread.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究涵盖了从 2003 年 2 月到 2023 年 2 月的 20 年时间,其中 监测 在 2014 年之后显着扩大。病毒活动在 11 月到 1 月之间达到顶峰,2017 年中期到 2020 年底之间 H5Nx HPAIV 明显缺失。
地点与对象
监测在全国范围内开展,覆盖28个省级地区。它的重点是野生鸟类,包括对活禽的主动监测和对病禽和死禽的被动监测,特别是沿中亚迁徙路线和东亚-澳大利亚迁徙路线等主要迁徙路线。
事件
《自然通讯》发表的一项长期研究通过全国范围的 监测 和基因组分析,调查了 进化、传播 以及 H5Nx 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 在中国 野生鸟类 的地理传播。研究证实,H5Nx HPAIV 是通过候鸟而非持续的本地循环重新引入中国的。
规模、影响与风险
Over 20 years, 219,445 samples were collected, yielding 1,344 avian influenza virus isolates (0.61% isolation rate). After 2014, 368 H5Nx HPAIV isolates were detected, including H5N1, H5N2, H5N6, and H5N8 subtypes. The study documented 46 distinct H5Nx HPAIV detection events, with most occurring during migration and overwintering seasons. Globally, outbreaks linked to wild birds grew significantly after 2016, often outnumbering poultry outbreaks from autumn 2020 onward.
应对、状态与不确定性
研究结果强调需要持续进行 监测、基因组监测和国际合作来检测新的病毒变体。研究指出,虽然 H5Nx HPAIV 在 2017 年中期至 2020 年末期间不存在,但低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 继续传播,这表明 传播 可能受到抑制,而不是证实不存在。该研究强调了候鸟在 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒洲际传播中的作用。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Wild bird surveillance reveals how H5Nx flu keeps returning to China

News Brief
A 20-year nationwide surveillance study in China, published in Nature Communications, reveals how H5Nx highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) are repeatedly reintroduced through migratory birds rather than sustained local circulation. Researchers collected 219,445 wild bird samples from 2003 to 2023, isolating 1,344 avian influenza viruses (0.61% rate). After expanding surveillance in 2014, 368 H5Nx HPAIVs were detected, including H5N1, H5N2, H5N6, and H5N8 subtypes. Viral activity peaked seasonally between November and January, with a notable absence of H5Nx HPAIVs from mid-2017 to late 2020. Phylogenetic analyses showed that most recent viruses in China were genetically closer to European and North Asian strains, indicating repeated reintroductions via migratory flyways. The study underscores the need for enhanced international surveillance and genomic monitoring to track viral evolution and spread.
Original source text (English)
Two decades of monitoring show how migratory birds, viral reassortment, and global flyways are reshaping the spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza. Study: Evolving dynamics of H5Nx avian influenza in China revealed by long-term wild bird surveillance . Image Credit: GatisVil / Shutterstock In a recent study published as an Article in Press in the journal Nature Communications , a group of researchers investigated the long-term evolution, transmission, and geographic spread of H5Nx highly pathogenic avian influenza virus ( HPAIV ) in China's wild birds through nationwide surveillance and genomic analyses. Background Avian flu outbreaks affect millions of birds, disrupting global poultry production and threatening wildlife conservation and public health. Highly pathogenic avian influenza ( HPAI ) H5Nx viruses have far exceeded their original geographic range by spreading across continents with the help of migratory birds and often engaging in the exchange of genetic material. Since various continents are connected through migratory flyways, it is important to determine the pattern of the spread of these viruses and the manner in which they evolve. Further research is needed to strengthen international surveillance and improve early detection of newly emerging viral lineages. About the study The researchers conducted nationwide surveillance of avian influenza viruses ( AIVs ) in wild birds across China from February 2003 through February 2023. During this period, 219,445 samples were collected from 28 provincial-level regions. Surveillance before 2013 was largely restricted to northeastern China, especially Heilongjiang Province, but this changed after the establishment of the Bird Flu Monitoring and Alert System ( BFMAS ) in 2014. The surveillance project included both active monitoring with live birds and passive surveillance with reports of sick and dead birds. Samples collected included fresh fecal samples, tissues, and swabs from various sources. The specimens collected were transported under temperature-controlled conditions and underwent virus isolation in specific pathogen-free embryonic chicken eggs. Identification of the viruses was done through hemagglutination tests, subtyping, ribonucleic acid ( RNA ) extraction, amplification, and Sanger sequencing. The scientists performed a thorough analysis of global outbreak cases from the Food and Agriculture Organization (FAO) records, as well as obtained open-source genome sequence data from the Global Initiative on Sharing All Influenza Data ( GISAID ) and the National Center for Biotechnology Information ( NCBI ) databases. Phylogenetic, phylogeographic, statistical, and evolutionary analyses were then performed to evaluate viral diversity, transmission pathways, geographic spread, host transitions, and long-term evolutionary patterns of H5Nx viruses. Study results Nationwide surveillance identified substantial changes in the occurrence and distribution of H5Nx HPAIVs over the 20-year monitoring period. Researchers collected 219,445 bird samples in China from February 2003 to February 2023 and isolated 1,344 AIVs , yielding an overall isolation rate of 0.61%. Prior to 2014, there was limited monitoring, and no H5Nx HPAIVs were detected . Following the introduction of the BFMAS , surveillance expanded considerably, yielding 368 H5Nx HPAIV isolates, including H5N1, H5N2, H5N6, and H5N8 viruses. Viral activity showed a clear seasonal pattern, with peaks between November and January and a drop-off in summer. Interestingly, no H5Nx HPAIVs were detected between mid-2017 and late 2020, despite the continued circulation of low-pathogenic avian influenza viruses ( LPAIVs ), suggesting possible suppression rather than confirming the absence of transmission. Temporal shifts in viral subtypes were also evident. H5N1 predominated during 2014-2015, H5N8 became the dominant subtype during 2016-2017 and 2020, and H5N1 re-emerged between 2021 and 2023. These transitions closely mirrored global changes in clade 2.3.4.4b viruses and reflected ongoing viral evolution through antigenic drift, reassortment, and host immune pressures. The surveillance program documented 46 distinct H5Nx HPAIV detection events, revealing marked geographic and seasonal variation. Most detections occurred along major migratory flyways, particularly the Central Asia Flyway and the East Asian-Australasian Flyway, with approximately three-quarters of events recorded between September and February during migration and overwintering seasons. Phylogenetic analyses identified three major viral clades circulating in China, with clade 2.3.4.4b as the dominant lineage, exhibiting the greatest genetic diversity and geographic distribution. This clade continued to evolve through reassortment, creating multiple H5N1 and H5N8 genotypes over time. Worldwide outbreak analyses also showed that, while poultry accounted for the majority of outbreaks throughout the study period, outbreaks linked to wild birds grew significantly after 2016 and often outnumbered poultry outbreaks from autumn 2020 onward. Phylogeographic analysis revealed that the majority of earlier H5Nx transmission events occurred in East Asia, whereas more recent viruses found in China were genetically closer to viruses circulating in Europe and North Asia than to locally derived variants. Most clade 2.3.4.4b viruses detected after 2020 were consistent with repeated reintroductions through migratory birds rather than continuous local transmission. Conclusion The findings showed that long-term surveillance of wild birds contributes to a better understanding of the evolution, reassortment, and worldwide spread of H5Nx HPAIVs . According to the research, clade 2.3.4.4b has emerged as the dominant lineage mediating intercontinental spread, and the latest viruses found in China largely represent repeated reintroductions through migratory birds rather than sustained local circulation. These observations emphasize the need for ongoing surveillance, genomic monitoring, and international cooperation to detect new viral variants and explore their routes of transmission. Journal reference: Li, X., Lv, X., Li, Y., Fei, M., Zhang, S., An, Q., Qin, S., Peng, P., Li, J., Liang, H., Xiu, Y., Li, Z., Wang, Y., Wang, Y., Zeng, X., Hou, Z., Sun, H., Xu, Y., Li, Y., & Chai, H. (2026). Evolving dynamics of H5Nx avian influenza in China revealed by long-term wild bird surveillance. Nature Communications . DOI : 10.1038/s41467-026-76039-9, https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-026-76039-9
View five news elements
Time
The study covered a 20-year period from February 2003 to February 2023, with surveillance expanding significantly after 2014. Viral activity peaked between November and January, with a notable absence of H5Nx HPAIVs between mid-2017 and late 2020.
Location and population
The surveillance was conducted nationwide across China, covering 28 provincial-level regions. It focused on wild birds, including both active monitoring of live birds and passive surveillance of sick and dead birds, particularly along major migratory flyways like the Central Asia Flyway and the East Asian-Australasian Flyway.
Event
A long-term study published in Nature Communications investigated the evolution, transmission, and geographic spread of H5Nx highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) in China's wild birds through nationwide surveillance and genomic analyses. The research confirmed that H5Nx HPAIVs were reintroduced to China through migratory birds rather than sustained local circulation.
Scale, impact and risk
Over 20 years, 219,445 samples were collected, yielding 1,344 avian influenza virus isolates (0.61% isolation rate). After 2014, 368 H5Nx HPAIV isolates were detected, including H5N1, H5N2, H5N6, and H5N8 subtypes. The study documented 46 distinct H5Nx HPAIV detection events, with most occurring during migration and overwintering seasons. Globally, outbreaks linked to wild birds grew significantly after 2016, often outnumbering poultry outbreaks from autumn 2020 onward.
Response, status and uncertainty
The findings highlight the need for ongoing surveillance, genomic monitoring, and international cooperation to detect new viral variants. The study noted that while H5Nx HPAIVs were absent between mid-2017 and late 2020, low-pathogenic avian influenza viruses (LPAIVs) continued to circulate, suggesting possible suppression rather than confirmed absence of transmission. The research emphasized the role of migratory birds in the intercontinental spread of clade 2.3.4.4b viruses.
原文 / Source
Published: 2026-07-29  |  Publisher: Purdue University College of Veterinary Medicine
